physics.py　风场物理下推　（115→57 行）

1from __future__ import annotations
3from dataclasses import dataclass
5import numpy as np
7from .mathutils import magnitude, meteorological_dir_from_uv
8from .types import TerrainField, WindField
11@dataclass(slots=True)
12-14class PhysicsDiagnostics: f_elev: object
15-17 f_rough: object f_slope: object delta_elev: object
20-21def mean_grid(grid: list[list[float]]) -> float: arr = np.asarray(grid, dtype=np.float64)
22-23 if arr.size == 0: return 0.0
24 return float(arr.mean())
27-31def downscale_wind( u_km: float, v_km: float, terrain: TerrainField, w_km: float = 0.0,
32-35 altitude_levels: int = 5, *, altitude_step_m: float = 50.0, u_100: float | None = None,
36-37 v_100: float | None = None, ) -> tuple[WindField, PhysicsDiagnostics]:
38 elev = np.asarray(terrain.elevation, dtype=np.float64)
39-40 if elev.ndim != 2 or elev.size == 0: empty = WindField(u=[], v=[], w=[])
41 blank: list[list[float]] = []
42 return empty, PhysicsDiagnostics(f_elev=blank, f_rough=blank, f_slope=blank, delta_elev=blank)
44 slope = np.asarray(terrain.slope, dtype=np.float64)
45 aspect = np.asarray(terrain.aspect, dtype=np.float64)
46 roughness = np.asarray(terrain.roughness, dtype=np.float64)
47-48 levels = max(1, int(altitude_levels)) alt_step = max(float(altitude_step_m), 1e-6)
50-51 mean_elev = float(elev.mean()) speed_10 = magnitude(u_km, v_km)
52 dir_10 = meteorological_dir_from_uv(u_km, v_km)
53 has_100 = u_100 is not None and v_100 is not None
54-55 if has_100: speed_100 = magnitude(float(u_100), float(v_100))
56 dir_100 = meteorological_dir_from_uv(float(u_100), float(v_100))
57-58 else: speed_100 = speed_10
59 dir_100 = dir_10
61-62 delta = elev - mean_elev factor_elev = np.clip(1.0 + 0.0015 * delta, 0.7, 1.3)
63 factor_rough = np.exp(-0.4 * roughness)
64 theta = np.arctan2(np.sin(aspect - dir_10), np.cos(aspect - dir_10))
65 factor_slope = np.clip(1.0 + 0.5 * slope * np.cos(theta), 0.6, 1.4)
66-67 upslope = slope * np.cos(theta) ridge_lift = slope * np.sin(theta) * np.sin(theta)
69-70 level_idx = np.arange(levels, dtype=np.float64)[:, None, None] z_agl = level_idx * alt_step
71-73 log10 = np.log(10.0) log100 = np.log(100.0) t_shear = np.clip(
74-77 (np.log(np.maximum(z_agl, 10.0)) - log10) / max(log100 - log10, 1e-6), 0.0, 1.0, )
78-79 if levels == 1: t_shear = np.zeros((1, 1, 1), dtype=np.float64)
81 base_speed = speed_10 * (1.0 - t_shear) + speed_100 * t_shear
82 ddir = (dir_100 - dir_10 + np.pi) % (2.0 * np.pi) - np.pi
83 base_dir = dir_10 + t_shear * ddir
85 level_ratio = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, levels, dtype=np.float64)[:, None, None] if levels > 1 else np.zeros((1, 1, 1))
87 shear_gain = (0.02 + 0.04 * level_ratio) if has_100 else (0.08 + 0.18 * level_ratio)
88 roughness_decay = np.exp(-1.2 * roughness[None, :, :] * (1.0 - 0.6 * level_ratio))
89 speed = base_speed * factor_elev[None, :, :] * factor_slope[None, :, :] * roughness_decay * (1.0 + shear_gain)
90 direction = np.arctan2(
91 np.sin(base_dir + (0.1 + 0.06 * level_ratio) * np.sin(theta)[None, :, :] + 0.04 * level_ratio),
92 np.cos(base_dir + (0.1 + 0.06 * level_ratio) * np.sin(theta)[None, :, :] + 0.04 * level_ratio),
93-95 ) u_field = -speed * np.sin(direction) v_field = -speed * np.cos(direction)
97-98 thermal_decay = 1.0 - 0.45 * level_ratio wave_gain = 0.7 + 0.9 * level_ratio
99-100 w_field = np.clip( w_km * (0.9 - 0.2 * level_ratio)
101 + (2.4 * thermal_decay) * upslope[None, :, :]
102 - (0.9 - 0.35 * level_ratio) * roughness[None, :, :]
103-104 + wave_gain * 0.6 * ridge_lift[None, :, :] + 0.0012 * delta[None, :, :]
105-108 + 0.25 * level_ratio, -3.5, 4.5, )
110-111 return WindField(u=u_field, v=v_field, w=w_field), PhysicsDiagnostics( f_elev=factor_elev,
112-115 f_rough=factor_rough, f_slope=factor_slope, delta_elev=delta, )

37@dataclass(slots=True)
38-39class LinearRegressor: weights: list[float]
40-42 bias: float means: list[float] scales: list[float]
44-45 def predict(self, features: list[float]) -> float: total = self.bias
46 for value, weight, mean, scale in zip(features, self.weights, self.means, self.scales):
47 total += ((value - mean) / scale) * weight
48 return total
50-53 def to_dict(self) -> dict: return { "model_type": "linear", "weights": self.weights,
54-57 "bias": self.bias, "means": self.means, "scales": self.scales, }

69-71def train_ridge_regression( x_rows: list[list[float]], y_values: list[float],
72-75 learning_rate: float = 0.03, epochs: int = 800, l2: float = 0.01, ) -> LinearRegressor:
76 del learning_rate, epochs
77-79 if not x_rows: raise ValueError("Training data is empty.") feature_count = len(x_rows[0])
80-81 means = [0.0] * feature_count scales = [1.0] * feature_count
82-83 for idx in range(feature_count): column = [row[idx] for row in x_rows]
84 mean = sum(column) / len(column)
85-86 variance = sum((value - mean) ** 2 for value in column) / len(column) means[idx] = mean
87 scales[idx] = max(math.sqrt(variance), 1e-6)
88 normalized = []
89 for row in x_rows:
90 normalized.append([(value - mean) / scale for value, mean, scale in zip(row, means, scales)])
92-93 design = [[1.0] + row for row in normalized] dim = feature_count + 1
94-95 xtx = [[0.0 for _ in range(dim)] for _ in range(dim)] xty = [0.0 for _ in range(dim)]
96-98 for row, target in zip(design, y_values): for i in range(dim): xty[i] += row[i] * target
99-100 for j in range(dim): xtx[i][j] += row[i] * row[j]
101-103 for i in range(1, dim): xtx[i][i] += l2 solution = _solve_linear_system(xtx, xty)
104 return LinearRegressor(weights=solution[1:], bias=solution[0], means=means, scales=scales)
107def _solve_linear_system(matrix: list[list[float]], vector: list[float]) -> list[float]:
108-109 size = len(vector) augmented = [row[:] + [rhs] for row, rhs in zip(matrix, vector)]
110 for col in range(size):
111 pivot = max(range(col, size), key=lambda idx: abs(augmented[idx][col]))
112-113 if abs(augmented[pivot][col]) < 1e-12: continue
114 augmented[col], augmented[pivot] = augmented[pivot], augmented[col]
115 pivot_value = augmented[col][col]
116-117 for j in range(col, size + 1): augmented[col][j] /= pivot_value
118-120 for row in range(size): if row == col: continue
121 factor = augmented[row][col]
122-123 if factor == 0.0: continue
124-125 for j in range(col, size + 1): augmented[row][j] -= factor * augmented[col][j]
126 return [augmented[idx][size] for idx in range(size)]
129@dataclass(slots=True)

134-135 left: "TreeNode | None" = None right: "TreeNode | None" = None
137 def predict(self, features: list[float]) -> float:

151 return payload

469-471def train_gradient_boosted_trees( x_rows: list[list[float]], y_values: list[float],
472-474 learning_rate: float = 0.08, num_estimators: int = 28, max_depth: int = 4,
475-477 min_samples_leaf: int = 24, max_bins: int = 16, feature_subsample_ratio: float = 0.7,
478-479) -> GradientBoostedTreesRegressor: if not x_rows:
480 raise ValueError("Training data is empty.")
481-482 sample_count = len(x_rows) base_value = sum(y_values) / sample_count
483 predictions = [base_value for _ in range(sample_count)]
484-485 trees: list[DecisionTreeRegressor] = [] rng = random.Random(23)
487 for _ in range(num_estimators):
488 residuals = [target - pred for target, pred in zip(y_values, predictions)]
489-492 root = _build_tree( x_rows=x_rows, targets=residuals, indices=list(range(sample_count)),
493-496 depth=0, max_depth=max_depth, min_samples_leaf=min_samples_leaf, max_bins=max_bins,
497-499 feature_subsample_ratio=feature_subsample_ratio, rng=rng, )
500-501 tree = DecisionTreeRegressor(root=root) trees.append(tree)
502 for idx, features in enumerate(x_rows):
503 predictions[idx] += learning_rate * tree.predict(features)
505 return GradientBoostedTreesRegressor(base_value=base_value, learning_rate=learning_rate, trees=trees)
508-511def _build_tree( x_rows: list[list[float]], targets: list[float], indices: list[int],
512-515 depth: int, max_depth: int, min_samples_leaf: int, max_bins: int,
516-518 feature_subsample_ratio: float, rng: random.Random, ) -> TreeNode:
519 node_value = _mean([targets[idx] for idx in indices])
520 if depth >= max_depth or len(indices) <= min_samples_leaf * 2:
521 return TreeNode(value=node_value)
523-524 feature_count = len(x_rows[0]) feature_indices = list(range(feature_count))
525-526 rng.shuffle(feature_indices) take = max(1, int(feature_count * feature_subsample_ratio))
527-530 candidate_features = feature_indices[:take] best_feature = None best_threshold = None
531-532 best_score = math.inf best_left: list[int] | None = None

538 left = [idx for idx in indices if x_rows[idx][feature_index] <= threshold]
539 right = [idx for idx in indices if x_rows[idx][feature_index] > threshold]
540-541 if len(left) < min_samples_leaf or len(right) < min_samples_leaf: continue

543-544 if score < best_score: best_score = score

577-579 rng, ), )
582-584def _candidate_thresholds( x_rows: list[list[float]], indices: list[int],
585-587 feature_index: int, max_bins: int, ) -> list[float]:
588 values = sorted(x_rows[idx][feature_index] for idx in indices)
589-591 if len(values) < 2: return [] thresholds: list[float] = []
592-593 for bin_idx in range(1, max_bins + 1): pos = int(len(values) * bin_idx / (max_bins + 1))
594-597 if pos <= 0 or pos >= len(values): continue left = values[pos - 1] right = values[pos]
598 threshold = (left + right) * 0.5
599-600 if not thresholds or abs(threshold - thresholds[-1]) > 1e-9: thresholds.append(threshold)
601 return thresholds
604-605def _squared_error(targets: list[float], indices: list[int]) -> float: if not indices:
606 return 0.0
607 mean = _mean([targets[idx] for idx in indices])
608 return sum((targets[idx] - mean) ** 2 for idx in indices)
611-612def _mean(values: list[float]) -> float: return sum(values) / max(len(values), 1)

227 z_n, h, w = belief_map.levels, belief_map.height, belief_map.width
228-231 if z_n <= 0 or h <= 0 or w <= 0: return wind_u = np.asarray(wind.u, dtype=np.float64)

237 wind_w = np.asarray(wind_w, dtype=np.float64).reshape(z_n, h, w)
239 arrays = belief_map.field_arrays
240 if arrays is not None and "last_update" in arrays and "wind_var_u" in arrays:

249-250 else: last_update = np.empty((z_n, h, w), dtype=np.float64)
251 var_u = np.empty((z_n, h, w), dtype=np.float64)
252 var_v = np.empty((z_n, h, w), dtype=np.float64)
253 var_w = np.empty((z_n, h, w), dtype=np.float64)
254 cov_uv = np.empty((z_n, h, w), dtype=np.float64)
255 mode_s = np.empty((z_n, h, w), dtype=np.float64)

257-258 mode_u = np.empty((z_n, h, w), dtype=np.float64) cells = belief_map.cells
259-260 for z in range(z_n): layer = cells[z]
261-262 for y in range(h): row = layer[y]
263-264 for x in range(w): cell = row[x]
265-266 last_update[z, y, x] = cell.last_update var_u[z, y, x] = cell.wind_var_u
267-268 var_v[z, y, x] = cell.wind_var_v var_w[z, y, x] = cell.wind_var_w
269-270 cov_uv[z, y, x] = cell.wind_cov_uv mode_s[z, y, x] = cell.mode_prob_sink
271-272 mode_n[z, y, x] = cell.mode_prob_neutral mode_u[z, y, x] = cell.mode_prob_uplift
274 age = np.maximum(step - last_update, 0.0)
275 process_var = self.process_noise * (1.0 + self.decay_per_step * age)
277 next_s = 0.72 * mode_s + 0.18 * mode_n + 0.06 * mode_u
278 next_n = 0.20 * mode_s + 0.64 * mode_n + 0.20 * mode_u
279-280 next_u = 0.08 * mode_s + 0.18 * mode_n + 0.74 * mode_u floor = 1e-6
281 total = np.maximum(next_s + next_n + next_u, floor * 3.0)
282-283 mode_s = np.maximum(next_s, floor) / total mode_n = np.maximum(next_n, floor) / total
284-286 mode_u = np.maximum(next_u, floor) / total var_u = np.minimum(4.0, var_u + process_var)
287-288 var_v = np.minimum(4.0, var_v + process_var) var_w = np.minimum(4.0, var_w + process_var)
289-293 cov_uv = cov_uv * max(0.0, 1.0 - self.decay_per_step) pred_u = wind_u pred_v = wind_v
294 pred_w = wind_w
295 if arrays is not None and "wind_u" in arrays:
296 prior_u = np.asarray(arrays["wind_u"], dtype=np.float64)
297 prior_v = np.asarray(arrays["wind_v"], dtype=np.float64)
298 prior_w = np.asarray(arrays["wind_w"], dtype=np.float64)
299 prior_mag = np.abs(prior_u) + np.abs(prior_v) + np.abs(prior_w)
300 informed = (last_update > 0) | (prior_mag > 1e-6)
301 pred_noise = max(float(self.observation_noise), 0.10) + 0.05
302-303 ku = var_u / (var_u + pred_noise) kv = var_v / (var_v + pred_noise)
304-306 kw = var_w / (var_w + pred_noise) ku = np.where(informed, ku, 1.0)
307-308 kv = np.where(informed, kv, 1.0) kw = np.where(informed, kw, 1.0)
310 fresh = np.exp(-2.0 * self.decay_per_step * age)
311 resist = np.where(last_update > 0, 1.0 - 0.55 * fresh, 1.0)
316-317 innov_h = np.hypot(prior_u - pred_u, prior_v - pred_v) innov_w = np.abs(prior_w - pred_w)
318 edge_protect_obs = np.clip(0.55 * innov_h + 0.35 * innov_w, 0.0, 0.70)
319 edge_protect_prior = np.clip(0.28 * innov_h + 0.15 * innov_w, 0.0, 0.40)
320-322 edge_protect = np.where( last_update > 0, edge_protect_obs,
323-324 np.where(informed, edge_protect_prior, 0.0), )
325-327 gain_scale = resist * (1.0 - edge_protect) ku = ku * gain_scale kv = kv * gain_scale
328-329 kw = kw * gain_scale wind_u = prior_u + ku * (pred_u - prior_u)
330-331 wind_v = prior_v + kv * (pred_v - prior_v) wind_w = prior_w + kw * (pred_w - prior_w)
332 var_u = np.minimum(4.0, (1.0 - ku) * var_u + ku * pred_noise)
333 var_v = np.minimum(4.0, (1.0 - kv) * var_v + kv * pred_noise)
334 var_w = np.minimum(4.0, (1.0 - kw) * var_w + kw * pred_noise)
336 mode_entropy = _entropy_np(mode_s, mode_n, mode_u)
337 horizontal_speed = np.hypot(wind_u, wind_v)
338-339 uncertainty = ((var_u + var_v + var_w) / 3.0) + 0.25 * mode_entropy hazard = _clamp01_np(
340-341 0.58 * mode_s + 0.18 * _logistic_np((-wind_w - 0.25) * 1.7)
342-343 + 0.12 * _logistic_np(horizontal_speed - 5.0) + 0.10 * _clamp01_np(uncertainty / 3.0)
344-347 ) safety = _clamp01_np( 0.52 * hazard
348 + 0.28 * _clamp01_np(uncertainty / 3.0)
349 + 0.20 * _clamp01_np(np.maximum(-wind_w, 0.0) / 2.5)
350-353 ) expected = ( 0.55 * np.maximum(wind_w, 0.0)
354-356 - 0.85 * np.maximum(-wind_w, 0.0) + (1.25 * mode_u - 0.95 * mode_s) - 0.28 * safety
357-362 ) confidence = 1.0 / (1.0 + uncertainty) belief_map.field_arrays = { "wind_u": wind_u,
363-365 "wind_v": wind_v, "wind_w": wind_w, "mode_prob_sink": mode_s,
366-368 "mode_prob_neutral": mode_n, "mode_prob_uplift": mode_u, "wind_var_u": var_u,
369-371 "wind_var_v": var_v, "wind_var_w": var_w, "wind_cov_uv": cov_uv,
372-374 "belief_entropy": mode_entropy, "uncertainty": uncertainty, "hazard_prob": hazard,
375-377 "safety_penalty": safety, "expected_energy_gain": expected, "confidence": confidence,
378-379 "last_update": last_update, }
381-384 def update_with_observation( self, belief_map: BeliefMap, observation: Observation,

390-392 observation_radius: int | None = None, advect: bool = True, ) -> None:

395 observed_modes = _observe_mode_probs(observation.w_obs, energy_gain)

419-422 if y1 <= y0 or x1 <= x0: return yy, xx = np.mgrid[y0:y1, x0:x1]
423 distance = np.hypot(xx - observation.x, yy - observation.y)
424-425 influence = np.maximum(0.0, 1.0 - distance / (radius + 1)) mask = influence > 0.0
426-427 if not np.any(mask): return

430 wind_v = np.asarray(arrays["wind_v"][level, y0:y1, x0:x1], dtype=np.float64).copy()
431 wind_w = np.asarray(arrays["wind_w"][level, y0:y1, x0:x1], dtype=np.float64).copy()

433 var_v = np.asarray(arrays["wind_var_v"][level, y0:y1, x0:x1], dtype=np.float64).copy()

437 mode_n = np.asarray(arrays["mode_prob_neutral"][level, y0:y1, x0:x1], dtype=np.float64).copy()
438 mode_u = np.asarray(arrays["mode_prob_uplift"][level, y0:y1, x0:x1], dtype=np.float64).copy()
439 expected = np.asarray(arrays["expected_energy_gain"][level, y0:y1, x0:x1], dtype=np.float64).copy()
440 hazard = np.asarray(arrays["hazard_prob"][level, y0:y1, x0:x1], dtype=np.float64).copy()
441 safety = np.asarray(arrays["safety_penalty"][level, y0:y1, x0:x1], dtype=np.float64).copy()
443 measurement_var = self.observation_noise / np.maximum(influence * influence, 1e-6)
444-446 p00, p01, p10, p11 = var_u, cov_uv, cov_uv, var_v s00 = p00 + measurement_var s01 = p01
447-449 s10 = p10 s11 = p11 + measurement_var det_s = np.maximum((s00 * s11) - (s01 * s10), 1e-6)
450-453 inv_s00 = s11 / det_s inv_s01 = -s01 / det_s inv_s10 = -s10 / det_s inv_s11 = s00 / det_s
454-455 k00 = p00 * inv_s00 + p01 * inv_s10 k01 = p00 * inv_s01 + p01 * inv_s11
456-457 k10 = p10 * inv_s00 + p11 * inv_s10 k11 = p10 * inv_s01 + p11 * inv_s11
458-459 residual_u = observation.u_obs - wind_u residual_v = observation.v_obs - wind_v

464-465 wind_w = wind_w + kalman_w * residual_w predicted_residual = magnitude3(
466-467 observation.u_obs - predicted_u, observation.v_obs - predicted_v,
468-469 observation.w_obs - predicted_w, )
470-471 updated_p00 = (1.0 - k00) * p00 - k01 * p10 updated_p01 = (1.0 - k00) * p01 - k01 * p11
472-473 updated_p10 = -k10 * p00 + (1.0 - k11) * p10 updated_p11 = -k10 * p01 + (1.0 - k11) * p11
474-475 var_u = np.maximum(0.02, updated_p00) var_v = np.maximum(0.02, updated_p11)
476 var_w = np.maximum(0.02, (1.0 - kalman_w) * var_w)
477 cov_uv = np.clip(0.5 * (updated_p01 + updated_p10), -1.0, 1.0)
478-479 gain_scale = 0.5 * (k00 + k11) blend = _clamp01_np(gain_scale * influence + 0.18)
480-481 obs_s, obs_n, obs_u = observed_modes mode_s, mode_n, mode_u = _blend_mode_probs_np(
482 mode_s, mode_n, mode_u, obs_s, obs_n, obs_u, blend
483-484 ) mode_entropy = _entropy_np(mode_s, mode_n, mode_u)
485 expected = (1.0 - gain_scale * influence) * expected + (gain_scale * influence) * energy_gain
486-487 expected_from_wind = ( 0.55 * np.maximum(wind_w, 0.0)
488-490 - 0.85 * np.maximum(-wind_w, 0.0) + (1.25 * mode_u - 0.95 * mode_s) - 0.28 * safety
491-492 ) expected = 0.5 * expected + 0.5 * expected_from_wind
493 uncertainty = ((var_u + var_v + var_w) / 3.0) + 0.25 * mode_entropy
494 local_shear = np.hypot(wind_u - predicted_u, wind_v - predicted_v)
495-496 observed_hazard = _clamp01_np( 0.42 * mode_s
497 + 0.18 * _logistic_np((-observation.w_obs - 0.2) * 2.2)
498 + 0.16 * _clamp01_np(np.full_like(influence, predicted_residual / 3.0))
499-500 + 0.12 * _clamp01_np(local_shear / 4.0) + 0.12 * _clamp01_np(uncertainty / 3.0)
501-504 ) hazard = 0.65 * hazard + 0.35 * observed_hazard safety = _clamp01_np( 0.50 * hazard
505 + 0.25 * _clamp01_np(uncertainty / 3.0)
506 + 0.15 * _clamp01_np(abs(observation.acceleration) / 0.35)
507 + 0.10 * _clamp01_np(max(0.0, -observation.climb_rate) / 1.4)
508-509 ) confidence = 1.0 / (1.0 + uncertainty)
512-513 def _put(name: str, patch: np.ndarray) -> None: arr = arrays[name]

516-517 layer[mask] = patch[mask] arr[level, y0:y1, x0:x1] = layer

522-524 _put("wind_var_u", var_u) _put("wind_var_v", var_v) _put("wind_var_w", var_w)

527-528 _put("mode_prob_neutral", mode_n) _put("mode_prob_uplift", mode_u)

531-532 _put("hazard_prob", hazard) _put("safety_penalty", safety)
533-534 _put("expected_energy_gain", expected) _put("confidence", confidence)

536-537 last[mask] = float(step) arrays["last_update"][level, y0:y1, x0:x1] = last

563-566 def _update_with_observation_cells( self, belief_map: BeliefMap, observation: Observation,

661 def _advect_along_wind(self, belief_map: BeliefMap, x: int, y: int, z: int) -> None:

665 origin.wind_u = float(arrays["wind_u"][z, y, x])

698 cell.safety_penalty = float(arrays["safety_penalty"][z, ny, nx])

701 cell.wind_v = (1.0 - influence) * cell.wind_v + influence * origin.wind_v
702 cell.wind_w = (1.0 - influence) * cell.wind_w + influence * origin.wind_w
703 cell.wind_var_u = min(4.0, (1.0 - influence) * cell.wind_var_u + influence * (origin.wind_var_u + self.advection_noise))

743-745def _clamp_level(level: int, levels: int) -> int: if levels <= 0: return 0
746 return max(0, min(levels - 1, int(level)))
749def _observe_mode_probs(vertical_w: float, energy_gain: float) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
750 uplift_score = _logistic(2.6 * vertical_w + 0.9 * energy_gain)
751 sink_score = _logistic(-2.4 * vertical_w - 0.7 * energy_gain)
752 neutral_score = max(0.05, 1.0 - abs(vertical_w) - 0.22 * abs(energy_gain))
753 return _normalize_modes(sink_score, neutral_score, uplift_score)
756-757def _blend_mode_probs( prior: tuple[float, float, float],
758-760 observed: tuple[float, float, float], update_gain: float, ) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
761 sink = (1.0 - update_gain) * prior[0] + update_gain * observed[0]
762 neutral = (1.0 - update_gain) * prior[1] + update_gain * observed[1]
763 uplift = (1.0 - update_gain) * prior[2] + update_gain * observed[2]
764 return _normalize_modes(sink, neutral, uplift)
767-770def _blend_mode_probs_np( mode_s: np.ndarray, mode_n: np.ndarray, mode_u: np.ndarray,
771-774 obs_s: float, obs_n: float, obs_u: float, update_gain: np.ndarray,
775) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray, np.ndarray]:
776 sink = (1.0 - update_gain) * mode_s + update_gain * obs_s
777 neutral = (1.0 - update_gain) * mode_n + update_gain * obs_n
778-779 uplift = (1.0 - update_gain) * mode_u + update_gain * obs_u floor = 1e-6
780 total = np.maximum(sink + neutral + uplift, floor * 3.0)
781 return np.maximum(sink, floor) / total, np.maximum(neutral, floor) / total, np.maximum(uplift, floor) / total
784def _normalize_modes(sink: float, neutral: float, uplift: float) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
785-786 floor = 1e-6 total = max(sink + neutral + uplift, floor * 3.0)
787 return max(sink, floor) / total, max(neutral, floor) / total, max(uplift, floor) / total
790-791def _entropy(*probs: float) -> float: total = 0.0
792-794 for prob in probs: if prob > 1e-9: total -= prob * math.log(prob, 2)
795 return total
798-799def _entropy_np(*probs: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray: total = np.zeros_like(probs[0])
800-801 for prob in probs: safe = np.maximum(prob, 1e-12)
802 total = total - np.where(prob > 1e-9, prob * np.log2(safe), 0.0)
803 return total
806def _expected_energy_from_cell(cell: BeliefCell, horizontal_speed: float) -> float:
807 del horizontal_speed # unused: undirected speed is not an energy gain
808 mode_bias = (1.25 * cell.mode_prob_uplift) - (0.95 * cell.mode_prob_sink)
809-810 return ( 0.55 * max(cell.wind_w, 0.0)
811-813 - 0.85 * max(-cell.wind_w, 0.0) + mode_bias - 0.28 * cell.safety_penalty
814 )
817-819def _logistic(value: float) -> float: if value >= 0.0: exp_term = math.exp(-value)
820 return 1.0 / (1.0 + exp_term)
821 exp_term = math.exp(value)
822 return exp_term / (1.0 + exp_term)
825-826def _logistic_np(value: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray: return 1.0 / (1.0 + np.exp(-value))
829-830def _clamp01(value: float) -> float: return max(0.0, min(1.0, value))
833-834def _clamp01_np(value: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray: return np.clip(value, 0.0, 1.0)

57-58class CostBreakdown: energy_j: float
59-61 progress_reward_j: float uncertainty_cost_j: float safety_cost_j: float
62-64 altitude_bias_j: float vertical_maneuver_cost_j: float total_cost_j: float
67def normalize_state(point: tuple[int, int] | tuple[int, int, int]) -> State3D:
68-69 if len(point) == 2: return int(point[0]), int(point[1]), 0
70 return int(point[0]), int(point[1]), int(point[2])
73def neighbors(x: int, y: int, z: int, width: int, height: int, min_z: int, max_z: int) -> list[State3D]:
74 result: list[State3D] = []
75-77 for dz in (-1, 0, 1): for dy in (-1, 0, 1): for dx in (-1, 0, 1):
78-82 if dx == 0 and dy == 0 and dz == 0: continue nx = x + dx ny = y + dy nz = z + dz
83 if 0 <= nx < width and 0 <= ny < height and min_z <= nz <= max_z:
84 result.append((nx, ny, nz))
85 return result
88-92def effective_cost( belief_map: BeliefMap, current: State3D, nxt: State3D, mission: Mission,
93-95 risk_weight: float = 1.2, safety_weight: float = 1.5, ) -> float:
96-100 return transition_cost_breakdown( belief_map, current, nxt, mission,
101-103 risk_weight=risk_weight, safety_weight=safety_weight, ).total_cost_j
106-109def transition_cost_breakdown( belief_map: BeliefMap, current: State3D, nxt: State3D,
110-113 mission: Mission, risk_weight: float = 1.2, safety_weight: float = 1.5, ) -> CostBreakdown:
114-115 cx, cy, cz = current nx, ny, nz = nxt
116-117 cell = _sample_belief_state(belief_map, float(nx), float(ny), float(nz)) move_dx = nx - cx
118-120 move_dy = ny - cy move_dz = nz - cz move_norm = max(math.hypot(move_dx, move_dy), 1e-6)
121 tailwind = (cell["wind_u"] * (move_dx / move_norm)) + (cell["wind_v"] * (move_dy / move_norm))
122 wind_reward = max(-8.0, min(8.0, tailwind))
123 vertical_reward = 2.2 * cell["wind_w"] - 0.6 * max(move_dz, 0)
124 horizontal_to_goal = math.hypot(mission.goal[0] - nx, mission.goal[1] - ny)
125 altitude_bias_reward = _altitude_bonus(nz, mission, x=nx, y=ny, belief_cell=cell, move_dx=move_dx, move_dy=move_dy)
127 approach_scale = 1.0 if horizontal_to_goal <= 5.0 else 0.15
128 altitude_goal_reward = approach_scale * 2.4 * (
129 abs(goal_altitude_gap(current, mission) / max(mission.max_altitude_level - mission.min_altitude_level + 1, 1))
130 - abs(goal_altitude_gap(nxt, mission) / max(mission.max_altitude_level - mission.min_altitude_level + 1, 1))
131-133 ) transition_energy = transition_energy_j( airspeed=mission.nominal_airspeed,
134-137 current=current, nxt=nxt, local_u=cell["wind_u"], local_v=cell["wind_v"],
138-139 local_w=cell["wind_w"], step_distance_m=mission.step_distance_m,
140 altitude_step_m=mission.altitude_step_m,
141 climb_cost_per_level_j=mission.climb_cost_per_level_j,
142-143 hover_power_w=mission.hover_power_w, cruise_power_w=mission.cruise_power_w,
144 hotel_power_w=mission.hotel_power_w,
145 headwind_power_per_mps_w=mission.headwind_power_per_mps_w,
146 climb_power_per_mps_w=mission.climb_power_per_mps_w,
147 descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w=mission.descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w,
148-149 terrain_dz_m=_mission_terrain_dz(mission, cx, cy, nx, ny), )
150 progress_reward = 18.0 * (
151 cell["expected_energy_gain"] + 0.35 * wind_reward + vertical_reward + altitude_goal_reward
152-153 ) uncertainty_cost = 22.0 * risk_weight * max(cell["uncertainty"], 0.0)
154 safety_cost = 28.0 * safety_weight * cell["safety_penalty"]
155 altitude_bias_cost = -22.0 * altitude_bias_reward
156 vertical_maneuver_cost = mission.climb_cost_per_level_j * max(move_dz, 0) + 0.2 * mission.climb_cost_per_level_j * max(-move_dz, 0)
157 goal_altitude_direction = goal_altitude_gap(current, mission) - goal_altitude_gap(nxt, mission)
158-159 if horizontal_to_goal <= 5.0: if goal_altitude_direction < 0:
160 vertical_maneuver_cost += 1.4 * mission.climb_cost_per_level_j * abs(goal_altitude_direction)
161 elif goal_altitude_direction > 0:
162 vertical_maneuver_cost -= 0.9 * mission.climb_cost_per_level_j * abs(goal_altitude_direction)
163-165 else: if move_dz > 0 and (cell["wind_w"] > 0.15 or wind_reward > 0.5):
166 vertical_maneuver_cost *= 0.35
167-168 elif move_dz > 0 and wind_reward < -0.5: vertical_maneuver_cost *= 1.4
169 total_cost = transition_energy - progress_reward + uncertainty_cost + safety_cost + altitude_bias_cost + vertical_maneuver_cost
170-172 return CostBreakdown( energy_j=transition_energy, progress_reward_j=progress_reward,
173-174 uncertainty_cost_j=uncertainty_cost, safety_cost_j=safety_cost,
175-176 altitude_bias_j=altitude_bias_cost, vertical_maneuver_cost_j=vertical_maneuver_cost,
177-178 total_cost_j=total_cost, )
181def heuristic(point: State3D, goal: State3D, mission: Mission) -> float:
182 horizontal = math.hypot(goal[0] - point[0], goal[1] - point[1]) * mission.step_distance_m
183 vertical = abs(goal[2] - point[2]) * mission.altitude_step_m
184 return math.hypot(horizontal, vertical)
187-189def estimate_energy_to_goal_j( point: tuple[float, float, float], goal: State3D,
190-191 mission: Mission, local_wind: dict[str, float] | None = None,
192-194 belief_map: BeliefMap | None = None, probe_corridors: bool = False, ) -> float:
195 """Residual energy to goal; samples wind/terrain along the remaining segment.
197 When probe_corridors=True and a belief map is available, also scores mild
198-199 lateral offsets and returns the cheapest option. """
200-204 energies = estimate_energies_to_goal_batch( [point], goal, mission, belief_map=belief_map,
205 local_winds=[local_wind] if local_wind is not None else None,
206-207 probe_corridors=probe_corridors, )
208 return float(energies[0])
211-212def estimate_energies_to_goal_batch( points: list[tuple[float, float, float]],
213-215 goal: State3D, mission: Mission, belief_map: BeliefMap | None = None,
216-217 local_winds: list[dict[str, float] | None] | None = None, probe_corridors: bool = False,
218-219) -> list[float]: """Batch residual energy-to-goal for many points (same goal)."""
220-222 if not points: return [] gx, gy, gz = float(goal[0]), float(goal[1]), float(goal[2])
223 n = len(points)
225-226 if belief_map is None: out: list[float] = []
227-228 for i, point in enumerate(points): local = None if local_winds is None else local_winds[i]
229 out.append(_segment_energy_closed(point, (gx, gy, gz), mission, local))
230 return out
232-235 fracs = (0.2, 0.55, 0.85) xs: list[float] = [] ys: list[float] = [] zs: list[float] = []
236 meta: list[tuple[float, float, float, float, float]] = [] # x0,y0,x1,y1,z
237-238 for point in points: x0, y0, z = float(point[0]), float(point[1]), float(point[2])
239 meta.append((x0, y0, gx, gy, z))
240-241 for f in fracs: xs.append(x0 + f * (gx - x0))
242-243 ys.append(y0 + f * (gy - y0)) zs.append(z)
244 wind = _sample_wind_batch(belief_map, xs, ys, zs)
245 wu = np.asarray(wind["wind_u"], dtype=np.float64).reshape(n, 3)
246 wv = np.asarray(wind["wind_v"], dtype=np.float64).reshape(n, 3)
247 ww = np.asarray(wind["wind_w"], dtype=np.float64).reshape(n, 3)
248 xs0 = np.asarray([m[0] for m in meta], dtype=np.float64)
249 ys0 = np.asarray([m[1] for m in meta], dtype=np.float64)
250 xs1 = np.asarray([m[2] for m in meta], dtype=np.float64)
251 ys1 = np.asarray([m[3] for m in meta], dtype=np.float64)
252-254 zs_arr = np.asarray([m[4] for m in meta], dtype=np.float64) dx = xs1 - xs0 dy = ys1 - ys0
255-256 horiz_m = np.hypot(dx, dy) * mission.step_distance_m bearings = np.arctan2(dy, dx)
257-258 cos_b = np.cos(bearings) sin_b = np.sin(bearings)
259 tw = np.mean(wu * cos_b[:, None] + wv * sin_b[:, None], axis=1)
260-261 uplift = np.mean(ww, axis=1) airspeed = max(mission.nominal_airspeed, 4.0)
262 groundspeed = np.maximum(4.0, airspeed + tw)
263 time_s = np.where(horiz_m > 1e-6, horiz_m / groundspeed, 0.0)
264-266 headwind = np.maximum(0.0, -tw) nominal_power_w = ( mission.cruise_power_w
267-268 + mission.hotel_power_w + mission.headwind_power_per_mps_w * headwind
269 - 18.0 * np.maximum(0.0, uplift)
270-272 ) base = time_s * np.maximum(40.0, nominal_power_w) dz_levels = gz - zs_arr
273-275 climb = np.where( dz_levels >= 0.0, dz_levels * mission.climb_cost_per_level_j,
276-277 dz_levels * mission.altitude_step_m * mission.descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w, )
278-279 base = base + climb elev = getattr(mission, "elevation", None)
280 if elev is not None:
281 rises = terrain_climb_along_line_batch(elev, xs0, ys0, xs1, ys1, samples=3)
282 climb_scale = mission.climb_cost_per_level_j / max(mission.altitude_step_m, 1e-6)
283 base = base + np.where(horiz_m > 1e-6, rises * climb_scale, 0.0)
284 out = base.tolist()
286 if probe_corridors and belief_map is not None:
287-288 for i, (x0, y0, x1, y1, z) in enumerate(meta): horiz_cells = math.hypot(gx - x0, gy - y0)
289-290 if horiz_cells <= 6.0: continue
291 px, py = -(gy - y0) / horiz_cells, (gx - x0) / horiz_cells
292 for sign in (-1.0, 1.0):
293 via = (x0 + 0.5 * (gx - x0) + sign * 1.5 * px, y0 + 0.5 * (gy - y0) + sign * 1.5 * py, z)
294 via_e = estimate_energy_to_goal_j(via, goal, mission, belief_map=belief_map, probe_corridors=False)
295-296 out[i] = min( out[i],
297-298 via_e + 35.0 * 1.5 * mission.step_distance_m / max(mission.nominal_airspeed, 4.0), )
299 return out
302-303def _segment_energy_closed( point: tuple[float, float, float],
304-306 goal: tuple[float, float, float], mission: Mission, local_wind: dict[str, float] | None,
307-308) -> float: x0, y0, z = float(point[0]), float(point[1]), float(point[2])
309-310 gx, gy, gz = goal horiz_m = math.hypot(gx - x0, gy - y0) * mission.step_distance_m
311-312 if horiz_m <= 1e-6: base = 0.0
313-314 else: bearing = math.atan2(gy - y0, gx - x0)
315-316 tw = 0.0 uplift = 0.0
317 if local_wind is not None:
318 tw = local_wind.get("wind_u", 0.0) * math.cos(bearing) + local_wind.get("wind_v", 0.0) * math.sin(bearing)
319 uplift = float(local_wind.get("wind_w", 0.0))
320-321 airspeed = max(mission.nominal_airspeed, 4.0) groundspeed = max(4.0, airspeed + tw)
322-324 time_s = horiz_m / groundspeed headwind = max(0.0, -tw) nominal_power_w = (
325-326 mission.cruise_power_w + mission.hotel_power_w
327-328 + mission.headwind_power_per_mps_w * headwind - 18.0 * max(0.0, uplift)
329-331 ) base = time_s * max(40.0, nominal_power_w) terrain_rise_m = terrain_climb_along_line_m(
332 getattr(mission, "elevation", None), x0, y0, gx, gy, samples=3
333 )
334 base += (terrain_rise_m / max(mission.altitude_step_m, 1e-6)) * mission.climb_cost_per_level_j
335 dz_levels = gz - z
336-337 if dz_levels >= 0: base += dz_levels * mission.climb_cost_per_level_j
338 else:
339 base += dz_levels * mission.altitude_step_m * mission.descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w
340 return base
343-346def plan_path( belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission, risk_weight: float = 1.2,

356-357 terminal_progress_weight: float = 24.0, ) -> list[State3D]:

362-363 safety_weight=safety_weight, anytime_rounds=anytime_rounds,
364-365 heuristic_weight_start=heuristic_weight_start, heuristic_weight_end=heuristic_weight_end,

375-378def plan_path_details( belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission, risk_weight: float = 1.2,
379-381 safety_weight: float = 1.5, anytime_rounds: int = 4, heuristic_weight_start: float = 2.4,
382-384 heuristic_weight_end: float = 1.0, search_node_budget: int = 9000, horizon_steps: int = 7,
385-387 beam_width: int = 28, branch_width: int = 10, discount_factor: float = 0.93,
388-389 terminal_progress_weight: float = 24.0, return_cost_map_cache: object | None = None,
390-391 truth_field: dict | None = None, ) -> dict:
395 elev_src = getattr(mission, "elevation", None)
396 if elev_src is not None and not isinstance(elev_src, np.ndarray):
397 mission.elevation = np.asarray(elev_src, dtype=np.float64)
399-400 start = _continuous_state_tuple(mission.start) goal = normalize_state(mission.goal)
401 horizontal_to_goal = math.hypot(goal[0] - start[0], goal[1] - start[1])
402-405 guide_horizon_cells = 50.0 / max(float(mission.step_distance_m), 1e-6) use_return_map = (
406-407 mission.home is not None and mission.max_return_cost_j is not None
408-409 and horizontal_to_goal > 8.0 and _return_budget_is_tight(mission, start, goal)
410-411 ) want_guides = horizontal_to_goal > guide_horizon_cells
412 sticky_cruise = getattr(mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None)
413 clearance_for_skip = float(getattr(mission, "clearance_agl_level", 1.0))
416 high_wind_sticky = (
417 sticky_cruise is not None and float(sticky_cruise) > clearance_for_skip + 1.2 + 1e-9
418-424 ) sticky_open_cruise = ( sticky_cruise is not None
425-426 and want_guides and not use_return_map
427-428 and horizontal_to_goal > max(guide_horizon_cells, 8.0) and not high_wind_sticky
429-432 ) guide_future = None return_future = None pool: ThreadPoolExecutor | None = None
433 return_cost_map = return_cost_map_cache if (use_return_map and return_cost_map_cache is not None) else None
434 need_return_compute = use_return_map and return_cost_map is None
435 workers = int(want_guides) + int(need_return_compute)
438-439 if os.environ.get("WINDFARM_N_JOBS") and workers > 1: workers = 1
440-441 if workers > 0: pool = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=workers)
442-443 if want_guides: guide_future = pool.submit(
444 _energy_guide_paths, start, goal, belief_map, mission, truth_field
445 )
446-449 if need_return_compute: if workers == 1 and want_guides: return_future = None
450 return_cost_map = compute_return_cost_map(belief_map, mission)
451-452 else: return_future = pool.submit(compute_return_cost_map, belief_map, mission)
454-455 if return_future is not None: return_cost_map = return_future.result()
457 candidates: list[dict] = []
458-459 if sticky_open_cruise: best_path = _greedy_progress_path(start, goal, belief_map, mission)
460-463 planning_mode = "greedy_progress" candidates.append( { "mode": planning_mode,
464-467 "path": best_path, "path_cost": 0.0, "note": "sticky_cruise_skip_mpc", }
468 )
469-472 else: strict_result = _run_anytime_continuous_mpc( belief_map=belief_map, mission=mission,
473-475 risk_weight=risk_weight, safety_weight=safety_weight, anytime_rounds=anytime_rounds,
476-477 heuristic_weight_start=heuristic_weight_start, heuristic_weight_end=heuristic_weight_end,
478-479 search_node_budget=search_node_budget, horizon_steps=horizon_steps,
480-482 beam_width=beam_width, branch_width=branch_width, discount_factor=discount_factor,
483-484 terminal_progress_weight=terminal_progress_weight, return_cost_map=return_cost_map,
485-486 candidate_mode="strict_return", )
487-488 best_path = strict_result["path"] candidates = strict_result["candidates"]
489-491 planning_mode = "mpc_strict_return" needs_relaxed = return_cost_map is not None and (
492-494 not best_path or len(best_path) <= 1 or not _continuous_goal_reached(best_path[-1], goal)
495 )
496 if needs_relaxed or horizontal_to_goal <= 8.0:
497-499 relaxed_result = _run_anytime_continuous_mpc( belief_map=belief_map, mission=mission,
500-501 risk_weight=risk_weight, safety_weight=safety_weight,
502-503 anytime_rounds=max(2, anytime_rounds), heuristic_weight_start=heuristic_weight_start,
504 heuristic_weight_end=heuristic_weight_end,
505-506 search_node_budget=max(search_node_budget // 2, 2000), horizon_steps=horizon_steps,
507-508 beam_width=max(beam_width // 2, 8), branch_width=branch_width,
509 discount_factor=discount_factor,
510-511 terminal_progress_weight=terminal_progress_weight * 1.35, return_cost_map=None,
512-513 candidate_mode="relaxed_return", )
514 candidates.extend(relaxed_result["candidates"])
515 if relaxed_result["path"] and (
516-517 _continuous_goal_reached(relaxed_result["path"][-1], goal) or len(best_path) <= 1
518 or heuristic(_continuous_state_tuple(relaxed_result["path"][-1]), goal, mission)
519 < heuristic(_continuous_state_tuple(best_path[-1]), goal, mission)
520-521 ): best_path = relaxed_result["path"]
522 planning_mode = "mpc_relaxed_return"
524 best_path = best_path[: mission.max_steps + 1]
525-526 if len(best_path) <= 1: best_path = _greedy_progress_path(start, goal, belief_map, mission)
527 planning_mode = "greedy_progress"
530 guide_paths: list[tuple[str, list[tuple[float, float, float]]]] = []
531-533 try: if guide_future is not None: guide_paths = guide_future.result()
534 elif want_guides:
535 guide_paths = _energy_guide_paths(start, goal, belief_map, mission, truth_field)
536-539 finally: if pool is not None: pool.shutdown(wait=False) ranked = []
540 mpc_completed_energy = math.inf
541-542 if best_path and len(best_path) > 1: ranked.append((planning_mode, best_path))
543 mpc_completed_energy = _completed_plan_energy_j(best_path, goal, belief_map, mission)
544-546 for label, path in guide_paths: ranked.append((label, path)) best_energy = math.inf
547-548 best_label = planning_mode chosen = best_path
549 clearance = float(getattr(mission, "clearance_agl_level", 1.0))
550 straight_items: list[tuple[float, str, list[tuple[float, float, float]]]] = []
551 for label, path in guide_paths:
552-553 if not label.startswith("guide_straight_agl") or not path or len(path) <= 1: continue
554 cruise_z = _cruise_z_from_guide_label(label, clearance, mission)
555 straight_items.append((cruise_z, label, path))
556 straight_energies: dict[float, float] = {}
557 straight_paths: dict[float, tuple[str, list[tuple[float, float, float]]]] = {}
558 if straight_items:
559 energies = _completed_plans_energy_j([p for _, _, p in straight_items], goal, belief_map, mission)
560 for (cruise_z, label, path), energy in zip(straight_items, energies):
561-562 straight_energies[cruise_z] = energy straight_paths[cruise_z] = (label, path)
563 straight_guide_energy = min(straight_energies.values()) if straight_energies else math.inf
564 bands = list(straight_energies.keys()) if straight_energies else _cruise_band_candidates(belief_map, mission)
565 sticky_for_scores = getattr(mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None)
566-568 if sticky_for_scores is not None and bands: coarse = 0.25
569 near = []
570-571 for z in bands: zf = float(z)
572 on_coarse = abs(zf / coarse - round(zf / coarse)) <= 1e-9
573-574 if ( abs(zf - float(sticky_for_scores)) <= 0.40 + 1e-9
575-576 or abs(zf - clearance) <= 0.15 + 1e-9 or on_coarse
577-578 ): near.append(z)
579-580 if near: bands = near
581 step = _cruise_band_step(mission)
585 if straight_energies:
586 select_scores = {z: straight_energies[z] for z in bands if z in straight_energies}
587-588 if not select_scores: select_scores = dict(straight_energies)
589 cruise_fair: dict[float, float] = {}
590-591 else: cruise_fair = _band_selection_scores(start, goal, belief_map, mission, bands)
592 select_scores = cruise_fair
593 raw_band_scores = dict(select_scores) if select_scores else {}
594-595 sticky_now = getattr(mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None) sz_now = float(start[2])
599-601 route_terrain_rise = _mission_route_terrain_rise_m(mission) ambient_wind_mps = 0.0
602 probe_z = float(getattr(mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None) or clearance)
603-605 probe_path = _agl_guide_polyline( (float(start[0]), float(start[1]), probe_z), goal,
606-610 belief_map, mission, via_xy=None, cruise_z=probe_z, )
611 if len(probe_path) > 1:
612 ambient_wind_mps, _, _ = _path_wind_utilization_stats(probe_path, belief_map)
614 if select_scores and step <= 0.26 + 1e-12:
615 floor_z = min(select_scores.keys(), key=lambda z: abs(z - clearance))
616-618 floor_e = max(select_scores[floor_z], 1.0) pen = 0.01 * floor_e select_scores = {
619 z: e + pen * max(0.0, float(z) - clearance) for z, e in select_scores.items()
620 }
622 if sticky_now is not None and sz_now >= clearance + 0.25 and select_scores and horizontal_to_goal > 8.0:
623 climb_cost = float(getattr(mission, "climb_cost_per_level_j", 180.0))
624-626 hold = min(sz_now, float(sticky_now)) select_scores = { z: (
627 e + 0.35 * climb_cost * max(0.0, hold - float(z))
628-629 if abs(float(z) - hold) <= 0.55 else e
630 )
631 for z, e in select_scores.items()
632 }
634 if raw_band_scores:
635 clr_key = min(raw_band_scores.keys(), key=lambda z: abs(float(z) - clearance))
636 stk_key = min(raw_band_scores.keys(), key=lambda z: abs(float(z) - float(sticky_now)))
637 if raw_band_scores[stk_key] > raw_band_scores[clr_key] * 1.02:
638 select_scores = dict(raw_band_scores)
639-640 if step <= 0.26 + 1e-12: floor_e = max(select_scores[clr_key], 1.0)
641-642 pen = 0.01 * floor_e select_scores = {
643 z: e + pen * max(0.0, float(z) - clearance) for z, e in select_scores.items()
644 }
649 if select_scores:
650 ms_od = getattr(mission, "home", None) or getattr(mission, "start", start)
651-652 if ( _route_axis_aspect(ms_od, goal) <= ROUTE_AXIS_ASPECT_MAX
653 and _od_along_wind_mps(
654 ms_od, goal, belief_map, truth_field=truth_field, cruise_z=float(clearance)

1279-1281def _greedy_progress_path( start: tuple[float, float, float], goal: State3D,
1282-1284 belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission, ) -> list[tuple[float, float, float]]:
1285 """Guarantee a non-empty next waypoint toward the goal when MPC collapses."""
1286-1287 min_level, max_level = _search_level_bounds(belief_map, mission) dx = goal[0] - start[0]
1288-1289 dy = goal[1] - start[1] horizontal = math.hypot(dx, dy)
1290 step = min(1.0, horizontal) if horizontal > 1e-6 else 0.0
1291-1292 if horizontal > 1e-6: nx = start[0] + (dx / horizontal) * step
1293 ny = start[1] + (dy / horizontal) * step
1294-1295 else: nx, ny = start[0], start[1]
1296-1297 if horizontal > 2.5: floor = _effective_agl_floor(mission, horizontal)
1298-1302 preferred = _preferred_cruise_altitude( belief_map, start[0], start[1], goal,
1303-1305 max(min_level, int(math.floor(floor))), max_level, current_z=start[2],
1306 climb_cost_per_level_j=mission.climb_cost_per_level_j,
1307 clearance_agl_level=getattr(mission, "clearance_agl_level", 1.0),
1308-1309 preferred_cruise_agl=getattr(mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None), )
1310-1311 preferred = max(preferred, floor) dz = clamp(preferred - start[2], -1.0, 1.0)
1312-1313 else: dz = clamp(goal[2] - start[2], -1.5, 1.5)
1314-1315 if abs(goal[2] - start[2]) > 0.2: nx, ny = start[0], start[1]
1316 floor_now = _effective_agl_floor(mission, horizontal)
1317 nz = clamp(start[2] + dz, max(float(min_level), floor_now if horizontal > 2.5 else float(min_level)), float(max_level))
1318-1322 nxt = ( clamp(nx, 0.0, belief_map.width - 1), clamp(ny, 0.0, belief_map.height - 1), nz, )
1323 if math.hypot(nxt[0] - start[0], nxt[1] - start[1]) < 1e-4 and abs(nxt[2] - start[2]) < 1e-4:
1324 return [start]
1325 return [start, nxt]
1328-1330def _polyline_model_energy_j( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]], belief_map: BeliefMap,
1331-1332 mission: Mission, ) -> float:
1333 """Same accounting family as eval baselines: transition_energy_j + uplift discount."""
1334 energies = _polylines_model_energy_j([path], belief_map, mission)
1335 return energies[0] if energies else 0.0
1338-1339def _polylines_model_energy_j( paths: list[list[tuple[float, float, float]]],
1340-1342 belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission, ) -> list[float]:
1343 """Batch-score many polylines in one transition_energy_batch call."""
1344-1346 if not paths: return [] counts: list[int] = []
1347 cur_rows: list[tuple[float, float, float]] = []
1348 nxt_rows: list[tuple[float, float, float]] = []
1349-1351 for path in paths: if len(path) <= 1: counts.append(0)
1352 continue
1353-1354 n = len(path) - 1 counts.append(n)
1355-1356 for a, b in zip(path, path[1:]): cur_rows.append((float(a[0]), float(a[1]), float(a[2])))
1357 nxt_rows.append((float(b[0]), float(b[1]), float(b[2])))
1358-1359 if not cur_rows: return [0.0 for _ in paths]
1361 currents = np.asarray(cur_rows, dtype=np.float64)
1362 nexts = np.asarray(nxt_rows, dtype=np.float64)
1363 samples = _sample_belief_states_batch(belief_map, currents[:, 0], currents[:, 1], currents[:, 2])
1364 elev = getattr(mission, "elevation", None)
1365-1368 if elev is not None: terrain_dz = terrain_delta_m_batch( elev, currents[:, 0],
1369-1372 currents[:, 1], nexts[:, 0], nexts[:, 1], )
1373-1374 else: terrain_dz = np.zeros(currents.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
1375-1377 step_e = transition_energy_batch( airspeed=mission.nominal_airspeed, currents=currents,
1378-1380 nexts=nexts, local_u=samples["wind_u"], local_v=samples["wind_v"],
1381-1382 local_w=samples["wind_w"], step_distance_m=mission.step_distance_m,
1383 altitude_step_m=mission.altitude_step_m,
1384 climb_cost_per_level_j=mission.climb_cost_per_level_j,
1385-1386 hover_power_w=mission.hover_power_w, cruise_power_w=mission.cruise_power_w,
1387 hotel_power_w=mission.hotel_power_w,
1388 headwind_power_per_mps_w=mission.headwind_power_per_mps_w,
1389 climb_power_per_mps_w=mission.climb_power_per_mps_w,
1390 descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w=mission.descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w,
1391-1392 terrain_dz_m=terrain_dz, )
1394-1395 out: list[float] = [] offset = 0
1396-1398 for n in counts: if n <= 0: out.append(0.0)
1399-1400 else: out.append(float(np.sum(step_e[offset : offset + n])))
1401 offset += n
1402 return out
1405-1407def _path_mean_uncertainty( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]], belief_map: BeliefMap,
1408-1410) -> float: if len(path) <= 1: return 1.0
1411 pts = np.asarray(path[:-1], dtype=np.float64)
1412 samples = _sample_belief_states_batch(belief_map, pts[:, 0], pts[:, 1], pts[:, 2])
1413 return float(np.mean(samples["uncertainty"]))
1416-1417def _path_wind_utilization_stats( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]],
1418-1421 belief_map: BeliefMap, *, max_samples: int = 24, speed_q: float | None = None,
1422) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
1423 """Horizontal wind (m/s), mean w, mean uplift probability along a path.
1425 ``speed_q``: if set (e.g. 0.75), use that quantile of path speed instead of the
1426-1427 mean — better for localized shear corridors without diluting the edge. """
1428-1430 if len(path) <= 1: return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0 pts = path[:-1]
1431-1432 if len(pts) > max_samples: idx = np.linspace(0, len(pts) - 1, max_samples).astype(np.int32)
1433 pts = [pts[int(i)] for i in idx]
1434 arr = np.asarray(pts, dtype=np.float64)
1435 samples = _sample_belief_states_batch(belief_map, arr[:, 0], arr[:, 1], arr[:, 2])
1436 speed = np.hypot(samples["wind_u"], samples["wind_v"])
1437 uplift = samples.get("mode_prob_uplift")
1438 mean_uplift = float(np.mean(uplift)) if uplift is not None else 0.0
1439-1440 if speed_q is None: speed_val = float(np.mean(speed))
1441-1442 else: speed_val = float(np.quantile(speed, float(speed_q)))
1443 return speed_val, float(np.mean(samples["wind_w"])), mean_uplift

1600-1602def _belief_map_from_truth_field( belief_map: BeliefMap, truth_field: dict,
1603) -> BeliefMap | None:
1604 """Belief whose wind_* layers mirror truth (scoring only; arrays-only + cached)."""

1611-1613 int(belief_map.levels), id(u_obj), )
1614 hit = _TRUTH_BELIEF_CACHE.get(key)
1615-1616 if hit is not None: return hit
1617 out = create_scoring_belief_map(belief_map.width, belief_map.height, belief_map.levels)
1618 for attr, key_name in (("wind_u", "u"), ("wind_v", "v"), ("wind_w", "w")):
1619-1620 if key_name not in truth_field: continue

1624-1625 if len(_TRUTH_BELIEF_CACHE) >= 8: _TRUTH_BELIEF_CACHE.clear()
1626 _TRUTH_BELIEF_CACHE[key] = out
1627 return out
1630-1631def _corridor_truth_beats_straight( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]],
1632-1633 straight_path: list[tuple[float, float, float]] | None, truth_belief: BeliefMap | None,
1634-1637 mission: Mission, *, win_need: float = CORRIDOR_TRUTH_WIN_NEED, ) -> bool:
1638 """True when corridor Joules under truth ≤ straight * win_need (or no truth)."""
1639-1640 if truth_belief is None: return True
1641-1642 if straight_path is None or len(straight_path) <= 1 or len(path) <= 1: return True
1643 straight_e = _polyline_model_energy_j(straight_path, truth_belief, mission)
1644 path_e = _polyline_model_energy_j(path, truth_belief, mission)
1645 if not math.isfinite(straight_e) or not math.isfinite(path_e) or straight_e <= 0.0:
1646 return True
1647 return path_e <= straight_e * float(win_need)
1650def _parse_corridor_prior_via(label: str) -> tuple[float, float] | None:
1651 """Extract distilled prior via from ``guide_corridor_prior_{x}_{y}_z…`` labels."""
1652-1653 if not str(label).startswith("guide_corridor_prior_"): return None
1654 parts = str(label).split("_")
1655-1656 try: i = parts.index("prior")
1657 return (float(parts[i + 1]), float(parts[i + 2]))
1658-1659 except (ValueError, IndexError): return None
1662-1663def _via_is_midroute_lock( mission: Mission,
1664-1666 via: tuple[float, float] | tuple[float, float, float], *, min_span_cells: float = 8.0,
1667-1668) -> bool: """True when via is far from launch — a real prior, not a near-term path cell."""
1669 od = getattr(mission, "home", None) or getattr(mission, "start", None)
1670-1671 if od is None: return False
1672 return math.hypot(float(via[0]) - float(od[0]), float(via[1]) - float(od[1])) >= min_span_cells
1675-1677def _cruise_releases_lateral_via( mission: Mission, clearance: float,
1678-1679 cruise_z: float | None = None, ) -> bool:
1680 """Any climb above clearance drops XY locks.
1682 taiwan r0: prior@z1 then straight@z≈2.7. sichuan micro-layers (~clearance+0.07)
1683-1684 also need the via gone so the micro hold is not fought by a lateral lock. """
1685-1687 if bool(getattr(mission, "cruise_climb_earned", False)): return True z = cruise_z
1688-1689 if z is None: z = getattr(mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None)
1690 return z is not None and float(z) > float(clearance) + 0.05 + 1e-9
1693def _load_sticky_score_config() -> dict | None:
1694 """Load optional logistic sticky weights from WINDFARM_STICKY_SCORE_JSON."""

1700 return None

1712 return _STICKY_SCORE_CACHE
1715def _od_cross_wind_mps(start, goal, belief_map, truth_field=None, cruise_z: float = 1.0) -> float:
1716 """Mean cross-track wind on the full OD (positive = left of track)."""
1717-1718 sx, sy = float(start[0]), float(start[1]) gx, gy = float(goal[0]), float(goal[1])
1719-1720 dx, dy = gx - sx, gy - sy span = math.hypot(dx, dy)
1721-1723 if span < 1e-9: return 0.0 nx, ny = -dy / span, dx / span
1724 if truth_field is not None and "u" in truth_field and "v" in truth_field:
1725-1726 n = max(8, min(24, int(span) + 1)) total = 0.0
1727-1728 for i in range(n): t = i / max(n - 1, 1)
1729 x, y = sx + dx * t, sy + dy * t
1730 u = float(trilinear_sample(truth_field["u"], x, y, cruise_z))
1731-1732 v = float(trilinear_sample(truth_field["v"], x, y, cruise_z)) total += u * nx + v * ny
1733 return total / n
1734-1737 a = (sx, sy, float(cruise_z)) b = (gx, gy, float(cruise_z)) path = [a, b]
1738 arr = np.asarray(path, dtype=np.float64)
1739 samples = _sample_belief_states_batch(belief_map, arr[:, 0], arr[:, 1], arr[:, 2])
1740 total = 0.0
1741 for i in range(len(path)):
1742 total += float(samples["wind_u"][i]) * nx + float(samples["wind_v"][i]) * ny
1743 return total / max(len(path), 1)
1746-1747def _full_od_via_truth_margin( mission: Mission,
1748-1750 via: tuple[float, float] | tuple[float, float, float], goal, belief_map: BeliefMap,
1751-1754 truth_field: dict | None, *, cruise_z: float, ) -> float | None:
1755 """Return 1 - E_via/E_straight under truth, or None if unavailable."""
1756-1757 if truth_field is None or "u" not in truth_field: return None
1758 od = getattr(mission, "home", None) or getattr(mission, "start", None)
1759-1761 if od is None: return None origin = (float(od[0]), float(od[1]), float(cruise_z))
1762-1766 locked_path = _agl_guide_polyline( origin, goal, belief_map, mission,
1767-1769 via_xy=(float(via[0]), float(via[1])), cruise_z=float(cruise_z), )
1770 straight_path = _agl_guide_polyline(
1771 origin, goal, belief_map, mission, via_xy=None, cruise_z=float(cruise_z)
1772 )
1773-1774 if len(locked_path) <= 1 or len(straight_path) <= 1: return None
1775 truth_belief = _belief_map_from_truth_field(belief_map, truth_field)
1776-1777 if truth_belief is None: return None
1778 straight_e = _polyline_model_energy_j(straight_path, truth_belief, mission)
1779 path_e = _polyline_model_energy_j(locked_path, truth_belief, mission)
1780 if not math.isfinite(straight_e) or not math.isfinite(path_e) or straight_e <= 0.0:
1781 return None
1782 return 1.0 - float(path_e) / float(straight_e)
1785-1788def _along_allows_prior_sticky( mission: Mission, goal, belief_map: BeliefMap,
1789-1791 truth_field: dict | None, *, cruise_z: float,
1792 via: tuple[float, float] | tuple[float, float, float] | None = None,
1793-1794 remain_horiz: float | None = None, ) -> bool:
1795-1797 """Whether midroute prior sticky is allowed. Legacy mode (default): hard mild-along band.
1798-1799 Logistic mode (WINDFARM_STICKY_SCORE_JSON with mode=logistic): continuous score. """
1800 od = getattr(mission, "home", None) or getattr(mission, "start", None)
1801-1803 if od is None: return False along = _od_along_wind_mps(
1804 od, goal, belief_map, truth_field=truth_field, cruise_z=float(cruise_z)
1805 )
1809 if not (
1810 float(CORRIDOR_AXIS_HEADWIND_SKIP_MPS) < float(along) < float(PRIOR_STICKY_ALONG_MAX_MPS)
1811-1813 ): return False cfg = _load_sticky_score_config()
1814 mode = str((cfg or {}).get("mode", "legacy")).lower()
1815-1818 if cfg is None or mode == "legacy": return True cross = _od_cross_wind_mps(
1819 od, goal, belief_map, truth_field=truth_field, cruise_z=float(cruise_z)
1820-1821 ) margin = 0.0
1822-1823 if via is not None: m = _full_od_via_truth_margin(
1824 mission, via, goal, belief_map, truth_field, cruise_z=float(cruise_z)
1825 )
1826-1827 if m is not None: margin = float(m)
1828 full_od = math.hypot(float(goal[0]) - float(od[0]), float(goal[1]) - float(od[1]))
1829-1830 if remain_horiz is None: remain_horiz = full_od
1831 remain_frac = float(remain_horiz) / max(full_od, 1e-6)
1832-1833 remain_frac = max(0.0, min(remain_frac, 1.5)) aspect = _route_axis_aspect(od, goal)
1835-1837 bias = float(cfg.get("bias", 0.0)) logit = ( bias
1838 + float(cfg.get("w_along", 0.0)) * float(along)
1839 + float(cfg.get("w_along2", 0.0)) * float(along) * float(along)
1840 + float(cfg.get("w_abs_cross", 0.0)) * abs(float(cross))
1841 + float(cfg.get("w_truth_margin", 0.0)) * float(margin)
1842 + float(cfg.get("w_remain", 0.0)) * float(remain_frac)
1843 + float(cfg.get("w_aspect", 0.0)) * float(aspect)
1844-1845 ) threshold = float(cfg.get("threshold", 0.0))
1846 return logit > threshold
1849-1850def _full_od_via_truth_strong( mission: Mission,

1854-1858 truth_field: dict | None, *, cruise_z: float, win_need: float = 0.970, ) -> bool:
1859 """Full-OD truth: locked via beats clearance straight by ``win_need`` (eval only)."""

1882 )
1885def _mission_route_terrain_rise_m(mission: Mission) -> float:
1886 """Home→goal DEM rise for the whole mission (stable; not mid-flight remainder).
1888 Prefer ``home`` over replan ``start`` so Taiwan-class sticky near the goal
1889-1890 still sees the full-route rise (and Shandong stays flat for the strong-flat gate). """
1891-1892 mid = id(mission) cached = _ROUTE_TERRAIN_RISE_CACHE.get(mid)
1893-1895 if cached is not None: return cached elev = getattr(mission, "elevation", None)
1896-1897 if elev is None: _ROUTE_TERRAIN_RISE_CACHE[mid] = 0.0
1898 return 0.0
1899 home = getattr(mission, "home", None)
1900 start = home if home is not None else getattr(mission, "start", None)
1901 goal = getattr(mission, "goal", None)
1902-1903 if start is None or goal is None: _ROUTE_TERRAIN_RISE_CACHE[mid] = 0.0
1904 return 0.0
1905-1909 rise = float( terrain_climb_along_line_m( elev, float(start[0]), float(start[1]),
1910-1913 float(goal[0]), float(goal[1]), samples=8, )
1914 )
1915-1916 if len(_ROUTE_TERRAIN_RISE_CACHE) >= 64: _ROUTE_TERRAIN_RISE_CACHE.clear()
1917 _ROUTE_TERRAIN_RISE_CACHE[mid] = rise
1918 return rise
1921def _mission_has_route_terrain_relief(mission: Mission) -> bool:
1922 step_m = max(float(getattr(mission, "altitude_step_m", 50.0)), 1.0)
1923 return _mission_route_terrain_rise_m(mission) >= step_m
1926def _route_axis_aspect(start, goal) -> float:
1927 """min(|dx|,|dy|)/max(|dx|,|dy|); 0 = pure axis, ~1 = 45° diagonal."""
1928-1929 dx = abs(float(goal[0]) - float(start[0])) dy = abs(float(goal[1]) - float(start[1]))
1930 return min(dx, dy) / max(dx, dy, 1e-6)
1933def _along_track_wind_mps(path, belief_map) -> float:
1934 """Mean along-track wind (+tailwind) on a polyline under belief."""
1935-1937 if path is None or len(path) < 2: return 0.0 pts = path[:-1]
1938-1939 if len(pts) > 24: idx = np.linspace(0, len(pts) - 1, 24).astype(np.int32)
1940 pts = [pts[int(i)] for i in idx]
1941 arr = np.asarray(pts, dtype=np.float64)
1942 samples = _sample_belief_states_batch(belief_map, arr[:, 0], arr[:, 1], arr[:, 2])
1943-1944 total = 0.0 n = 0
1945-1946 for i, pt in enumerate(pts): nxt = path[min(i + 1, len(path) - 1)]
1947-1948 dx = float(nxt[0]) - float(pt[0]) dy = float(nxt[1]) - float(pt[1])
1949 norm = math.hypot(dx, dy)
1950-1951 if norm < 1e-9: continue
1952 total += (float(samples["wind_u"][i]) * dx + float(samples["wind_v"][i]) * dy) / norm
1953 n += 1
1954 return total / max(n, 1)
1957def _od_along_wind_mps(start, goal, belief_map, truth_field=None, cruise_z: float = 1.0) -> float:
1958 """Along-track wind on the full OD at cruise AGL; prefer truth when available."""
1959 a = (float(start[0]), float(start[1]), float(cruise_z))
1960 b = (float(goal[0]), float(goal[1]), float(cruise_z))
1961 if truth_field is not None and "u" in truth_field and "v" in truth_field:
1963-1965 sx, sy = a[0], a[1] gx, gy = b[0], b[1] dx, dy = gx - sx, gy - sy
1966 span = math.hypot(dx, dy)
1967-1970 if span < 1e-9: return 0.0 n = max(8, min(24, int(span) + 1)) total = 0.0
1971-1972 for i in range(n): t = i / max(n - 1, 1)
1973 x, y = sx + dx * t, sy + dy * t
1974 u = float(trilinear_sample(truth_field["u"], x, y, cruise_z))
1975 v = float(trilinear_sample(truth_field["v"], x, y, cruise_z))
1976 total += (u * dx + v * dy) / span
1977 return total / n
1978 return _along_track_wind_mps([a, b], belief_map)
1981-1984def _prior_soft_allowed_for_mission( mission: Mission, *, ambient_wind_mps: float | None,
1985-1986) -> bool: """Mission-level soft prior eligibility (universal; no map names).
1988 Rising DEM rays always allowed. Flat rays only when path ambient is mild
1989-1990 (keeps Fujian soft gains; blocks Shandong strong-flat thrash). """
1991-1992 if _mission_has_route_terrain_relief(mission): return True
1993-1994 if ambient_wind_mps is None: return False
1995 return float(ambient_wind_mps) < CORRIDOR_PRIOR_SOFT_FLAT_MAX_AMB_MPS
1998-1999def _prior_truth_soft_ok( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]],
2000-2001 straight_path: list[tuple[float, float, float]] | None, truth_field: dict | None,
2002-2005 belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission, *, ambient_wind_mps: float | None = None,
2006-2007 has_terrain_relief: bool = False, ) -> bool:
2008 """True when a distilled prior still wins clearly under offline truth wind.
2010 Skips Taiwan-class DEM shortcuts (has_relief) and calm air. Soft-admit is for
2011 belief-blind shear lobes; flat+strong-ambient missions are excluded (Shandong).
2012 Also requires a truth lateral speed edge so arid control maps (Gansu) cannot
2013-2014 soft-commit a mild Joules flicker with no shear lobe. """
2015-2016 if has_terrain_relief: return False
2017 if not _prior_soft_allowed_for_mission(mission, ambient_wind_mps=ambient_wind_mps):
2018 return False
2019 if ambient_wind_mps is not None and float(ambient_wind_mps) < CORRIDOR_HARD_FLOOR_MPS:
2020 return False
2021-2022 if truth_field is None or "u" not in truth_field or "v" not in truth_field: return False
2023-2024 if straight_path is None or len(straight_path) <= 1 or len(path) <= 1: return False
2025 truth_belief = _belief_map_from_truth_field(belief_map, truth_field)
2026-2027 if truth_belief is None: return False
2028-2029 if not _corridor_has_lateral_edge(path, truth_belief, straight_path): return False
2030-2034 return _corridor_truth_beats_straight( path, straight_path, truth_belief, mission,
2035-2036 win_need=CORRIDOR_PRIOR_SOFT_TRUTH_NEED, )
2039-2040def distill_truth_1via( start: tuple[float, float, float],
2041-2043 goal: State3D | tuple[float, float, float], belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission,
2044-2046 truth_field: dict, *, cruise_z: float,
2047 offsets_m: tuple[float, ...] = (100.0, 150.0, 200.0, 250.0, 350.0),
2048-2049 min_truth_save: float = 0.005, min_belief_save: float = 0.005,
2050) -> tuple[float, float] | None:
2051 """Best 1-via XY when *both* truth and live belief beat straight at cruise_z.
2053 Dual-agree: truth-only distill previously helped Liaoning but hurt Shanxi when
2054-2055 belief falsely scored the oracle via. Altitude is never taken from the oracle. """
2056-2057 if "u" not in truth_field or "v" not in truth_field: return None
2058-2059 sx, sy = float(start[0]), float(start[1]) gx, gy = float(goal[0]), float(goal[1])
2060-2061 dx, dy = gx - sx, gy - sy horiz = math.hypot(dx, dy)
2062-2064 if horiz <= 1e-6: return None px, py = -dy / horiz, dx / horiz
2065 cell_m = max(float(mission.step_distance_m), 1e-6)
2066 truth_belief = _belief_map_from_truth_field(belief_map, truth_field)
2067-2069 if truth_belief is None: return None straight = _agl_guide_polyline(
2070 start, goal, truth_belief, mission, via_xy=None, cruise_z=float(cruise_z)
2071 )
2072-2073 if len(straight) <= 1: return None
2074 straight_truth_e = _polyline_model_energy_j(straight, truth_belief, mission)
2075 straight_belief_e = _polyline_model_energy_j(straight, belief_map, mission)
2076-2077 if ( not math.isfinite(straight_truth_e)
2078-2079 or straight_truth_e <= 0.0 or not math.isfinite(straight_belief_e)
2080 or straight_belief_e <= 0.0
2081-2084 ): return None best_via: tuple[float, float] | None = None best_truth_e = straight_truth_e
2085 require_belief = float(min_belief_save) >= 0.0
2086-2088 for sign in (-1.0, 1.0): for off_m in offsets_m: offset = float(off_m) / cell_m
2089 mx = clamp(sx + 0.5 * dx + sign * offset * px, 0.0, belief_map.width - 1)
2090 my = clamp(sy + 0.5 * dy + sign * offset * py, 0.0, belief_map.height - 1)
2091 path = _agl_guide_polyline(
2092 start, goal, truth_belief, mission, via_xy=(mx, my), cruise_z=float(cruise_z)
2093 )
2094-2096 if len(path) <= 1: continue e_t = _polyline_model_energy_j(path, truth_belief, mission)
2097-2098 if not math.isfinite(e_t) or e_t >= best_truth_e: continue
2099-2101 if require_belief: e_b = _polyline_model_energy_j(path, belief_map, mission)
2102-2103 if not math.isfinite(e_b): continue
2104-2105 if e_b > straight_belief_e * (1.0 - float(min_belief_save)): continue
2106-2107 best_truth_e = e_t best_via = (float(mx), float(my))
2108-2109 if best_via is None: return None
2110-2111 if best_truth_e > straight_truth_e * (1.0 - float(min_truth_save)): return None
2112 return best_via
2115-2118def _corridor_via_xy( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]], sx: float, sy: float,
2119-2121 gx: float, gy: float, ) -> tuple[float, float] | None:
2122 """Waypoint with largest cross-track distance — proxy for the corridor via."""
2123-2126 if len(path) < 3: return None dx, dy = gx - sx, gy - sy span = math.hypot(dx, dy)
2127-2130 if span <= 1e-9: return None best_xy: tuple[float, float] | None = None best_d = -1.0
2131-2132 for p in path[1:-1]: cross = abs((float(p[0]) - sx) * dy - (float(p[1]) - sy) * dx) / span
2133-2134 if cross > best_d: best_d = cross
2135 best_xy = (float(p[0]), float(p[1]))
2136 return best_xy
2139-2142def _corridor_terrain_relief_m( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]], mission: Mission, *,
2143-2145 sx: float | None = None, sy: float | None = None, gx: float | None = None,
2146-2147 gy: float | None = None, via_xy: tuple[float, float] | None = None,
2148) -> tuple[float, float]:
2149 """Return (relief_m, direct_rise_m). Positive relief = corridor climbs less DEM."""
2150 elev = getattr(mission, "elevation", None)
2151-2152 if elev is None or len(path) < 2: return 0.0, 0.0
2153-2154 if sx is None or sy is None: sx, sy = float(path[0][0]), float(path[0][1])
2155-2156 if gx is None or gy is None: gx, gy = float(path[-1][0]), float(path[-1][1])
2157 direct_rise = terrain_climb_along_line_m(elev, sx, sy, gx, gy, samples=8)
2158 via = via_xy if via_xy is not None else _corridor_via_xy(path, sx, sy, gx, gy)
2159-2160 if via is None: return 0.0, float(direct_rise)
2161 via_rise = terrain_climb_along_line_m(elev, sx, sy, via[0], via[1], samples=4)
2162 via_rise += terrain_climb_along_line_m(elev, via[0], via[1], gx, gy, samples=4)
2163 return float(direct_rise - via_rise), float(direct_rise)

2249-2250def _corridor_has_light_vertical_edge( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]],

2263-2264 if float(w_mean) < CORRIDOR_LIGHT_W_MIN_MPS: return False
2265-2266 if float(w_mean) < float(s_w) + CORRIDOR_LIGHT_W_ADVANTAGE_MPS: return False

2297-2298def _corridor_has_light_air_edge( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]],
