2324-2327def _corridor_wind_usable( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]], belief_map: BeliefMap, *,
2328 straight_path: list[tuple[float, float, float]] | None = None,
2329-2330 allow_shear_unlock: bool = False, allow_terrain_relief: bool = False,
2331-2332 allow_light_vertical: bool = False, ) -> bool:
2333 """Horizontal-wind floor — blocks calm fields that invent tiny Joules wins.
2335 Between HARD_FLOOR and MIN, corridors are allowed only with a proven lateral
2336 edge vs the straight ray (weak-but-sheared routes; still blocks uniform calm).
2337-2338 Below HARD_FLOOR, terrain-relief or light-air (w / |speed|) unlocks may pass. """
2339-2340 if allow_terrain_relief or allow_light_vertical: return True
2341 speed, _w_mean, _uplift_p = _path_wind_utilization_stats(path, belief_map)
2342-2343 if speed < CORRIDOR_HARD_FLOOR_MPS: return False
2344-2345 if speed >= CORRIDOR_MIN_WIND_MPS: return True
2346-2347 if not allow_shear_unlock or straight_path is None: return False
2348 return _corridor_has_lateral_edge(path, belief_map, straight_path)
2351-2354def _corridor_ambient_ok( amb_speed: float, *, has_edge: bool,
2355-2356 has_terrain_relief: bool = False, has_light_vertical: bool = False,
2357-2358 calm_prior_ok: bool = False, ) -> bool:
2359 """Straight-ray ambient gate with shear / terrain / light-vertical unlocks."""
2360-2361 if has_light_vertical: return amb_speed >= CORRIDOR_LIGHT_FLOOR_MPS
2362-2367 if has_terrain_relief: if ( float(amb_speed) >= CORRIDOR_HARD_FLOOR_MPS
2368-2369 or has_edge or calm_prior_ok
2370-2371 ): return True
2372 return False
2373-2374 if amb_speed < CORRIDOR_HARD_FLOOR_MPS: return False
2375-2376 if amb_speed >= CORRIDOR_MIN_WIND_MPS: return True
2377 return bool(has_edge)
2380-2381def _corridor_has_lateral_edge( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]],
2382-2384 belief_map: BeliefMap, straight_path: list[tuple[float, float, float]] | None, ) -> bool:
2385 """True when the corridor shows a real wind edge vs the straight ray.
2387 In marginal ambient wind only horizontal-speed edges count — belief w/uplift
2388 noise otherwise invents "shear" on calm maps. Corridor speed uses p75 so a
2389-2390 localized shear lobe is not diluted by calm segments. """
2391-2392 if straight_path is None or len(straight_path) <= 1: return True
2394 speed, w_mean, uplift_p = _path_wind_utilization_stats(path, belief_map, speed_q=0.75)
2395-2396 s_speed, s_w, s_up = _path_wind_utilization_stats(straight_path, belief_map) need_w = max(
2397-2399 CORRIDOR_W_ADVANTAGE_MPS, CORRIDOR_W_ADVANTAGE_FRAC * min(max(abs(s_w), 0.05), 0.5), )
2400-2401 need_up = max( CORRIDOR_UPLIFT_ADVANTAGE,
2402-2403 CORRIDOR_UPLIFT_ADVANTAGE_FRAC * min(max(s_up, 0.05), 0.5), )
2404 if s_speed < CORRIDOR_MIN_WIND_MPS:
2408 return speed >= s_speed + CORRIDOR_SHEAR_SPEED_ADVANTAGE_MPS
2410-2411 need_speed = max( CORRIDOR_WIND_ADVANTAGE_MPS,
2412-2413 CORRIDOR_WIND_ADVANTAGE_FRAC * min(max(s_speed, 0.0), 2.0), )
2414 speed_edge = speed >= s_speed + need_speed
2415 vertical_edge = w_mean >= s_w + need_w or uplift_p >= s_up + need_up
2416 if s_speed < CORRIDOR_MARGINAL_WIND_MPS:
2418 mild_speed = speed >= s_speed + min(0.22, 0.55 * need_speed)
2419 return speed_edge or (mild_speed and vertical_edge)
2420 return speed_edge or vertical_edge
2423-2425def _corridor_wind_ok( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]], belief_map: BeliefMap,
2426-2427 straight_path: list[tuple[float, float, float]] | None = None, *,
2428-2429 require_lateral_edge: bool = False, ) -> bool:
2430 """Usable wind required; lateral edge optional (enforced via stricter Joules when absent)."""
2431-2432 if not _corridor_wind_usable(path, belief_map): return False
2433-2434 if not require_lateral_edge: return True
2435 return _corridor_has_lateral_edge(path, belief_map, straight_path)
2438-2439def _corridor_energy_win_need( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]],
2440-2442 straight_path: list[tuple[float, float, float]] | None, corridor_margin: float, *,
2443-2444 ambient_wind_mps: float | None = None, has_lateral_edge: bool = False,
2445-2446 has_terrain_relief: bool = False, has_light_vertical: bool = False,
2447-2449 route_aspect: float | None = None, along_wind_mps: float | None = None, ) -> float:
2450 """Stricter Joules gate when the corridor adds XY length or ambient wind is calm."""
2451 base = min(float(corridor_margin), CORRIDOR_WIN_NEED)
2452 if has_terrain_relief and (
2453 ambient_wind_mps is None or ambient_wind_mps < CORRIDOR_HARD_FLOOR_MPS
2454-2456 ): base = min(base, CORRIDOR_TERRAIN_WIN_NEED)
2457 elif has_light_vertical and (
2458 ambient_wind_mps is None or ambient_wind_mps < CORRIDOR_HARD_FLOOR_MPS
2459-2460 ): base = min(base, CORRIDOR_LIGHT_WIN_NEED)
2461-2462 elif ambient_wind_mps is not None and ambient_wind_mps < CORRIDOR_MARGINAL_WIND_MPS: if (
2463-2464 has_lateral_edge and ambient_wind_mps >= CORRIDOR_HARD_FLOOR_MPS
2465 and ambient_wind_mps < CORRIDOR_MIN_WIND_MPS
2466-2468 ): base = min(base, CORRIDOR_SHEAR_WIN_NEED)
2469-2470 else: base = min(base, CORRIDOR_CALM_WIN_NEED)
2472 if route_aspect is not None and float(route_aspect) <= ROUTE_AXIS_ASPECT_MAX:
2473 if along_wind_mps is not None and float(along_wind_mps) < 0.0:
2474 base = min(base, CORRIDOR_AXIS_HEADWIND_WIN_NEED)
2475-2476 else: base = min(base, CORRIDOR_AXIS_WIN_NEED)
2477-2478 if straight_path is None or len(straight_path) <= 1 or len(path) <= 1: return base
2479 s_len = _path_xy_length(straight_path)
2480-2482 if s_len <= 1e-6: return base detour_frac = max(0.0, _path_xy_length(path) / s_len - 1.0)
2483-2484 beta = ( CORRIDOR_LIGHT_DETOUR_WIN_BETA
2485 if has_light_vertical and not has_terrain_relief and not has_lateral_edge
2486 else CORRIDOR_DETOUR_WIN_BETA
2487 )
2488 return max(0.90, base - beta * detour_frac)
2491-2492def _polyline_risk_adjusted_energy_j( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]],
2493-2496 belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission, uncertainty_gain: float = 0.08, ) -> float:
2497 """Belief energy with an uncertainty surcharge (pessimize untrusted wind wins)."""
2498 base = _polyline_model_energy_j(path, belief_map, mission)
2499-2501 if not math.isfinite(base): return base unc = _path_mean_uncertainty(path, belief_map)
2502 return base * (1.0 + uncertainty_gain * max(0.0, unc))
2505-2506def _path_xy_length(path: list[tuple[float, float, float]]) -> float: total = 0.0
2507-2508 for a, b in zip(path, path[1:]): total += math.hypot(b[0] - a[0], b[1] - a[1])
2509 return total
2512-2514def _completed_plan_energy_j( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]], goal: State3D,
2515-2517 belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission, ) -> float:
2518 """Polyline energy of path, finishing to goal with a constant-AGL guide if needed."""
2519 return _completed_plans_energy_j([path], goal, belief_map, mission)[0]
2522-2524def _completed_plans_energy_j( paths: list[list[tuple[float, float, float]]], goal: State3D,
2525-2527 belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission, ) -> list[float]:
2528 """Batch version of _completed_plan_energy_j."""
2529-2531 if not paths: return [] clearance = float(getattr(mission, "clearance_agl_level", 1.0))
2532 cruise_z = getattr(mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None)
2533 cruise = float(cruise_z) if cruise_z is not None else clearance
2534 extended: list[list[tuple[float, float, float]]] = []
2535-2537 for path in paths: if not path: extended.append([])
2538 continue
2539-2540 if _continuous_goal_reached(path[-1], goal): extended.append(path)
2541 continue
2542-2545 finish = _agl_guide_polyline( _continuous_state_tuple(path[-1]), goal, belief_map,
2546-2549 mission, via_xy=None, cruise_z=cruise, )
2550-2551 if len(finish) > 1: extended.append(list(path) + list(finish[1:]))
2552-2553 else: extended.append(path)
2554 return _polylines_model_energy_j(extended, belief_map, mission)
2557-2559def _agl_guide_polyline( start: tuple[float, float, float], goal: State3D,
2560-2561 belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission,
2562 via_xy: tuple[float, float] | list[tuple[float, float]] | tuple[tuple[float, float], ...] | None = None,
2563-2564 cruise_z: float | None = None, ) -> list[tuple[float, float, float]]:
2565 """Constant-AGL guide packed like eval `straight_agl_baseline`.
2567 Coarse waypoint packing biases band ranking (elevated cruise looks worse than
2568 it is). Use the same climb/hold/descend discretization as the energy metric.
2569-2570 `via_xy` may be one point or an ordered sequence (multi-via corridor). """
2571 min_level, max_level = _search_level_bounds(belief_map, mission)
2572-2573 if cruise_z is None: cruise_z = float(getattr(mission, "clearance_agl_level", 1.0))
2574 cruise_z = clamp(float(cruise_z), float(min_level), float(max_level))
2575 sx, sy, sz = float(start[0]), float(start[1]), float(start[2])
2576 gx, gy, gz = float(goal[0]), float(goal[1]), float(goal[2])
2577-2578 width = max(belief_map.width - 1, 0) height = max(belief_map.height - 1, 0)
2580 def _clamp_path(pts: list[tuple[float, float, float]]) -> list[tuple[float, float, float]]:
2581 out: list[tuple[float, float, float]] = []
2582-2584 for x, y, z in pts: p = ( clamp(float(x), 0.0, float(width)),
2585 clamp(float(y), 0.0, float(height)),
2586-2587 clamp(float(z), float(min_level), float(max_level)), )
2588-2589 if not out: out.append(p)
2590 continue
2591 prev = out[-1]
2592 if math.hypot(p[0] - prev[0], p[1] - prev[1]) > 0.02 or abs(p[2] - prev[2]) > 0.02:
2593 out.append(p)
2594 return out if out else [(sx, sy, sz)]
2596 vias: list[tuple[float, float]] = []
2597 if via_xy is not None:
2598 if isinstance(via_xy, tuple) and len(via_xy) == 2 and not isinstance(via_xy[0], (tuple, list)):
2599 vias = [(float(via_xy[0]), float(via_xy[1]))]
2600-2601 else: vias = [(float(p[0]), float(p[1])) for p in via_xy] # type: ignore[arg-type]
2603 if not vias or math.hypot(gx - sx, gy - sy) < 5.0:
2604 return _clamp_path(straight_agl_guide_polyline((sx, sy, sz), (gx, gy, gz), agl_cruise=cruise_z))
2607 anchors: list[tuple[float, float, float]] = [(sx, sy, sz)]
2608-2611 for vx, vy in vias: anchors.append( ( clamp(float(vx), 0.0, float(width)),
2612-2614 clamp(float(vy), 0.0, float(height)), cruise_z, )
2615 )
2616-2617 anchors.append((gx, gy, gz)) merged: list[tuple[float, float, float]] = []
2618 for a, b in zip(anchors, anchors[1:]):
2619 leg = straight_agl_guide_polyline(a, b, agl_cruise=cruise_z)
2620-2621 if not merged: merged = list(leg)
2622-2623 else: merged.extend(list(leg[1:] if leg else []))
2624 return _clamp_path(merged)
2627def _cruise_band_step(mission: Mission) -> float:
2629 alt = max(float(getattr(mission, "altitude_step_m", 50.0) or 50.0), 1e-6)
2630 configured = float(getattr(mission, "cruise_band_step", 0.02) or 0.02)
2631 return max(configured, 0.5 / alt)
2634def _cruise_band_span(mission: Mission) -> float:
2635 return float(getattr(mission, "cruise_band_span", 2.0) or 2.0)
2638-2644def _local_agl_bands( center: float, *, lo: float, hi: float, step: float, radius: float,
2645-2646) -> list[float]: """Fine AGL samples in [center±radius] clipped to [lo, hi]."""
2647-2648 step = max(float(step), 0.01) a = max(float(lo), float(center) - float(radius))
2649 b = min(float(hi), float(center) + float(radius))
2650-2653 if b < a - 1e-12: return [] k0 = int(math.ceil((a - lo) / step - 1e-12))
2654 k1 = int(math.floor((b - lo) / step + 1e-12))
2655 return [round(lo + k * step, 10) for k in range(k0, k1 + 1)]
2658def _cruise_band_candidates(belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission) -> list[float]:
2659 """Cruise AGL candidates for planning.
2661 For fine steps (<0.2): coarse 0.25 grid over the span, plus a fine neighborhood
2662 around sticky/clearance (full fine refinement happens after coarse scoring).
2663-2664 Eval baselines still use the full fine grid via cruise_band_step. """
2665 clearance = float(getattr(mission, "clearance_agl_level", 1.0))
2666 min_level, max_level = _search_level_bounds(belief_map, mission)
2667-2668 fine = _cruise_band_step(mission) span = _cruise_band_span(mission)
2669 sticky = getattr(mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None)
2670-2671 lo = max(clearance, float(min_level)) hi = min(clearance + span, float(max_level))
2672-2673 if fine >= 0.2 - 1e-12: return cruise_agl_band_grid(
2674 clearance, float(min_level), float(max_level), step=fine, span_levels=span, sticky=sticky
2675 )
2676 coarse = 0.25
2679-2680 if sticky is not None and fine < 0.2 - 1e-12: bands = [clearance, float(sticky)]
2681 bands.extend(_local_agl_bands(float(sticky), lo=lo, hi=hi, step=fine, radius=0.35))
2682 bands.extend(_local_agl_bands(clearance, lo=lo, hi=hi, step=fine, radius=0.15))
2683-2684 bands.extend( cruise_agl_band_grid(
2685 clearance, float(min_level), float(max_level), step=coarse, span_levels=span, sticky=sticky
2686 )
2687-2688 ) uniq = sorted(set(round(float(z), 10) for z in bands if lo - 1e-12 <= z <= hi + 1e-12))
2689 return [clamp(z, float(min_level), float(max_level)) for z in uniq]
2690 bands = cruise_agl_band_grid(
2691 clearance, float(min_level), float(max_level), step=coarse, span_levels=span, sticky=sticky
2692-2694 ) bands.extend(_local_agl_bands(clearance, lo=lo, hi=hi, step=fine, radius=coarse))
2695 if sticky is not None:
2696 bands.extend(_local_agl_bands(float(sticky), lo=lo, hi=hi, step=fine, radius=coarse))
2697 uniq = sorted(set(round(float(z), 10) for z in bands))
2698 return [clamp(z, float(min_level), float(max_level)) for z in uniq]
2701def _straight_guide_label(cruise_z: float, clearance: float, step: float = 0.025) -> str:
2702 eps = max(1e-6, 0.51 * float(step))
2703-2704 if abs(cruise_z - clearance) < eps: return "guide_straight_agl"
2705 return f"guide_straight_agl_{format_agl_band_token(cruise_z)}"
2708def _cruise_z_from_guide_label(label: str, clearance: float, mission: Mission | None = None) -> float:
2709-2711 if label.startswith("guide_straight_agl_"): try: return float(label.rsplit("_", 1)[-1])
2712-2713 except ValueError: pass
2714-2715 if label.startswith("guide_straight_agl"): return clearance
2716 preferred = getattr(mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None) if mission is not None else None
2717-2718 if preferred is not None: return float(preferred)
2719 return clearance
2722-2725def _commit_preferred_cruise( mission: Mission, cruise_z: float, clearance: float,
2726-2727 band_energies: dict[float, float] | None = None, ) -> float:
2728 """Update preferred cruise AGL.
2730 Follows the newly selected band when band_energies show a clear improvement
2731-2732 either up or down. Only ratchet upward when scores are nearly tied. """
2733 step = _cruise_band_step(mission)
2734 max_level = float(getattr(mission, "max_altitude_level", 4))
2735-2736 min_level = float(getattr(mission, "min_altitude_level", 0)) z = snap_cruise_agl(
2737-2741 float(cruise_z), clearance=clearance, min_level=min_level, max_level=max_level, step=step,
2742-2743 ) prev = getattr(mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None)
2744-2745 if prev is not None and band_energies: prev = float(prev)
2747 def _lookup(level: float) -> float:
2748 key = min(band_energies.keys(), key=lambda k: abs(k - level))
2749 return band_energies[key]
2751-2752 prev_e = _lookup(prev) new_e = _lookup(z)
2753 argmin_z = float(min(band_energies.keys(), key=lambda k: band_energies[k]))
2754-2756 argmin_e = band_energies[argmin_z] clr = float(clearance) clr_e = _lookup(clr)
2759 already_earned = bool(getattr(mission, "cruise_climb_earned", False))
2760-2762 high_band = z > clr + 1.2 oneshot = already_earned and new_e <= clr_e * 0.85
2763 max_step = 0.70 if new_e <= clr_e * 0.90 else 0.35
2764 if already_earned and new_e <= clr_e * 0.90:
2765 max_step = max(max_step, 0.85 if high_band else 1.25)
2767 commit_dump_need = 0.92 if (already_earned and prev > clr + 1.0) else 0.98
2768-2769 if oneshot: pass # keep selector z
2770-2771 elif z + 1e-9 < prev: if new_e > prev_e * commit_dump_need and not (
2772 prev > clr + 0.5 * step and prev_e > clr_e * 1.02
2773-2774 ): z = prev
2775-2776 elif z < prev - max_step - 1e-9: z = prev - max_step
2777-2779 elif z > prev + 1e-9: sticky_need = 0.97 if already_earned else 0.95
2780 earned = new_e <= clr_e * 0.95 and new_e <= prev_e * sticky_need
2781-2782 if not earned: z = prev
2783-2785 else: if abs(new_e - prev_e) <= prev_e * 0.015: z = prev
2786 if not oneshot:
2787 if z > argmin_z + 2.0 * step + 1e-9 and _lookup(z) > argmin_e * 1.05 and z >= prev - 1e-9:
2788 sticky_need = 0.97 if already_earned else 0.95
2789 if argmin_z + 1e-9 >= prev and argmin_e <= prev_e * sticky_need and argmin_e <= clr_e * 0.95:
2790 z = min(argmin_z, prev + max_step)
2791-2792 if z > prev + max_step + 1e-9: z = prev + max_step
2793-2794 if z + 1e-9 < prev - max_step: z = prev - max_step
2795-2800 elif prev is not None: pass z = max(z, float(clearance)) z = snap_cruise_agl( z,
2801-2805 clearance=clearance, min_level=min_level, max_level=max_level, step=step, )
2806 mission.preferred_cruise_agl = z
2808-2809 if z > float(clearance) + 0.5 * step: if band_energies:
2810 clr_key = min(band_energies.keys(), key=lambda k: abs(k - float(clearance)))
2811 z_key = min(band_energies.keys(), key=lambda k: abs(k - z))
2812 if band_energies[z_key] <= band_energies[clr_key] * 0.95:
2813 mission.cruise_climb_earned = True
2814-2815 else: mission.cruise_climb_earned = True
2816-2817 elif z <= float(clearance) + 1e-9: mission.cruise_climb_earned = False
2818 return z
2822-2824def _band_selection_scores( start: tuple[float, float, float], goal: State3D,
2825-2828 belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission, bands: list[float], ) -> dict[float, float]:
2829 """Cruise-fair band scores: wind along-route as if already at cruise + climb fee.
2831 Comparing full paths from z=0 buries altitude-wind differences under climb cost.
2832 Scoring cruise-from-altitude + additive climb is terrain-agnostic and makes
2833-2835 higher-band wind visible to the selector. """ sz = float(start[2])
2836 climb_cost = float(getattr(mission, "climb_cost_per_level_j", 180.0))
2837-2839 if not bands: return {} paths: list[list[tuple[float, float, float]]] = []
2840 zs: list[float] = []
2841-2842 for raw_z in bands: z = float(raw_z)
2843 alt_start = (float(start[0]), float(start[1]), z)
2844 path = _agl_guide_polyline(alt_start, goal, belief_map, mission, via_xy=None, cruise_z=z)
2845-2846 paths.append(path) zs.append(z)
2847 energies = _polylines_model_energy_j(paths, belief_map, mission)
2848 scores: dict[float, float] = {}
2849-2851 for z, path, cruise_e in zip(zs, paths, energies): if len(path) <= 1: scores[z] = math.inf
2852-2853 else: scores[z] = float(cruise_e) + climb_cost * max(0.0, z - sz)
2854 return scores
2857-2859def _select_preferred_cruise_band( band_scores: dict[float, float], sticky: float | None,
2860-2861 clearance: float, remaining_horiz: float = 0.0,
2862-2864 tiebreak_scores: dict[float, float] | None = None, band_step: float = 0.5, *,
2865-2867 terrain_rise_m: float = 0.0, altitude_step_m: float = 50.0, climb_earned: bool = False,
2868-2869 ambient_wind_mps: float | None = None, ) -> float | None:
2870 """Pick cruise AGL from full-path band scores with evidence-gated changes.
2872 Universal rules (no map-specific thresholds):
2873 - Flat routes: climb only if winner beats clearance by ≥5% (belief aloft is noisy).
2874 - Significant along-route terrain rise: relax to ≥3% (ridge-following needs height);
2875 micro-layers (≤clearance+0.15) need only ≥1.5%.
2876 - Calm + flat: require ≥10% vs clearance before any climb (noisy aloft).
2877 - Calm + rising DEM: ≥3% unlocks mid/high bands (aloft uplift can be real; Hainan-class).
2878 - Dump toward clearance if sticky is ≥2% worse than clearance.
2879-2880 - Unearned sticky (never cleared the climb bar) eases down even if slightly cheaper. """
2881 del tiebreak_scores # reserved for callers; selection uses band_scores only
2882-2884 if not band_scores: return None step = max(float(band_step), 0.01)
2885 dump_need = 0.98 # ≥2% savings to dump toward a better lower band (default / approach)
2886 best_z = float(min(band_scores.keys(), key=lambda z: band_scores[z]))
2887 best_e = band_scores[best_z]
2888 clearance_key = float(min(band_scores.keys(), key=lambda z: abs(float(z) - float(clearance))))
2889 clearance_e = band_scores[clearance_key]
2890 terrain_relief = terrain_rise_m >= max(float(altitude_step_m), 1.0)
2892 calm_air = ambient_wind_mps is not None and float(ambient_wind_mps) < CORRIDOR_MIN_WIND_MPS
2894 strong_air = ambient_wind_mps is None or float(ambient_wind_mps) >= CORRIDOR_MARGINAL_WIND_MPS
2897 cruise_dump_need = 0.92 if (climb_earned and strong_air) else dump_need
2899 calm_hold_need = 0.97 if terrain_relief else 0.90
2901 def _climb_need_for(target_z: float, from_z: float | None) -> float:
2902 """Evidence bar vs clearance energy (lower multiplier ⇒ easier climb).
2904 Micro-layers on rising DEM: ≥1.5%. Calm+flat: ≥10%. Calm+relief: ≥3%.
2905-2906 High bands otherwise need 8–10% — stops Taiwan-class z→3 cascades. """
2908-2909 if target_z <= clearance_key + 0.15 + 1e-9: return 0.985 if terrain_relief else 0.98
2910 if calm_air and target_z > clearance_key + 0.5 * step:
2911 return 0.97 if terrain_relief else 0.90
2912 base = from_z if from_z is not None else clearance_key
2913 if terrain_relief and base <= clearance_key + 0.5 * step and target_z <= clearance_key + 1.0 + 1e-9:
2914 return 0.97
2915-2916 if target_z > clearance_key + 1.5 + 1e-9: return 0.90 # ≥10% vs clearance
2917-2918 if target_z > clearance_key + 1.0 + 1e-9: return 0.92 # ≥8% vs clearance
2919 return 0.95
2921 def _earned_climb(candidate_e: float, target_z: float, from_z: float | None = None) -> bool:
2922 return candidate_e <= clearance_e * _climb_need_for(target_z, from_z)
2924-2929 if sticky is None or remaining_horiz <= 6.0: if remaining_horiz <= 6.0: if (
2930-2932 sticky is not None and climb_earned and float(sticky) > clearance_key + 1.0 + 1e-9
2933 ):
2934 sticky_key = float(min(band_scores.keys(), key=lambda z: abs(float(z) - float(sticky))))
2935 sticky_e = band_scores[sticky_key]
2936 if sticky_e <= clearance_e * 1.02:
2937 if best_z + 1e-9 < sticky_key and best_e <= sticky_e * dump_need:
2938 return float(max(best_z, sticky_key - 0.35))
2939 return sticky_key
2940-2941 if best_z + 1e-9 < clearance_key and best_e <= clearance_e * 0.98: return best_z
2942 if remaining_horiz <= 3.5:
2943 return float(min(band_scores.keys(), key=lambda z: abs(float(z) - float(clearance_key))))
2944 if best_z > clearance_key + 0.5 * step and _earned_climb(best_e, best_z, clearance_key):
2947 cold_unlock = 0.97 if (calm_air and terrain_relief) else 0.85
2948 if best_z > clearance_key + 1.2 + 1e-9 and best_e > clearance_e * cold_unlock:
2949-2951 ceiling = clearance_key + 1.2 mid = { float(z): float(e)
2952 for z, e in band_scores.items()
2953 if float(z) <= ceiling + 1e-9
2954 }
2955-2956 if mid: mid_best = float(min(mid.keys(), key=lambda z: mid[z]))
2957 if mid[mid_best] <= clearance_e * _climb_need_for(mid_best, clearance_key):
2958 return mid_best
2959 return clearance_key
2960 return best_z
2961 return clearance_key
2963 sticky_key = float(min(band_scores.keys(), key=lambda z: abs(float(z) - float(sticky))))
2964 sticky_e = band_scores[sticky_key]
2967 if sticky_key > clearance_key + 0.5 * step and sticky_e > clearance_e * 1.02:
2968 return float(max(clearance_key, sticky_key - 0.35))
2972 if remaining_horiz <= 10.0:
2973 if best_z + 1e-9 < sticky_key and best_e <= sticky_e * dump_need:
2974 return float(min(sticky_key, max(best_z, sticky_key - 0.35)))
2975-2976 if climb_earned and sticky_key > clearance_key + 1.0 + 1e-9: return sticky_key
2977 if sticky_key > clearance_key + 0.5 * step:
2978 return float(max(clearance_key, sticky_key - 0.35))
2979 return sticky_key
2982 if remaining_horiz <= 16.0:
2983 if best_z + 1e-9 < sticky_key and best_e <= sticky_e * cruise_dump_need:
2984 return float(max(best_z, sticky_key - 0.35))
2985 need = _climb_need_for(best_z, sticky_key)
2986 if best_z >= sticky_key + 0.5 * step and _earned_climb(best_e, best_z, sticky_key) and best_e <= sticky_e * need:
2987 return float(min(best_z, sticky_key + 0.35))
2988 return sticky_key
2995 ceiling = clearance_key + 1.2
2996 if terrain_relief and (climb_earned and strong_air or calm_air):
2997 ceiling = clearance_key + 1.8
2998-2999 if strong_air and (climb_earned or terrain_relief): ceiling_unlock = 0.87
3000-3001 elif strong_air: ceiling_unlock = 0.85
3002-3003 elif terrain_relief: ceiling_unlock = 0.97
3004-3007 else: ceiling_unlock = 0.80 eligible = { float(z): float(e)
3008 for z, e in band_scores.items()
3009 if float(z) <= ceiling + 1e-9 or float(e) <= clearance_e * ceiling_unlock
3010 }
3012-3013 if climb_earned and strong_air: eligible[sticky_key] = sticky_e
3014-3015 if not eligible: eligible = {sticky_key: sticky_e}
3016 climb_best_z = float(min(eligible.keys(), key=lambda z: eligible[z]))
3017-3019 climb_best_e = eligible[climb_best_z] need = _climb_need_for(climb_best_z, sticky_key)
3020 high_target = climb_best_z > clearance_key + 1.0 + 1e-9
3021 sticky_need = need if high_target else (0.97 if climb_earned else need)
3022-3023 if ( climb_best_z >= sticky_key + 0.5 * step
3024-3025 and climb_best_e <= clearance_e * need and climb_best_e <= sticky_e * sticky_need
3026-3027 ): high_band = climb_best_z > clearance_key + 1.2
3028 jump = 0.70 if climb_best_e <= clearance_e * 0.90 else 0.35
3029 if climb_earned and (not high_target) and climb_best_e <= sticky_e * 0.97 and climb_best_e > clearance_e * 0.90:
3030 jump = max(jump, 0.50)
3031-3033 if climb_earned and climb_best_e <= clearance_e * 0.90: if high_band and not strong_air:
3034 jump = max(jump, 0.50)
3035-3036 else: jump = max(jump, 0.85 if high_band else 1.35)
3038-3039 if ( climb_earned
3040-3042 and strong_air and climb_best_e <= clearance_e * 0.85 and remaining_horiz > 22.0
3043-3044 ): return float(climb_best_z)
3045 return float(min(climb_best_z, sticky_key + jump))
3046 if climb_best_z + 1e-9 < sticky_key - 0.5 * step and climb_best_e <= sticky_e * cruise_dump_need:
3047 jump = 0.70 if climb_best_e <= sticky_e * 0.92 else 0.35
3048 return float(max(climb_best_z, sticky_key - jump))
3050-3051 if ( sticky_key > clearance_key + 0.5 * step
3052-3053 and not climb_earned and not _earned_climb(sticky_e, sticky_key, clearance_key)
3054-3055 ): return float(max(clearance_key, sticky_key - 0.35))
3057 if calm_air and sticky_key > clearance_key + 0.5 * step and sticky_e > clearance_e * calm_hold_need:
3058 return float(max(clearance_key, sticky_key - 0.35))
3061 if sticky_key > ceiling + 1e-9 and sticky_e > clearance_e * ceiling_unlock:
3062-3063 if climb_earned and strong_air and sticky_e <= clearance_e * 0.95: return sticky_key
3064 step_down = 0.70 if not strong_air else 0.50
3065 return float(max(ceiling, sticky_key - step_down))
3069 layer_cap = clearance_key + 1.67
3070-3071 if ( climb_earned
3072-3073 and strong_air and sticky_key > clearance_key + 1.0 + 1e-9
3074-3075 and sticky_key + 1e-9 < layer_cap and remaining_horiz > 18.0
3076-3077 ): step_up = max(step, 0.10)
3078-3079 upper = { float(z): float(e)
3080 for z, e in eligible.items()
3081 if sticky_key + 0.5 * step <= float(z) <= min(sticky_key + step_up, layer_cap) + 1e-9
3082 }
3083-3084 if upper: up_z = float(min(upper.keys(), key=lambda z: upper[z]))
3086-3087 if upper[up_z] <= sticky_e * 0.997: return float(up_z)
3088 return sticky_key
3091-3093def _energy_guide_paths( start: tuple[float, float, float], goal: State3D,
3094-3096 belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission, truth_field: dict | None = None,
3097) -> list[tuple[str, list[tuple[float, float, float]]]]:

3580-3582 def _prior_gen_soft_rescue( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]], belief_e: float,
3583-3587 bar: float, *, band_speed: float, has_relief: bool, ) -> bool:
3588 """Admit truth-clear priors even when uninformed belief Joules look worse.
3590 Jilin-class: empty belief scores the prior ~5% worse than straight while
3591 truth saves ~3% — a fixed belief slack cannot bridge that. Gate on truth
3592-3593 only (still skips DEM-relief / calm via `_prior_truth_soft_ok`). """

3666-3667 scored.append((0.9 * raw + 0.1 * raw, raw, label, path)) continue

3678-3679 def _band_token(label: str) -> str: return label.split("_z")[-1] if "_z" in label else ""

3697 locked = getattr(mission, "guide_via", None)

3837-3839def _run_anytime_continuous_mpc( belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission,
3840-3842 risk_weight: float, safety_weight: float, anytime_rounds: int,
3843-3845 heuristic_weight_start: float, heuristic_weight_end: float, search_node_budget: int,
3846-3849 horizon_steps: int, beam_width: int, branch_width: int, discount_factor: float,
3850-3851 terminal_progress_weight: float, return_cost_map: dict[State3D, float] | None,
3852-3853 candidate_mode: str, ) -> dict:
3854 weights = _weight_schedule(anytime_rounds, heuristic_weight_start, heuristic_weight_end)
3855-3856 start = _continuous_state_tuple(mission.start) goal = normalize_state(mission.goal)
3857-3858 best_path: list[tuple[float, float, float]] = [start] best_value = -math.inf
3859 best_frontier_point = normalize_state(start)
3860 best_frontier_score = heuristic(best_frontier_point, goal, mission)
3861 candidates: list[dict] = []
3862 for round_index, weight in enumerate(weights):
3863 round_beam_width = max(6, int(beam_width * (0.65 + 0.35 * ((round_index + 1) / max(len(weights), 1)))))
3864 round_branch_width = max(4, int(branch_width * (0.75 + 0.25 * ((round_index + 1) / max(len(weights), 1)))))
3865 round_horizon = max(2, min(horizon_steps + round_index, mission.max_steps))
3866 remaining_budget = max(200, search_node_budget // max(len(weights) - round_index, 1))
3867-3869 result = _beam_search_continuous_mpc( belief_map=belief_map, mission=mission,
3870-3872 risk_weight=risk_weight, safety_weight=safety_weight, node_budget=remaining_budget,
3873-3874 horizon_steps=round_horizon, beam_width=round_beam_width,
3875-3876 branch_width=round_branch_width, discount_factor=discount_factor,
3877 terminal_progress_weight=terminal_progress_weight * weight,
3878-3879 return_cost_map=return_cost_map, )
3880 chosen_path = result["path"] or [_continuous_state_tuple(mission.start)]
3881 chosen_cost = _path_cost(chosen_path, belief_map, mission, risk_weight, safety_weight)
3882-3885 candidates.append( { "round": round_index + 1, "mode": candidate_mode,
3886-3887 "heuristic_weight": weight, "path": chosen_path[: mission.max_steps + 1],
3888 "path_cost": chosen_cost,
3889 "path_cost_breakdown": _path_cost_breakdown(chosen_path, belief_map, mission, risk_weight, safety_weight),
3890 "goal_reached": bool(chosen_path and _continuous_goal_reached(chosen_path[-1], goal)),
3891-3893 "horizon_steps": round_horizon, "beam_width": round_beam_width, }
3894 )
3895-3896 if result["value"] > best_value: best_value = result["value"]
3897 best_path = chosen_path
3898 best_frontier_point = normalize_state(chosen_path[-1]) if chosen_path else normalize_state(start)
3899 best_frontier_score = heuristic(best_frontier_point, goal, mission)
3900-3901 if best_path and _continuous_goal_reached(best_path[-1], goal): break
3902-3904 return { "path": best_path[: mission.max_steps + 1], "candidates": candidates,
3905-3906 "best_frontier_point": best_frontier_point, }
3909-3911def _beam_search_continuous_mpc( belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission,
3912-3915 risk_weight: float, safety_weight: float, node_budget: int, horizon_steps: int,
3916-3918 beam_width: int, branch_width: int, discount_factor: float,
3919-3921 terminal_progress_weight: float, return_cost_map: dict[State3D, float] | None, ) -> dict:
3922-3923 start = _continuous_state_tuple(mission.start) goal = normalize_state(mission.goal)
3924 start_heading = math.atan2(goal[1] - start[1], goal[0] - start[0]) if math.hypot(goal[0] - start[0], goal[1] - start[1]) > 1e-6 else 0.0
3925 min_level, max_level = _search_level_bounds(belief_map, mission)
3926-3927 dt_s = mission.step_distance_m / max(mission.nominal_airspeed, 1e-6) beam = [{
3928 "state": DroneState(x=start[0], y=start[1], z=start[2], heading_rad=start_heading, battery_ratio=1.0, airspeed=mission.nominal_airspeed),
3929-3931 "path": [start], "value": 0.0, "cost": 0.0,
3932-3933 "goal_reached": _continuous_goal_reached(start, goal), }]
3934-3938 expanded = 0 best_candidate = { "state": start, "path": [start], "value": -math.inf,
3939-3941 "cost": 0.0, "goal_reached": _continuous_goal_reached(start, goal), }
3943-3945 for depth in range(max(1, horizon_steps)): if expanded >= node_budget or not beam: break
3946 next_beam: list[dict] = []
3947-3948 for candidate in beam: current = candidate["state"]
3949-3950 if candidate["goal_reached"]: next_beam.append(candidate)
3951 continue
3952-3954 options = _rank_continuous_successors( belief_map=belief_map, mission=mission,
3955-3958 current=current, path_history=candidate["path"], goal=goal, risk_weight=risk_weight,
3959-3961 safety_weight=safety_weight, return_cost_map=return_cost_map, branch_width=branch_width,
3962-3966 spent_cost=candidate["cost"], dt_s=dt_s, min_level=min_level, max_level=max_level, )
3967 expanded += len(options)
3968-3969 for option in options: step_cost = option["step_cost"]
3970-3971 step_reward = option["step_reward"] new_path = candidate["path"] + [option["point"]]
3972 new_value = candidate["value"] + (discount_factor ** depth) * step_reward
3973 new_cost = candidate["cost"] + step_cost
3974-3975 goal_reached = _continuous_goal_reached(option["point"], goal) terminal_bonus = 0.0
3976-3977 if goal_reached: terminal_bonus += terminal_progress_weight
3978 elif depth == horizon_steps - 1:
3979 terminal_bonus += _terminal_bonus(normalize_state(option["point"]), goal, mission, terminal_progress_weight)
3980-3983 next_beam.append( { "state": option["state"], "path": new_path,
3984-3987 "value": new_value + terminal_bonus, "cost": new_cost, "goal_reached": goal_reached, }
3988 )
3989-3993 if not next_beam: break next_beam.sort( key=lambda item: ( item["goal_reached"],
3994 item["value"],
3995 -heuristic(normalize_state((item["state"].x, item["state"].y, item["state"].z)), goal, mission),
3996-3998 ), reverse=True, )
3999 beam = next_beam[:beam_width]
4000 if beam:
4001 frontier_best = max(beam, key=lambda item: (item["goal_reached"], item["value"], len(item["path"])))
4002-4004 if ( frontier_best["goal_reached"] and not best_candidate["goal_reached"] ) or (
4005 frontier_best["goal_reached"] == best_candidate["goal_reached"]
4006 and frontier_best["value"] > best_candidate["value"]
4007-4008 ): best_candidate = frontier_best
4009 if any(item["goal_reached"] for item in beam):
4010-4011 best_candidate = max(beam, key=lambda item: (item["goal_reached"], item["value"])) break
4013-4014 return { "path": best_candidate["path"][: mission.max_steps + 1],
4015-4016 "value": best_candidate["value"], }
4019-4021def _reconstruct_path( came_from: dict[State3D, State3D | None], current: State3D,
4022-4023 max_steps: int, ) -> list[State3D]:
4024-4027 if current not in came_from: return [] path = [] cursor: State3D | None = current
4028-4029 while cursor is not None: path.append(cursor)
4030 cursor = came_from.get(cursor)
4031 path.reverse()
4032 return path[: max_steps + 1]
4035-4036def _weight_schedule(rounds: int, start: float, end: float) -> list[float]: if rounds <= 1:
4037 return [end]
4038 return [start + (end - start) * (idx / (rounds - 1)) for idx in range(rounds)]
4041def _continuous_state_tuple(point: tuple[int, int] | tuple[int, int, int] | tuple[float, float, float]) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
4042-4043 if len(point) == 2: return float(point[0]), float(point[1]), 0.0
4044 return float(point[0]), float(point[1]), float(point[2])
4047def _continuous_goal_reached(point: tuple[float, float, float], goal: State3D) -> bool:
4048 return math.hypot(point[0] - goal[0], point[1] - goal[1]) <= 0.45 and abs(point[2] - goal[2]) <= 0.55
4051def _terminal_bonus(point: State3D, goal: State3D, mission: Mission, terminal_progress_weight: float) -> float:
4052 distance = heuristic(point, goal, mission)
4053 return terminal_progress_weight / (1.0 + distance / max(mission.step_distance_m, 1e-6))
4056def path_distance_m(
4057 path: list[tuple[int, int] | tuple[int, int, int] | tuple[float, float, float]],
4058-4060 step_distance_m: float, altitude_step_m: float | None = None, ) -> float:
4061 distance = 0.0
4063 alt_m = float(step_distance_m if altitude_step_m is None else altitude_step_m)
4064-4065 for (x1, y1, z1), (x2, y2, z2) in zip( [_continuous_state_tuple(point) for point in path],
4066-4067 [_continuous_state_tuple(point) for point in path[1:]], ):
4068 horizontal = math.hypot(x2 - x1, y2 - y1) * step_distance_m
4069-4070 vertical = abs(z2 - z1) * alt_m distance += math.hypot(horizontal, vertical)
4071 return distance
4074-4076def _path_cost( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]], belief_map: BeliefMap,
4077-4080 mission: Mission, risk_weight: float, safety_weight: float, ) -> float:
4081-4083 if len(path) <= 1: return 0.0 currents = np.asarray(path[:-1], dtype=np.float64)
4084 nexts = np.asarray(path[1:], dtype=np.float64)
4085 samples = _sample_belief_states_batch(belief_map, nexts[:, 0], nexts[:, 1], nexts[:, 2])
4086 elev = getattr(mission, "elevation", None)
4087-4090 if elev is not None: terrain_dz = terrain_delta_m_batch( elev, currents[:, 0],
4091-4094 currents[:, 1], nexts[:, 0], nexts[:, 1], )
4095-4096 else: terrain_dz = np.zeros(currents.shape[0], dtype=np.float64)
4097-4099 step_e = transition_energy_batch( airspeed=mission.nominal_airspeed, currents=currents,
4100-4102 nexts=nexts, local_u=samples["wind_u"], local_v=samples["wind_v"],
4103-4104 local_w=samples["wind_w"], step_distance_m=mission.step_distance_m,
4105 altitude_step_m=mission.altitude_step_m,
4106 climb_cost_per_level_j=mission.climb_cost_per_level_j,
4107-4108 hover_power_w=mission.hover_power_w, cruise_power_w=mission.cruise_power_w,
4109 hotel_power_w=mission.hotel_power_w,
4110 headwind_power_per_mps_w=mission.headwind_power_per_mps_w,
4111 climb_power_per_mps_w=mission.climb_power_per_mps_w,
4112 descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w=mission.descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w,
4113-4114 terrain_dz_m=terrain_dz, )
4116 dz_levels = np.abs(nexts[:, 2].astype(np.int64) - currents[:, 2].astype(np.int64)).astype(np.float64)
4117-4118 total = ( step_e
4119 + 22.0 * risk_weight * samples["uncertainty"]
4120 + 28.0 * safety_weight * samples["safety_penalty"]
4121-4122 - 18.0 * samples["expected_energy_gain"] + 10.0 * dz_levels
4123 )
4124 return float(np.sum(total))
4127-4129def _path_cost_breakdown( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]], belief_map: BeliefMap,
4130-4133 mission: Mission, risk_weight: float, safety_weight: float, ) -> dict[str, float]:
4134-4137 totals = { "energy_j": 0.0, "progress_reward_j": 0.0, "uncertainty_cost_j": 0.0,
4138-4140 "safety_cost_j": 0.0, "altitude_bias_j": 0.0, "vertical_maneuver_cost_j": 0.0,
4141-4142 "total_cost_j": 0.0, }
4143 for current, nxt in zip(path, path[1:]):
4144 local = _sample_belief_state(belief_map, nxt[0], nxt[1], nxt[2])
4145-4147 energy_j = transition_energy_j( airspeed=mission.nominal_airspeed, current=current,
4148-4151 nxt=nxt, local_u=local["wind_u"], local_v=local["wind_v"], local_w=local["wind_w"],
4152-4153 step_distance_m=mission.step_distance_m, altitude_step_m=mission.altitude_step_m,
4154 climb_cost_per_level_j=mission.climb_cost_per_level_j,
4155-4156 hover_power_w=mission.hover_power_w, cruise_power_w=mission.cruise_power_w,
4157 hotel_power_w=mission.hotel_power_w,
4158 headwind_power_per_mps_w=mission.headwind_power_per_mps_w,
4159 climb_power_per_mps_w=mission.climb_power_per_mps_w,
4160 descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w=mission.descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w,
4161-4162 terrain_dz_m=_mission_terrain_dz(mission, current[0], current[1], nxt[0], nxt[1]), )
4163 progress_reward = 18.0 * local["expected_energy_gain"]
4164 uncertainty_cost = 22.0 * risk_weight * local["uncertainty"]
4165 safety_cost = 28.0 * safety_weight * local["safety_penalty"]
4166 altitude_bias = 8.0 * abs(goal_altitude_gap(normalize_state(current), mission)) - 8.0 * abs(goal_altitude_gap(normalize_state(nxt), mission))
4167 terrain_levels = _mission_terrain_dz(mission, current[0], current[1], nxt[0], nxt[1]) / max(mission.altitude_step_m, 1e-6)
4168 vertical_cost = 10.0 * abs(nxt[2] - current[2]) + 8.0 * max(0.0, terrain_levels)
4169 total = energy_j - progress_reward + uncertainty_cost + safety_cost + altitude_bias + vertical_cost
4170-4171 totals["energy_j"] += energy_j totals["progress_reward_j"] += progress_reward
4172-4173 totals["uncertainty_cost_j"] += uncertainty_cost totals["safety_cost_j"] += safety_cost
4174 totals["altitude_bias_j"] += altitude_bias
4175-4176 totals["vertical_maneuver_cost_j"] += vertical_cost totals["total_cost_j"] += total
4177 return totals
4180-4182def _rank_continuous_successors( belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission,
4183-4185 current: DroneState, path_history: list[tuple[float, float, float]], goal: State3D,
4186-4188 risk_weight: float, safety_weight: float, return_cost_map: dict[State3D, float] | None,
4189-4193 branch_width: int, spent_cost: float, dt_s: float, min_level: int, max_level: int,
4194-4195) -> list[dict]: local = _sample_belief_state(belief_map, current.x, current.y, current.z)
4196 floor = _effective_agl_floor(mission, horizontal_to_goal=math.hypot(goal[0] - current.x, goal[1] - current.y))
4197 effective_min = max(min_level, int(math.floor(floor)))
4198-4202 preferred_z = _preferred_cruise_altitude( belief_map, current.x, current.y, goal,
4203-4205 effective_min, max_level, current_z=current.z,
4206 climb_cost_per_level_j=mission.climb_cost_per_level_j,
4207 clearance_agl_level=getattr(mission, "clearance_agl_level", 1.0),
4208-4209 preferred_cruise_agl=getattr(mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None), )
4210-4214 preferred_z = max(preferred_z, floor) controls = _control_library( current, goal, local,
4215-4218 mission, preferred_z=preferred_z, belief_map=belief_map, )
4219 bearing = math.atan2(goal[1] - current.y, goal[0] - current.x) if math.hypot(goal[0] - current.x, goal[1] - current.y) > 1e-6 else current.heading_rad
4220 horizontal = math.hypot(goal[0] - current.x, goal[1] - current.y)
4221 level_lo = float(effective_min if horizontal > 5.0 else min_level)
4222-4225 level_hi = float(max_level) proposals: list[dict] = []
4226-4229 for control in controls: next_state, aero_energy = propagate_control( current, control,
4230-4233 dt_s=dt_s, local_u=local["wind_u"], local_v=local["wind_v"], local_w=local["wind_w"],
4234-4235 step_distance_m=mission.step_distance_m, altitude_step_m=mission.altitude_step_m,
4236 min_altitude_level=effective_min if horizontal > 5.0 else min_level,
4237-4238 max_altitude_level=max_level, )
4239-4240 point = ( clamp(next_state.x, 0.0, belief_map.width - 1),
4241-4242 clamp(next_state.y, 0.0, belief_map.height - 1), clamp(next_state.z, level_lo, level_hi),
4243 )
4244 if horizontal > 5.0 and goal[2] <= current.z + 0.25 and point[2] > preferred_z + 0.85:
4245 continue
4246-4249 proposals.append( { "state": next_state, "point": point,
4250-4252 "aero_energy": float(aero_energy), "control": control, }
4253 )
4254-4255 if not proposals: return []
4258 points = np.asarray([item["point"] for item in proposals], dtype=np.float64)
4259 next_locals = _sample_belief_states_batch(belief_map, points[:, 0], points[:, 1], points[:, 2])
4260 elev = getattr(mission, "elevation", None)
4261-4263 if elev is not None: terrain_dz = terrain_delta_m_batch( elev,
4264-4266 np.full(len(proposals), current.x), np.full(len(proposals), current.y), points[:, 0],
4267-4268 points[:, 1], )
4269-4270 else: terrain_dz = np.zeros(len(proposals), dtype=np.float64)
4271 currents = np.tile(np.asarray([current.x, current.y, current.z], dtype=np.float64), (len(proposals), 1))
4272-4274 terrain_energies = transition_energy_batch( airspeed=current.airspeed, currents=currents,
4275-4276 nexts=points, local_u=np.full(len(proposals), local["wind_u"]),
4277 local_v=np.full(len(proposals), local["wind_v"]),
4278-4279 local_w=np.full(len(proposals), local["wind_w"]), step_distance_m=mission.step_distance_m,
4280 altitude_step_m=mission.altitude_step_m,
4281 climb_cost_per_level_j=mission.climb_cost_per_level_j,
4282-4283 hover_power_w=mission.hover_power_w, cruise_power_w=mission.cruise_power_w,
4284 hotel_power_w=mission.hotel_power_w,
4285 headwind_power_per_mps_w=mission.headwind_power_per_mps_w,
4286 climb_power_per_mps_w=mission.climb_power_per_mps_w,
4287 descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w=mission.descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w,
4288-4289 terrain_dz_m=terrain_dz, )
4293-4294 n_prop = len(proposals) current_energy_to_goal = estimate_energy_to_goal_j(
4295 (current.x, current.y, current.z), goal, mission, local, belief_map=belief_map
4296-4297 ) next_pts = [item["point"] for item in proposals]
4298 next_energies = estimate_energies_to_goal_batch(
4299 next_pts, goal, mission, belief_map=belief_map, local_winds=None
4300-4302 ) segs = np.empty((n_prop * 3, 5), dtype=np.float64)
4303 look_cells_arr = np.empty(n_prop, dtype=np.float64)
4304 track_bearing_arr = np.empty(n_prop, dtype=np.float64)
4305 step_len_arr = np.empty(n_prop, dtype=np.float64)
4306 goal_look = min(4.0, max(horizontal, 1.0))
4307 goal_ax = current.x + math.cos(bearing) * goal_look
4308 goal_ay = current.y + math.sin(bearing) * goal_look
4309-4310 for i, point in enumerate(next_pts): step_dx = point[0] - current.x
4311-4312 step_dy = point[1] - current.y step_len = math.hypot(step_dx, step_dy)
4313 step_len_arr[i] = step_len
4314 track_bearing = math.atan2(step_dy, step_dx) if step_len > 1e-6 else bearing
4315-4316 track_bearing_arr[i] = track_bearing look_cells = min(4.0, max(2.0, horizontal * 0.35))
4317-4318 look_cells_arr[i] = look_cells ahead_x = point[0] + math.cos(track_bearing) * look_cells
4319 ahead_y = point[1] + math.sin(track_bearing) * look_cells
4320 segs[i * 3 + 0] = (current.x, current.y, point[0], point[1], point[2])
4321 segs[i * 3 + 1] = (point[0], point[1], ahead_x, ahead_y, point[2])
4322 segs[i * 3 + 2] = (current.x, current.y, goal_ax, goal_ay, point[2])
4323 track_tw, track_w = _integrate_tracks_batch(belief_map, segs, samples=2)
4325 terrain_rises = np.empty(n_prop, dtype=np.float64)
4326-4328 if elev is not None: terrain_rises[:] = terrain_climb_along_line_batch( elev,
4329-4332 np.full(n_prop, current.x), np.full(n_prop, current.y), points[:, 0], points[:, 1],
4333-4334 samples=2, )
4335-4339 else: terrain_rises.fill(0.0) nl_w = next_locals["wind_w"]
4340-4341 nl_unc = next_locals["uncertainty"] nl_safe = next_locals["safety_penalty"]
4342-4343 nl_gain = next_locals["expected_energy_gain"] nl_up = next_locals["mode_prob_uplift"]
4344-4346 nl_sink = next_locals["mode_prob_sink"] scored: list[dict] = []
4347-4348 near_goal = horizontal <= 5.0 final_approach = horizontal <= 3.5
4349-4350 alt_step = max(mission.altitude_step_m, 1e-6) climb_cost = mission.climb_cost_per_level_j
4351-4352 for i, item in enumerate(proposals): next_state = item["state"]
4353-4355 point = item["point"] aero_energy = item["aero_energy"] control = item["control"]
4356-4357 wind_w_i = float(nl_w[i]) terrain_rise_m = float(terrain_rises[i])
4358 terrain_energy = float(terrain_energies[i])
4359 step_energy = 0.55 * aero_energy + 0.45 * terrain_energy
4361 energy_progress_reward = current_energy_to_goal - float(next_energies[i])
4363 xy_progress = _goal_axis_progress(current, point, goal)
4364 altitude_progress = abs(goal[2] - current.z) - abs(goal[2] - point[2])
4365 alt_weight = 75.0 if final_approach else (20.0 if near_goal else 4.0)
4366 goal_progress_reward = 82.0 * xy_progress + alt_weight * altitude_progress
4368-4369 step_len = float(step_len_arr[i]) seg_tw = float(track_tw[i * 3 + 0])
4370-4371 seg_w = float(track_w[i * 3 + 0]) lookahead_tw = float(track_tw[i * 3 + 1])
4372-4373 lookahead_w = float(track_w[i * 3 + 1]) goal_tw = float(track_tw[i * 3 + 2])
4374 goal_w = float(track_w[i * 3 + 2])
4375 blend_tw = 0.40 * seg_tw + 0.35 * lookahead_tw + 0.25 * goal_tw
4376-4377 blend_w = 0.40 * seg_w + 0.35 * lookahead_w + 0.25 * goal_w wind_assist = (
4378-4380 70.0 * blend_tw - 38.0 * max(0.0, -blend_tw) + 48.0 * max(0.0, blend_w)
4381 - 36.0 * max(0.0, -blend_w)
4382 )
4383 if not near_goal:
4384 band_progress = abs(current.z - preferred_z) - abs(point[2] - preferred_z)
4385 wind_assist += 40.0 * band_progress - 12.0 * abs(point[2] - preferred_z)
4386-4387 free_lift = max(0.0, wind_w_i - 0.1) climb_dz = point[2] - current.z
4388 if abs(current.z - preferred_z) <= 0.3 and abs(point[2] - preferred_z) <= 0.35:
4389 wind_assist += 18.0 - 40.0 * abs(climb_dz)
4390 elif climb_dz > 0.05:
4391 if point[2] <= preferred_z + 0.35 and (free_lift > 0.08 or blend_tw >= -0.2):
4392 wind_assist += 28.0 * max(free_lift, 0.05) * climb_dz
4393 elif point[2] > preferred_z + 0.2 or blend_tw < -0.5:
4394 wind_assist -= 90.0 * max(climb_dz, point[2] - preferred_z)
4395-4396 else: wind_assist -= 50.0 * climb_dz
4397-4398 if terrain_rise_m > 2.0: terrain_cost = (terrain_rise_m / alt_step) * climb_cost
4399 wind_payoff = max(0.0, 55.0 * blend_tw + 40.0 * max(0.0, blend_w))
4400-4401 if wind_payoff < 1.25 * terrain_cost: wind_assist -= terrain_cost - 0.4 * wind_payoff
4402-4403 else: wind_assist -= 0.25 * terrain_cost
4405 cross_track = _cross_track_cells(mission.start, goal, point[0], point[1])
4406 if not near_goal and cross_track > 0.75:
4407 wind_assist -= 14.0 * max(0.0, cross_track - 0.75)
4408-4409 if step_len > 1e-6 and not near_goal: wasted = max(0.0, step_len - max(0.0, xy_progress))
4410 wind_assist -= 36.0 * wasted
4411 is_thermal = str(control.get("mode", "")) == "thermal_orbit"
4413 if not near_goal and xy_progress < 0.20 and not is_thermal:
4414 wind_assist -= 55.0 * max(0.0, blend_w)
4415 energy_progress_reward = min(energy_progress_reward, 10.0)
4417 risk_cost = 30.0 * risk_weight * float(nl_unc[i]) + 36.0 * safety_weight * float(nl_safe[i])
4418 revisit_penalty = _continuous_revisit_penalty(path_history, point) * 0.25
4419 speed_penalty = 8.0 * abs(next_state.airspeed - DEFAULT_ENVELOPE.best_glide_speed)
4420 step_cost = step_energy + risk_cost + speed_penalty + revisit_penalty
4422 return_budget = mission.max_return_cost_j
4423 if return_cost_map is not None and return_budget is not None:
4424 effective_budget = return_budget * (1.45 if near_goal else 1.15)
4425 return_cost = return_cost_map.get(normalize_state(point), math.inf)
4426-4427 if return_cost > effective_budget: continue
4428-4429 if spent_cost + step_cost + return_cost > effective_budget: continue
4431 if final_approach and point[2] > current.z + 0.05:
4432 goal_progress_reward -= 140.0 * (point[2] - current.z)
4434 uplift_scale = 0.08 if final_approach else (0.45 if near_goal else 1.15)
4435-4436 belief_energy_bonus = uplift_scale * ( 40.0 * float(nl_gain[i])
4437-4438 + 55.0 * float(nl_up[i]) - 30.0 * float(nl_sink[i])
4439 )
4440-4441 if is_thermal: belief_energy_bonus *= 1.25
4442-4443 elif not near_goal and xy_progress < 0.20: belief_energy_bonus *= 0.15
4444 step_reward = energy_progress_reward + goal_progress_reward + belief_energy_bonus + wind_assist - step_cost
4445-4449 scored.append( { "state": next_state, "point": point, "step_cost": step_cost,
4450-4453 "step_reward": step_reward, "score": step_reward, "control": control, }
4454 )
4455 scored.sort(key=lambda item: item["score"], reverse=True)
4456 return scored[:branch_width]
4459-4460def _continuous_revisit_penalty( path_history: list[tuple[float, float, float]],
4461-4463 point: tuple[float, float, float], lookback: int = 6, ) -> float:
4464 recent_points = path_history[-lookback:]
4465 repeats = sum(1 for prev in recent_points if math.hypot(prev[0] - point[0], prev[1] - point[1]) <= 0.75 and abs(prev[2] - point[2]) <= 0.6)
4466-4467 if repeats <= 0: return 0.0
4468 return 42.0 * repeats
4471-4473def _goal_axis_progress( current: DroneState, point: tuple[float, float, float],
4474-4475 goal: State3D, ) -> float:
4476 """Euclidean progress toward the goal (cells). Sideways motion gets no credit."""
4477-4478 dx_goal = goal[0] - current.x dy_goal = goal[1] - current.y
4479 goal_dist = math.hypot(dx_goal, dy_goal)
4480-4482 if goal_dist <= 1e-6: return 0.0 dx_step = point[0] - current.x
4483 dy_step = point[1] - current.y
4484 return max(0.0, (dx_step * dx_goal + dy_step * dy_goal) / goal_dist)
4487def _cross_track_cells(
4488 start: tuple[float, float] | tuple[float, float, float] | tuple[int, int] | tuple[int, int, int],
4489-4492 goal: State3D, x: float, y: float, ) -> float:
4493-4494 sx, sy = float(start[0]), float(start[1]) gx, gy = float(goal[0]), float(goal[1])
4495-4496 dx, dy = gx - sx, gy - sy length = math.hypot(dx, dy)
4497-4498 if length <= 1e-6: return math.hypot(x - sx, y - sy)
4500 return abs((x - sx) * dy - (y - sy) * dx) / length
4503-4508def _lookahead_wind( belief_map: BeliefMap, x: float, y: float, z: float, bearing: float,
4509-4510 cells: float = 3.0, ) -> tuple[float, float]:
4511 """Average along-track wind a few cells ahead of (x, y)."""
4512-4516 return _integrate_track_wind( belief_map, x, y, x + math.cos(bearing) * cells,
4517-4520 y + math.sin(bearing) * cells, z, samples=4, )
4523-4527def _integrate_track_wind( belief_map: BeliefMap, x0: float, y0: float, x1: float,
4528-4531 y1: float, z: float, samples: int = 5, ) -> tuple[float, float]:
4532 """Integrate along-track tailwind and vertical wind over a segment."""
4533-4534 tw, ww = _integrate_tracks_batch( belief_map,
4535-4537 np.asarray([[x0, y0, x1, y1, z]], dtype=np.float64), samples=samples, )
4538 return float(tw[0]), float(ww[0])
4541-4544def _integrate_tracks_batch( belief_map: BeliefMap, segs: np.ndarray, samples: int = 2,
4545-4546) -> tuple[np.ndarray, np.ndarray]: """Batch along-track wind integrate.
4548-4549 ``segs`` shape (N, 5): x0,y0,x1,y1,z → returns (tw[N], w[N]). """
4550-4551 segs = np.asarray(segs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1, 5) n = segs.shape[0]
4552-4555 if n == 0: return np.zeros(0), np.zeros(0) n_samp = max(2, int(samples)) x0 = segs[:, 0]
4556-4561 y0 = segs[:, 1] x1 = segs[:, 2] y1 = segs[:, 3] z = segs[:, 4] dx = x1 - x0 dy = y1 - y0
4562-4563 span = np.hypot(dx, dy) bearings = np.arctan2(dy, dx)
4564 bearings = np.where(span > 1e-9, bearings, 0.0)
4565 t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n_samp, dtype=np.float64)[None, :]
4566 xs = np.clip(x0[:, None] + dx[:, None] * t, 0.0, float(belief_map.width - 1)).reshape(-1)
4567 ys = np.clip(y0[:, None] + dy[:, None] * t, 0.0, float(belief_map.height - 1)).reshape(-1)
4568-4569 zs = np.repeat(z, n_samp) wind = _sample_wind_batch(belief_map, xs, ys, zs)
4570-4571 wu = wind["wind_u"].reshape(n, n_samp) wv = wind["wind_v"].reshape(n, n_samp)
4572-4573 ww = wind["wind_w"].reshape(n, n_samp) cos_b = np.cos(bearings)[:, None]
4574-4575 sin_b = np.sin(bearings)[:, None] tw = np.mean(wu * cos_b + wv * sin_b, axis=1)
4576 w_mean = np.mean(ww, axis=1)
4577 return tw, w_mean
4580def _sample_wind_batch(belief_map: BeliefMap, xs, ys, zs) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
4581 arrays = belief_map.field_arrays
4582 if arrays is None or "wind_u" not in arrays:
4583 arrays = ensure_belief_field_arrays(belief_map, _WIND_ATTRS)
4584 xs_a = np.asarray(xs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
4585 ys_a = np.asarray(ys, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
4586 zs_a = np.asarray(zs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
4587 return {attr: trilinear_sample_batch(arrays[attr], xs_a, ys_a, zs_a) for attr in _WIND_ATTRS}
4590def _heading_turn_rate(current_heading: float, desired_heading: float) -> float:
4591 err = math.atan2(math.sin(desired_heading - current_heading), math.cos(desired_heading - current_heading))
4592 return clamp(err, -DEFAULT_ENVELOPE.max_turn_rate_rad_s, DEFAULT_ENVELOPE.max_turn_rate_rad_s)
4595-4598def _control_library( current: DroneState, goal: State3D, local: dict[str, float],

4676-4677 0.45 * math.cos(goal_heading) + 0.55 * math.cos(wind_heading), )

4691 belief_map, current.x, current.y, ax, ay, current.z, samples=4
4692-4693 ) score += 60.0 * tw + 35.0 * max(0.0, ww) - 30.0 * max(0.0, -tw)

4698-4699 else: score += local["wind_u"] * math.cos(heading) + local["wind_v"] * math.sin(heading)

4708-4709 "climb_bias": cruise_bias, }
4710 )

4765def _sample_attr(belief_map: BeliefMap, attr: str, x: float, y: float, z: float) -> float:
4766 arrays = belief_map.field_arrays
4767 if arrays is not None and attr in arrays:
4768 return float(trilinear_sample(arrays[attr], x, y, z))
4769-4770 z = clamp(z, 0.0, belief_map.levels - 1) z0 = int(math.floor(z))
4771-4772 z1 = min(z0 + 1, belief_map.levels - 1) tz = z - z0
4773 lower = _sample_attr_2d(belief_map, attr, x, y, z0)
4774 upper = _sample_attr_2d(belief_map, attr, x, y, z1)
4775 return lower + (upper - lower) * tz
4778def _sample_attr_2d(belief_map: BeliefMap, attr: str, x: float, y: float, z: int) -> float:
4779-4780 x = clamp(x, 0.0, belief_map.width - 1) y = clamp(y, 0.0, belief_map.height - 1)
4781-4783 x0 = int(math.floor(x)) y0 = int(math.floor(y)) x1 = min(x0 + 1, belief_map.width - 1)
4784-4786 y1 = min(y0 + 1, belief_map.height - 1) tx = x - x0 ty = y - y0
4787 v00 = getattr(belief_map.cells[z][y0][x0], attr)
4788 v10 = getattr(belief_map.cells[z][y0][x1], attr)
4789 v01 = getattr(belief_map.cells[z][y1][x0], attr)
4790-4791 v11 = getattr(belief_map.cells[z][y1][x1], attr) top = v00 + (v10 - v00) * tx
4792 bottom = v01 + (v11 - v01) * tx
4793 return top + (bottom - top) * ty
4796-4799def _rank_successors( belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission, current: State3D,
4800-4804 goal: State3D, min_level: int, max_level: int, risk_weight: float, safety_weight: float,
4805-4807 return_cost_map: dict[State3D, float] | None, branch_width: int, spent_cost: float,
4808-4809) -> list[dict]: scored: list[dict] = []
4810-4814 for nxt in neighbors( current[0], current[1], current[2], belief_map.width,
4815-4818 belief_map.height, min_level, max_level, ):
4819-4823 breakdown = transition_cost_breakdown( belief_map, current, nxt, mission,
4824-4826 risk_weight=risk_weight, safety_weight=safety_weight, )
4827-4828 if not math.isfinite(breakdown.total_cost_j): continue
4829-4830 current_altitude_gap = abs(goal[2] - current[2]) next_altitude_gap = abs(goal[2] - nxt[2])
4831-4832 if next_altitude_gap > current_altitude_gap: continue
4833 if return_cost_map is not None and mission.max_return_cost_j is not None:
4834 return_cost = return_cost_map.get(nxt, math.inf)
4835-4836 if return_cost > mission.max_return_cost_j: continue
4837 if spent_cost + breakdown.total_cost_j + return_cost > mission.max_return_cost_j:
4838 continue
4839 current_energy_to_goal = estimate_energy_to_goal_j((float(current[0]), float(current[1]), float(current[2])), goal, mission)
4840 next_energy_to_goal = estimate_energy_to_goal_j((float(nxt[0]), float(nxt[1]), float(nxt[2])), goal, mission)
4842 energy_progress_reward = current_energy_to_goal - next_energy_to_goal
4844-4846 step_reward = energy_progress_reward - breakdown.total_cost_j scored.append( {
4847-4849 "state": nxt, "score": step_reward, "step_reward": step_reward,
4850-4851 "step_cost": breakdown.total_cost_j, }
4852 )
4853 scored.sort(key=lambda item: item["score"], reverse=True)
4854 return scored[:branch_width]
4857-4859def _return_budget_is_tight( mission: Mission, start: tuple[float, float, float],
4860-4861 goal: State3D, ) -> bool:
4862 """True when remaining return budget may bind — otherwise skip Dijkstra."""
4863-4864 budget = mission.max_return_cost_j home = mission.home
4865-4867 if budget is None or home is None: return False home_state = normalize_state(home)
4868 to_goal = estimate_energy_to_goal_j(start, goal, mission)
4869 goal_pt = (float(goal[0]), float(goal[1]), float(goal[2]))
4870 home_from_goal = estimate_energy_to_goal_j(goal_pt, home_state, mission)
4871 home_from_here = estimate_energy_to_goal_j(start, home_state, mission)
4872 need = max(to_goal + home_from_goal, home_from_here) * 1.45
4873 return float(budget) < need
4876-4882def _coarse_neighbors( x: int, y: int, z: int, width: int, height: int, min_z: int,
4883-4885 max_z: int, stride: int, ) -> list[State3D]:
4886 """26-connected neighbors on a stride-aligned XY lattice (full Z)."""
4887 result: list[State3D] = []
4888-4890 for dz in (-1, 0, 1): for dy in (-stride, 0, stride): for dx in (-stride, 0, stride):
4891-4895 if dx == 0 and dy == 0 and dz == 0: continue nx = x + dx ny = y + dy nz = z + dz
4896 if 0 <= nx < width and 0 <= ny < height and min_z <= nz <= max_z:
4897 result.append((nx, ny, nz))
4898 return result
4901class ReturnCostLookup:
4902 """Coarse-grid return costs with nearest-cell lookup (dict-compatible .get)."""
4904 __slots__ = ("costs", "stride")
4906 def __init__(self, costs: dict[State3D, float], stride: int = 1) -> None:
4907-4908 self.costs = costs self.stride = max(1, int(stride))
4910 def _key(self, point: State3D | tuple[float, float, float]) -> State3D:
4911-4912 x, y, z = normalize_state(point) stride = self.stride
4913-4914 if stride > 1: x = (x // stride) * stride
4915 y = (y // stride) * stride
4916 return x, y, z
4918 def get(self, point: State3D | tuple[float, float, float], default: float = math.inf) -> float:
4919 return self.costs.get(self._key(point), default)
4921 def __getitem__(self, point: State3D | tuple[float, float, float]) -> float:
4922 key = self._key(point)
4923-4924 if key not in self.costs: raise KeyError(point)
4925 return self.costs[key]
4927-4928 def __contains__(self, point: object) -> bool: try:
4929 return self._key(point) in self.costs # type: ignore[arg-type]
4930-4931 except Exception: return False
4933-4934 def __len__(self) -> int: return len(self.costs)
4937-4940def compute_return_cost_map( belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission, *,
4941-4942 xy_stride: int | None = None, ) -> ReturnCostLookup:
4943 """Dijkstra return costs from home.
4945 On wide fine grids, use XY stride>1 (default 2 when width≥32) so the node
4946-4948 count drops ~4× while lookups snap to the coarse lattice. """ width = belief_map.width
4949-4950 height = belief_map.height min_level, max_level = _search_level_bounds(belief_map, mission)
4951 stride = 2 if xy_stride is None and width >= 32 else max(1, int(xy_stride or 1))
4952 costs: dict[State3D, float] = {}
4953-4954 if mission.home is None: return ReturnCostLookup(costs, stride=stride)
4955 home = normalize_state(mission.home)
4956 home = ((home[0] // stride) * stride, (home[1] // stride) * stride, home[2])
4957-4958 costs[home] = 0.0 frontier: list[tuple[float, State3D]] = [(0.0, home)]
4959-4960 while frontier: current_cost, current = heapq.heappop(frontier)
4961-4963 if current_cost > costs.get(current, math.inf): continue nbrs = _coarse_neighbors(
4964-4972 current[0], current[1], current[2], width, height, min_level, max_level, stride, )
4973-4975 if not nbrs: continue nxt_arr = np.asarray(nbrs, dtype=np.float64)
4976 cur_arr = np.tile(np.asarray(current, dtype=np.float64), (len(nbrs), 1))
4977 samples = _sample_belief_states_batch(belief_map, nxt_arr[:, 0], nxt_arr[:, 1], nxt_arr[:, 2])
4978 elev = getattr(mission, "elevation", None)
4979-4982 if elev is not None: terrain_dz = terrain_delta_m_batch( elev, nxt_arr[:, 0],
4983-4986 nxt_arr[:, 1], cur_arr[:, 0], cur_arr[:, 1], )
4987-4988 else: terrain_dz = np.zeros(len(nbrs), dtype=np.float64)
4989-4991 step_costs = transition_energy_batch( airspeed=mission.nominal_airspeed, currents=nxt_arr,
4992-4994 nexts=cur_arr, local_u=samples["wind_u"], local_v=samples["wind_v"],
4995-4996 local_w=samples["wind_w"], step_distance_m=mission.step_distance_m,
4997 altitude_step_m=mission.altitude_step_m,
4998 climb_cost_per_level_j=mission.climb_cost_per_level_j,
4999-5000 hover_power_w=mission.hover_power_w, cruise_power_w=mission.cruise_power_w,
5001 hotel_power_w=mission.hotel_power_w,
5002 headwind_power_per_mps_w=mission.headwind_power_per_mps_w,
5003 climb_power_per_mps_w=mission.climb_power_per_mps_w,
5004 descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w=mission.descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w,
5005-5006 terrain_dz_m=terrain_dz, )
5007-5008 for nbr, step_cost in zip(nbrs, step_costs): new_cost = current_cost + float(step_cost)
5009-5010 if new_cost < costs.get(nbr, math.inf): costs[nbr] = new_cost
5011 heapq.heappush(frontier, (new_cost, nbr))
5012 return ReturnCostLookup(costs, stride=stride)
5015-5018def _return_step_cost( belief_map: BeliefMap, current: State3D, nxt: State3D,
5019-5020 mission: Mission, ) -> float:
5021 cx, cy, cz = current
5022 cell = _sample_belief_state(belief_map, float(cx), float(cy), float(cz))

5027-5029 local_u=cell["wind_u"], local_v=cell["wind_v"], local_w=cell["wind_w"],
5030-5031 step_distance_m=mission.step_distance_m, altitude_step_m=mission.altitude_step_m,
5032 climb_cost_per_level_j=mission.climb_cost_per_level_j,
5033-5034 hover_power_w=mission.hover_power_w, cruise_power_w=mission.cruise_power_w,
5035 hotel_power_w=mission.hotel_power_w,

5043-5046def _altitude_bonus( z: int, mission: Mission, x: float | None = None,
5047-5050 y: float | None = None, belief_cell=None, move_dx: float = 0.0, move_dy: float = 0.0,
5051-5052) -> float: """Cruise: reward wind-efficient altitude. Approach: reward goal altitude."""
5053-5054 if mission.max_altitude_level <= mission.min_altitude_level: return 0.0
5055 span = max(1, mission.max_altitude_level - mission.min_altitude_level)
5056 goal = normalize_state(mission.goal)
5057-5058 if x is None or y is None: return 1.0 - abs(z - goal[2]) / span
5059 horizontal = math.hypot(goal[0] - x, goal[1] - y)
5060-5061 if horizontal <= 5.0: return 1.0 - abs(z - goal[2]) / span
5063 move_norm = max(math.hypot(move_dx, move_dy), 1e-6)
5064-5065 if belief_cell is None: return 0.0
5066-5067 if isinstance(belief_cell, dict): wind_u = float(belief_cell["wind_u"])
5068-5069 wind_v = float(belief_cell["wind_v"]) wind_w = float(belief_cell["wind_w"])
5070 energy = float(belief_cell["expected_energy_gain"])
5071-5072 else: wind_u = float(belief_cell.wind_u)
5073-5074 wind_v = float(belief_cell.wind_v) wind_w = float(belief_cell.wind_w)
5075 energy = float(belief_cell.expected_energy_gain)
5076 tailwind = (wind_u * (move_dx / move_norm)) + (wind_v * (move_dy / move_norm))
5077 score = 0.45 * tailwind + 0.55 * wind_w + 0.35 * energy
5078 return max(-1.0, min(1.0, score / 4.0))
5081-5085def _preferred_cruise_altitude( belief_map: BeliefMap, x: float, y: float, goal: State3D,
5086-5088 min_level: int, max_level: int, current_z: float | None = None,
5089-5090 climb_cost_per_level_j: float = 180.0, clearance_agl_level: float = 1.0,
5091-5092 preferred_cruise_agl: float | None = None, ) -> float:
5093 """Pick the energy-optimal AGL band above the safety floor — no forced cruise height.
5095 Climb / hold / descend only when wind+energy scores justify it. Near the goal,
5096 track goal altitude for landing. Cruise never plans below clearance AGL.
5097-5098 A sticky preferred_cruise_agl (from multi-band guides) biases the band choice. """
5099 bearing = math.atan2(goal[1] - y, goal[0] - x) if math.hypot(goal[0] - x, goal[1] - y) > 1e-6 else 0.0
5100 horizontal_cells = max(math.hypot(goal[0] - x, goal[1] - y), 1.0)
5101-5102 z_now = float(min_level if current_z is None else current_z) goal_z = float(goal[2])
5103 floor = cruise_agl_floor(horizontal_cells, float(min_level), clearance_agl_level)
5104 search_min = max(min_level, int(math.floor(floor)))
5107-5108 if horizontal_cells <= 5.0: if horizontal_cells <= 3.5:
5109 return clamp(goal_z, float(min_level), float(max_level))
5110 return clamp(z_now + 0.6 * (goal_z - z_now), float(min_level), float(max_level))
5114 if preferred_cruise_agl is not None and horizontal_cells > 6.0:
5115 sticky = clamp(float(preferred_cruise_agl), float(search_min), float(max_level))
5116-5117 candidates = { int(round(clamp(z_now, search_min, max_level))),
5118 int(round(clamp(sticky, search_min, max_level))),
5119 int(round(clamp(sticky - 1.0, search_min, max_level))),
5120 int(round(clamp(sticky + 1.0, search_min, max_level))),
5121-5122 int(round(clamp(floor, search_min, max_level))), }
5123 levels = sorted(candidates)
5124-5125 else: levels = list(range(search_min, max_level + 1))
5126-5127 if not levels: return clamp(z_now, float(min_level), float(max_level))
5130 fracs = (0.0, 0.3, 0.6)
5131 track_xy = [(x + f * (goal[0] - x), y + f * (goal[1] - y)) for f in fracs]
5132-5134 xs: list[float] = [x] ys: list[float] = [y] zs: list[float] = [z_now]
5136-5137 level_here_idx: dict[int, int] = {} level_track_idx: dict[int, tuple[int, int, int]] = {}
5138-5139 for level in levels: level_here_idx[level] = len(xs)
5140-5143 xs.append(x) ys.append(y) zs.append(float(level)) track_ids = []
5144-5145 for sx, sy in track_xy: track_ids.append(len(xs))
5146-5148 xs.append(sx) ys.append(sy) zs.append(float(level))
5149 level_track_idx[level] = (track_ids[0], track_ids[1], track_ids[2])
5151-5152 packed = _sample_belief_states_batch(belief_map, xs, ys, zs) cos_b = math.cos(bearing)
5153 sin_b = math.sin(bearing)
5154 here = {attr: float(packed[attr][0]) for attr in _BELIEF_SAMPLE_ATTRS}
5156-5157 def band_tailwind(level: int) -> float: i0, i1, i2 = level_track_idx[level]
5158 tw = 0.0
5159 for i in (i0, i1, i2):
5160 tw += float(packed["wind_u"][i]) * cos_b + float(packed["wind_v"][i]) * sin_b
5161 return tw / 3.0
5163-5164 def band_score(level: int) -> float: i = level_here_idx[level]
5165-5166 local_u = float(packed["wind_u"][i]) local_v = float(packed["wind_v"][i])
5167-5168 local_w = float(packed["wind_w"][i]) energy = float(packed["expected_energy_gain"][i])
5169-5170 uplift = float(packed["mode_prob_uplift"][i]) sink = float(packed["mode_prob_sink"][i])
5171-5172 unc = float(packed["uncertainty"][i]) safety = float(packed["safety_penalty"][i])
5173-5175 tw = band_tailwind(level) wind_saving = horizontal_cells * ( 22.0 * tw
5176-5178 - 20.0 * max(0.0, -tw) + 16.0 * max(0.0, local_w) - 18.0 * max(0.0, -local_w)
5179-5181 + 10.0 * energy + 5.0 * uplift - 12.0 * sink
5182-5183 ) free_lift = clamp(2.5 * max(0.0, here["wind_w"] - 0.1), 0.0, 2.0)
5184 powered_climb = max(0.0, float(level) - z_now - free_lift)
5185 climb_up = powered_climb * climb_cost_per_level_j
5186 terminal_align = abs(float(level) - goal_z) * 0.7 * climb_cost_per_level_j
5187 leave_current = abs(float(level) - z_now) * 0.2 * climb_cost_per_level_j
5188-5189 return ( wind_saving
5190-5194 - climb_up - terminal_align - leave_current - 8.0 * unc - 6.0 * safety
5195 )
5198 anchor = int(round(clamp(max(z_now, floor) if goal_z <= z_now + 0.25 else max(z_now, goal_z, floor), search_min, max_level)))
5199-5201 if anchor not in level_here_idx: anchor = levels[0] best_z = float(anchor)
5202-5204 best_score = band_score(anchor) margin = 80.0 + 10.0 * max(0.0, 10.0 - horizontal_cells)
5205 guide_target: float | None = None
5206 if preferred_cruise_agl is not None and horizontal_cells > 6.0:
5207 guide_target = clamp(float(preferred_cruise_agl), float(search_min), float(max_level))
5208 margin = 35.0 + 6.0 * max(0.0, 10.0 - horizontal_cells)
5209 tw_now = band_tailwind(anchor)
5210-5212 for level in levels: if level == anchor: continue
5213 score = band_score(level)
5214 if level > z_now + 0.4 and band_tailwind(level) < tw_now - 0.45:
5215-5216 if guide_target is None or abs(float(level) - guide_target) > 0.6: continue
5217 level_margin = margin
5218 if guide_target is not None and abs(float(level) - guide_target) <= 0.6:
5219-5220 level_margin = min(level_margin, 20.0) score += 45.0
5221-5222 if score > best_score + level_margin: best_score = score
5223 best_z = float(level)
5225-5226 if guide_target is not None: target = float(guide_target)
5227 target_level = int(round(clamp(target, search_min, max_level)))
5228-5229 if target_level in level_here_idx: target_score = band_score(target_level)
5230-5231 if target_score > best_score - 55.0: best_z = target
5234 if here["wind_w"] > 0.35 and best_z <= z_now + 0.2 and max_level >= search_min:
5235 up = int(min(max_level, math.floor(z_now) + 1))
5236 if up in level_here_idx and band_tailwind(up) >= tw_now - 0.2:
5237-5238 if band_score(up) > best_score - 40.0: best_z = max(best_z, float(up))
5240 if here["wind_w"] > 0.18 and horizontal_cells > 7.0 and tw_now >= -0.35:
5241 soft = min(float(max_level), max(z_now, floor) + 0.9)
5242 soft_i = int(clamp(round(soft), search_min, max_level))
5243 if soft_i in level_here_idx and band_tailwind(soft_i) >= tw_now - 0.2 and band_score(soft_i) > best_score - 50.0:
5244 best_z = max(best_z, min(soft, float(soft_i)))
5248 if horizontal_cells <= 8.0:
5249 max_recoverable = goal_z + max(0.4, (horizontal_cells - 0.5) / 2.0)
5250-5251 if horizontal_cells > 0.5: hold = floor
5252-5253 if preferred_cruise_agl is not None: hold = max(hold, float(preferred_cruise_agl))
5254 max_recoverable = max(max_recoverable, hold)
5255 best_z = min(best_z, max_recoverable)
5258 best_z = max(best_z, floor)
5259 climb_cap = 1.35 if guide_target is not None and guide_target > z_now + 0.4 else 1.0
5260 best_z = clamp(best_z, z_now - 1.0, z_now + climb_cap)
5261 best_z = clamp(best_z, float(search_min), float(max_level))
5262-5263 if goal_z > z_now + 0.25: best_z = max(best_z, min(goal_z, z_now + 1.1))
5264 return best_z
5267def goal_altitude_gap(point: State3D, mission: Mission) -> int:
5268 return normalize_state(mission.goal)[2] - point[2]
5271def _search_level_bounds(belief_map: BeliefMap, mission: Mission) -> tuple[int, int]:
5272-5274 if belief_map.levels <= 0: return 0, 0 min_level = max(0, mission.min_altitude_level)
5275 max_level = min(belief_map.levels - 1, mission.max_altitude_level)
5276-5277 if min_level > max_level: min_level = max_level
5278 return min_level, max_level
5281def _mission_terrain_dz(mission: Mission, x0: float, y0: float, x1: float, y1: float) -> float:

86 coarse_history: deque[tuple[float, float, float]] = field(default_factory=lambda: deque(maxlen=4))
87 cell_history_window: dict[tuple[int, int, int], deque[tuple[float, float, float]]] = field(default_factory=dict)
88 window_size: int = 4
90 def advance_header(self, timestamp: str, u_km: float, v_km: float, w_km: float = 0.0) -> None:
91-92 previous = self.prev_coarse self.timestamp = timestamp
93 self.current_coarse = (u_km, v_km, w_km)
94 self.prev_u_km = previous[0] if previous else u_km
95 self.prev_v_km = previous[1] if previous else v_km
96-97 self.prev_w_km = previous[2] if previous else w_km self.delta_u_km = u_km - self.prev_u_km
98-99 self.delta_v_km = v_km - self.prev_v_km self.delta_w_km = w_km - self.prev_w_km
100-101 self.prev_coarse = (u_km, v_km, w_km) self.coarse_history.append((u_km, v_km, w_km))
103 def temporal_features(self, x: int, y: int, z: int, u_local: float, v_local: float, w_local: float) -> dict[str, float]:
104 previous = self.cell_history.get((x, y, z))
105 prev_local_u = previous["u_local"] if previous else u_local
106 prev_local_v = previous["v_local"] if previous else v_local
107 prev_local_w = previous["w_local"] if previous else w_local
108 local_window = list(self.cell_history_window.get((x, y, z), deque()))
109 coarse_window = list(self.coarse_history)
110 coarse_u_values = [item[0] for item in coarse_window] or [self.current_coarse[0]]
111 coarse_v_values = [item[1] for item in coarse_window] or [self.current_coarse[1]]
112 coarse_w_values = [item[2] for item in coarse_window] or [self.current_coarse[2]]
113 local_u_values = [item[0] for item in local_window] or [prev_local_u]
114 local_v_values = [item[1] for item in local_window] or [prev_local_v]
115 local_w_values = [item[2] for item in local_window] or [prev_local_w]
116 prev2_coarse = coarse_window[-2] if len(coarse_window) >= 2 else self.current_coarse
117 prev3_coarse = coarse_window[-3] if len(coarse_window) >= 3 else prev2_coarse
118 prev2_local = local_window[-2] if len(local_window) >= 2 else (prev_local_u, prev_local_v, prev_local_w)
119 prev3_local = local_window[-3] if len(local_window) >= 3 else prev2_local
120 return {
121 "prev_u_km": getattr(self, "prev_u_km", getattr(self, "current_coarse", (0.0, 0.0, 0.0))[0]),
122 "prev_v_km": getattr(self, "prev_v_km", getattr(self, "current_coarse", (0.0, 0.0, 0.0))[1]),
123 "prev_w_km": getattr(self, "prev_w_km", getattr(self, "current_coarse", (0.0, 0.0, 0.0))[2]),
124 "delta_u_km": getattr(self, "delta_u_km", 0.0),
125 "delta_v_km": getattr(self, "delta_v_km", 0.0),
126-127 "delta_w_km": getattr(self, "delta_w_km", 0.0), "prev_local_u": prev_local_u,
128-129 "prev_local_v": prev_local_v, "prev_local_w": prev_local_w,
130 "prev_local_speed": magnitude3(prev_local_u, prev_local_v, prev_local_w),
131-132 "local_trend_u": u_local - prev_local_u, "local_trend_v": v_local - prev_local_v,
133-134 "local_trend_w": w_local - prev_local_w, "prev2_u_km": prev2_coarse[0],
135-136 "prev2_v_km": prev2_coarse[1], "prev2_w_km": prev2_coarse[2],
137-138 "prev3_u_km": prev3_coarse[0], "prev3_v_km": prev3_coarse[1],
139 "prev3_w_km": prev3_coarse[2],
140 "coarse_u_mean": sum(coarse_u_values) / len(coarse_u_values),
141 "coarse_v_mean": sum(coarse_v_values) / len(coarse_v_values),
142 "coarse_w_mean": sum(coarse_w_values) / len(coarse_w_values),
143-144 "prev2_local_u": prev2_local[0], "prev2_local_v": prev2_local[1],
145-146 "prev2_local_w": prev2_local[2], "prev3_local_u": prev3_local[0],
147-148 "prev3_local_v": prev3_local[1], "prev3_local_w": prev3_local[2],
149 "local_u_mean": sum(local_u_values) / len(local_u_values),
150 "local_v_mean": sum(local_v_values) / len(local_v_values),
151-152 "local_w_mean": sum(local_w_values) / len(local_w_values), }
154 def update_cell_history(self, u_grid, v_grid, w_grid, z: int) -> None:
155-156 u_arr = np.asarray(u_grid, dtype=np.float64) v_arr = np.asarray(v_grid, dtype=np.float64)
157 w_arr = np.asarray(w_grid, dtype=np.float64)
158-159 if u_arr.ndim != 2 or u_arr.size == 0: self.cell_history = {}
160 return
161-163 height, width = u_arr.shape z_i = int(z) next_history: dict = {}
164-165 windows = self.cell_history_window wsize = self.window_size
167-168 for y in range(height): u_row = u_arr[y]
169-170 v_row = v_arr[y] w_row = w_arr[y]
171-172 for x in range(width): uu = float(u_row[x])
173-176 vv = float(v_row[x]) ww = float(w_row[x]) key = (x, y, z_i) window = windows.get(key)
177-178 if window is None: window = deque(maxlen=wsize)
179 windows[key] = window
180 window.append((uu, vv, ww))
181 next_history[key] = {"u_local": uu, "v_local": vv, "w_local": ww}
182 self.cell_history = next_history
184 def observe_cell(self, x: int, y: int, z: int, u_local: float, v_local: float, w_local: float) -> None:
185-186 key = (x, y, z) window = self.cell_history_window.get(key)
187-188 if window is None: window = deque(maxlen=self.window_size)
189 self.cell_history_window[key] = window
190 window.append((u_local, v_local, w_local))
191 self.cell_history[key] = {"u_local": u_local, "v_local": v_local, "w_local": w_local}

226 def create_context(self, start: tuple[int, int] | tuple[int, int, int], goal: tuple[int, int] | tuple[int, int, int]) -> NavigationContext:
227 width = len(self.pipeline.terrain.elevation[0])

232-234 start=actual_start, goal=actual_goal, max_steps=self.config.mission.max_steps,
235-236 step_distance_m=self.config.mission.step_distance_m, home=actual_start,
237 max_return_cost_j=None,

239 hover_power_w=getattr(self.config.mission, "hover_power_w", 105.0),

243 climb_power_per_mps_w=getattr(self.config.mission, "climb_power_per_mps_w", 125.0),

258-260 z=float(actual_start[2]), heading_rad=0.0, battery_ratio=1.0,
261-262 airspeed=mission.nominal_airspeed, )

268 battery_j=battery_j,

270 predict_session=PredictSession(window_size=self.config.model.temporal_window_size, coarse_history=deque(maxlen=self.config.model.temporal_window_size)),
271-272 planned_path=[actual_start], )
