274 return context
276-279 def step( self, context: NavigationContext, sample: CoarseWindSample,
280-282 observation: Observation | None = None, truth_field: dict | None = None, ) -> dict:
283 context.step += 1
284 is_keyframe = context.step == 1 or context.step % max(1, self.config.simulation.report_keyframe_interval) == 0
285 prediction, physics = self._forecast_fields(context, sample, observation, is_keyframe)
286-287 self.updater.apply_prediction( context.belief_map,
288 WindField(u=prediction["u_layers"], v=prediction["v_layers"], w=prediction["w_layers"]),
289-290 context.step, )
291 observation_payload = None
292 if observation:
293 observation = self._clamp_observation(observation, context.belief_map.width, context.belief_map.height)
294-296 self.updater.update_with_observation( context.belief_map, observation,
297 prediction["u"][observation.y][observation.x],
298 prediction["v"][observation.y][observation.x],
299-301 prediction["w"][observation.y][observation.x], context.step, )
302-303 observation_payload = { "position": [observation.x, observation.y, observation.z],
304-306 "u_obs": observation.u_obs, "v_obs": observation.v_obs, "w_obs": observation.w_obs,
307-308 "airspeed": observation.airspeed, "ground_speed": observation.ground_speed,
309-311 "climb_rate": observation.climb_rate, "acceleration": observation.acceleration, }
314-319 self._apply_truth_probes( context, prediction, truth_field, timestamp=sample.timestamp, )
321-322 self._refresh_prior_via(context, truth_field) context.latest_truth_field = truth_field
324 self._replan(context)
325 step_energy_j = self._move_if_possible(context, prediction, truth_field)
326 frame = self._build_frame(context, sample.timestamp, physics, prediction, observation_payload, truth_field, is_keyframe)
327-328 frame["step_energy_j"] = step_energy_j context.trace.append(frame)
329 return frame
331 def _resolved_guide_cruise_agl(self, context: NavigationContext) -> float:
332 """Sticky preferred cruise, else parse from guide_straight_agl_* mode label."""
333 clearance = float(getattr(context.mission, "clearance_agl_level", 1.0))
334 preferred = getattr(context.mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None)
335-336 if preferred is not None: return float(preferred)
337 mode = str((context.latest_planning or {}).get("planning_mode") or "")
338 return float(_cruise_z_from_guide_label(mode, clearance, context.mission))
340 def _guide_cruise_target_z(self, context: NavigationContext, horizontal: float, total_horiz: float) -> float | None:
341 """Baseline-like AGL profile while locked to a straight/corridor energy guide."""
342 mode = str((context.latest_planning or {}).get("planning_mode") or "")
343 if not (mode.startswith("guide_straight_agl") or mode.startswith("guide_corridor_")):
344 return None
345 cruise = self._resolved_guide_cruise_agl(context)
346 goal_z = float(context.mission.goal[2]) if len(context.mission.goal) >= 3 else 0.0
347-348 if total_horiz < 1e-6: return goal_z
349 progress = clamp(1.0 - horizontal / total_horiz, 0.0, 1.0)
352-353 if progress < 0.12: return cruise
354-355 if progress > 0.85: blend = clamp((1.0 - progress) / 0.15, 0.0, 1.0)
356 return goal_z + (cruise - goal_z) * blend
357-358 if horizontal <= 0.5: return goal_z
359 return cruise
361-364 def _baseline_like_next_waypoint( self, context: NavigationContext, next_waypoint: tuple,
365-366 horizontal: float, ) -> tuple[float, float, float]:
367 """Apply baseline AGL profile; straight guides track the eval baseline polyline."""
368-369 goal = context.mission.goal gx, gy = float(goal[0]), float(goal[1])
370-371 sx, sy = context.state.x, context.state.y ms = context.mission.start
372 total_horiz = math.hypot(float(goal[0]) - float(ms[0]), float(goal[1]) - float(ms[1]))
373 total_horiz = max(total_horiz, horizontal, 1.0)
374 mode = str((context.latest_planning or {}).get("planning_mode") or "")
375 cruise = self._resolved_guide_cruise_agl(context)
377-380 if mode.startswith("guide_straight_agl"): start_xyz = ( float(ms[0]), float(ms[1]),
381-382 float(ms[2]) if len(ms) > 2 else 0.0, )
383-387 goal_xyz = ( float(goal[0]), float(goal[1]), float(goal[2]) if len(goal) > 2 else 0.0, )
390 dx0, dy0 = goal_xyz[0] - start_xyz[0], goal_xyz[1] - start_xyz[1]
391-392 span = math.hypot(dx0, dy0) or 1.0 ux, uy = dx0 / span, dy0 / span
393 cross = abs((sx - start_xyz[0]) * (-uy) + (sy - start_xyz[1]) * ux)
394-395 if cross > 0.35: start_xyz = (sx, sy, float(context.state.z))
396 pts = straight_agl_guide_polyline(start_xyz, goal_xyz, agl_cruise=cruise)
397 nxt = next_polyline_waypoint(pts, sx, sy, ahead_cells=0.02)
398-399 if nxt is None: return (gx, gy, goal_xyz[2])
400-401 return ( clamp(nxt[0], 0.0, context.belief_map.width - 1),
402 clamp(nxt[1], 0.0, context.belief_map.height - 1),
403 clamp(nxt[2], float(context.mission.min_altitude_level), float(context.mission.max_altitude_level)),
404 )
406 target_z = self._guide_cruise_target_z(context, horizontal, total_horiz)
407 if target_z is None:
408 return (float(next_waypoint[0]), float(next_waypoint[1]), float(next_waypoint[2]))
410-411 tx, ty = float(next_waypoint[0]), float(next_waypoint[1]) dx, dy = tx - sx, ty - sy
412-413 dist = math.hypot(dx, dy) step = min(1.0, dist) if dist > 1e-6 else 0.0
414-415 if dist > 1e-6: nx = sx + dx / dist * step
416 ny = sy + dy / dist * step
417-422 else: nx, ny = tx, ty progress = clamp(1.0 - horizontal / total_horiz, 0.0, 1.0)
423 clearance = float(getattr(context.mission, "clearance_agl_level", 1.0))
424 max_up = (0.5 * clearance + 0.05) if progress < 0.12 else 1.2
425 z = context.state.z + clamp(target_z - context.state.z, -1.0, max_up)
426 z = clamp(z, float(context.mission.min_altitude_level), float(context.mission.max_altitude_level))
427 return (nx, ny, z)

490 def return_home_radius_cells(self, context: NavigationContext) -> float:
491-494 nominal_step_energy = max( 1.0, transition_energy_j( airspeed=context.state.airspeed,
495-499 current=(0, 0, 0), nxt=(1, 0, 0), local_u=0.0, local_v=0.0, local_w=0.0,
500 step_distance_m=context.mission.step_distance_m,
501 altitude_step_m=context.mission.altitude_step_m,
502 climb_cost_per_level_j=context.mission.climb_cost_per_level_j,
503 hover_power_w=context.mission.hover_power_w,
504 cruise_power_w=context.mission.cruise_power_w,
505 hotel_power_w=context.mission.hotel_power_w,
506 headwind_power_per_mps_w=context.mission.headwind_power_per_mps_w,
507 climb_power_per_mps_w=context.mission.climb_power_per_mps_w,
508-509 descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w=context.mission.descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w, ),
510-511 ) reserve = context.mission.reserve_energy_ratio * self.config.mission.battery_capacity_j
512 usable = max(0.0, context.battery_j - reserve)
513 return usable / nominal_step_energy
515 def return_home_budget_j(self, context: NavigationContext) -> float:
516 reserve = context.mission.reserve_energy_ratio * self.config.mission.battery_capacity_j
517 return max(0.0, context.battery_j - reserve)
519 def _replan(self, context: NavigationContext) -> None:
520 if context.latest_planning and context.step > 1:
521 interval = max(1, self.config.planner.replan_interval_steps)
522-523 if context.step % interval != 0 and len(context.planned_path) > 1: return
524-525 context.mission.max_return_cost_j = self.return_home_budget_j(context) horiz = math.hypot(

527-528 float(context.mission.goal[1]) - context.state.y, )

587-589 return_cost_map_cache=cached, truth_field=getattr(context, "latest_truth_field", None), )
590 context.mission.guide_via = getattr(plan_mission, "guide_via", None)
591 context.mission.launch_along_mps = getattr(plan_mission, "launch_along_mps", None)
592 context.mission.guide_straight_streak = int(
593 getattr(plan_mission, "guide_straight_streak", 0) or 0
594-596 ) new_pref = getattr(plan_mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None)
597 new_earned = bool(getattr(plan_mission, "cruise_climb_earned", False))
598 old_pref = getattr(context.mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None)
599 old_earned = bool(getattr(context.mission, "cruise_climb_earned", False))
600 clearance = float(getattr(context.mission, "clearance_agl_level", 1.0))
604-605 goal = context.mission.goal ms = context.mission.start
606 horiz = math.hypot(float(goal[0]) - context.state.x, float(goal[1]) - context.state.y)
607 total = math.hypot(float(goal[0]) - float(ms[0]), float(goal[1]) - float(ms[1]))
608-609 total = max(total, horiz, 1.0) progress = clamp(1.0 - horiz / total, 0.0, 1.0)
610-611 freeze_high = ( old_earned
612-613 and old_pref is not None and float(old_pref) > clearance + 1.0 + 1e-9
614-615 and 0.50 <= progress < 0.95 and horiz > 0.5
616 )
617 if freeze_high and (new_pref is None or float(new_pref) < float(old_pref) - 1e-9):
618 context.mission.preferred_cruise_agl = float(old_pref)
619 context.mission.cruise_climb_earned = True
620-621 else: context.mission.preferred_cruise_agl = new_pref
622 context.mission.cruise_climb_earned = new_earned
623-624 context.latest_planning = planning fresh_map = planning.get("return_cost_map")
625-626 if fresh_map is not None: context.return_cost_map_cache = fresh_map

628 context.planned_path = planning["path"]

632-633 if ( cruise_now is not None
634 and bool(getattr(context.mission, "cruise_climb_earned", False))
635-637 and float(cruise_now) > clearance + 1.0 + 1e-9 and progress >= 0.85 and horiz > 0.5
638-643 ): start_xyz = ( float(ms[0]), float(ms[1]), float(ms[2]) if len(ms) > 2 else 0.0, )
644-648 goal_xyz = ( float(goal[0]), float(goal[1]), float(goal[2]) if len(goal) > 2 else 0.0, )
649 pts = straight_agl_guide_polyline(start_xyz, goal_xyz, agl_cruise=float(cruise_now))
650 nxt = next_polyline_waypoint(pts, context.state.x, context.state.y, ahead_cells=0.02)
651-652 if nxt is not None: context.planned_path = [
653-654 (context.state.x, context.state.y, context.state.z), (
655 clamp(nxt[0], 0.0, context.belief_map.width - 1),
656-658 clamp(nxt[1], 0.0, context.belief_map.height - 1), clamp( nxt[2],
659-660 float(context.mission.min_altitude_level), float(context.mission.max_altitude_level),
661-663 ), ), ]

666-667 **(context.latest_planning or {}), "planning_mode": f"guide_straight_agl_{token}",
668-669 "path": context.planned_path, }

672 def _ensure_goal_approach(self, context: NavigationContext) -> list[tuple[float, float, float] | tuple[int, int, int]]:
673-674 path = list(context.planned_path or []) goal = context.mission.goal
675-676 gx, gy = float(goal[0]), float(goal[1]) gz = float(goal[2]) if len(goal) >= 3 else 0.0
677 sx, sy, sz = context.state.x, context.state.y, context.state.z
678 horizontal = math.hypot(gx - sx, gy - sy)
679-682 if horizontal > 3.5 or abs(sz - gz) <= 0.45: return path dz = clamp(gz - sz, -1.2, 1.2)
683-684 if horizontal > 1e-6: step = min(1.0, horizontal)
685-686 nx = sx + (gx - sx) / horizontal * step ny = sy + (gy - sy) / horizontal * step
687-691 else: nx, ny = sx, sy nxt = ( nx, ny,
692 clamp(sz + dz, float(context.mission.min_altitude_level), float(context.mission.max_altitude_level)),
693 )
694 return [(sx, sy, sz), nxt]
696-699 def _move_if_possible( self, context: NavigationContext, prediction: dict,
700-701 truth_field: dict | None = None, ) -> float:
702-703 if len(context.planned_path) <= 1 or context.battery_j <= 0.0: return 0.0
704-705 next_waypoint = context.planned_path[1] goal = context.mission.goal
706 horizontal = math.hypot(float(goal[0]) - context.state.x, float(goal[1]) - context.state.y)
709 next_waypoint = self._baseline_like_next_waypoint(context, next_waypoint, horizontal)
711 preferred = getattr(context.mission, "preferred_cruise_agl", None)
712 ms = context.mission.start
713 total_horiz = math.hypot(float(goal[0]) - float(ms[0]), float(goal[1]) - float(ms[1]))
714 total_horiz = max(total_horiz, horizontal, 1.0)
715 progress = clamp(1.0 - horizontal / total_horiz, 0.0, 1.0)
719 mode_now = str((context.latest_planning or {}).get("planning_mode") or "")
720-721 if ( preferred is not None
722-723 and 0.12 <= progress <= 0.85 and horizontal > 0.5
724 and not mode_now.startswith("guide_straight_agl")
725-730 ): next_waypoint = ( float(next_waypoint[0]), float(next_waypoint[1]), float(preferred), )

732 local_v = trilinear_sample(prediction["v_layers"], context.state.x, context.state.y, context.state.z)
733 local_w = trilinear_sample(prediction["w_layers"], context.state.x, context.state.y, context.state.z)
734 safety_penalty = _sample_belief_scalar(context.belief_map, "safety_penalty", context.state.x, context.state.y, context.state.z)
735-736 prev = (context.state.x, context.state.y, context.state.z) floor = cruise_agl_floor(
737-738 horizontal, float(context.mission.min_altitude_level),
739-740 float(getattr(context.mission, "clearance_agl_level", 1.0)), )
741 min_z = max(float(context.mission.min_altitude_level), floor if horizontal > 5.0 else float(context.mission.min_altitude_level))
742 mode = str((context.latest_planning or {}).get("planning_mode") or "")
743 if mode.startswith("guide_straight_agl") or mode.startswith("guide_corridor_"):
745-746 heading = ( context.state.heading_rad
747 if horizontal < 1e-6
748 else math.atan2(float(next_waypoint[1]) - context.state.y, float(next_waypoint[0]) - context.state.x)
749 )

752 z_lo = float(context.mission.min_altitude_level)
753-755 else: z_lo = float(min_z) next_state = DroneState(
756 x=clamp(float(next_waypoint[0]), 0.0, context.belief_map.width - 1),

759-760 heading_rad=heading, battery_ratio=context.state.battery_ratio,
761-762 airspeed=context.state.airspeed, )
763-766 else: next_state = advance_continuous_state( context.state, next_waypoint,
767-770 local_u=local_u, local_v=local_v, local_w=local_w, safety_penalty=safety_penalty,
771 step_distance_m=context.mission.step_distance_m,
772 altitude_step_m=context.mission.altitude_step_m,
773 min_altitude_level=int(math.floor(min_z)),
774-775 max_altitude_level=context.mission.max_altitude_level, )
778 if truth_field is not None and "u" in truth_field:
779 energy_u = trilinear_sample(truth_field["u"], prev[0], prev[1], prev[2])
780 energy_v = trilinear_sample(truth_field["v"], prev[0], prev[1], prev[2])
781 energy_w = trilinear_sample(truth_field["w"], prev[0], prev[1], prev[2])
782-783 else: energy_u, energy_v, energy_w = local_u, local_v, local_w
784-788 terrain_dz_m = terrain_delta_m( context.terrain.elevation, prev[0], prev[1], next_state.x,
789-790 next_state.y, )
792-794 step_headwind = horizontal_headwind_mps( prev, (next_state.x, next_state.y, next_state.z),
795-797 energy_u, energy_v, )
798 nominal_airspeed = float(getattr(context.mission, "nominal_airspeed", context.state.airspeed))
799 proposed_airspeed = speed_to_fly_airspeed(nominal_airspeed, step_headwind, energy_w)
800-802 nxt_xyz = (next_state.x, next_state.y, next_state.z) energy_kw = dict( current=prev,
803-806 nxt=nxt_xyz, local_u=energy_u, local_v=energy_v, local_w=energy_w,
807 step_distance_m=context.mission.step_distance_m,
808 altitude_step_m=context.mission.altitude_step_m,
809 climb_cost_per_level_j=context.mission.climb_cost_per_level_j,
810 hover_power_w=context.mission.hover_power_w,
811 cruise_power_w=context.mission.cruise_power_w,
812 hotel_power_w=context.mission.hotel_power_w,
813 headwind_power_per_mps_w=context.mission.headwind_power_per_mps_w,
814 climb_power_per_mps_w=context.mission.climb_power_per_mps_w,
815 descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w=context.mission.descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w,
816-817 terrain_dz_m=terrain_dz_m, )
818 e_proposed = transition_energy_j(airspeed=proposed_airspeed, **energy_kw)
819-820 step_airspeed = proposed_airspeed required_energy = e_proposed
821 if proposed_airspeed > nominal_airspeed + 1e-6:
822 e_nominal = transition_energy_j(airspeed=nominal_airspeed, **energy_kw)
823-824 if e_proposed > e_nominal: step_airspeed = nominal_airspeed
825 required_energy = e_nominal
826-829 next_state = DroneState( x=next_state.x, y=next_state.y, z=next_state.z,
830-831 heading_rad=next_state.heading_rad, battery_ratio=next_state.battery_ratio,
832-833 airspeed=step_airspeed, )
835-836 if required_energy > context.battery_j: context.planned_path = [context.planned_path[0]]
837 return 0.0
838 context.battery_j = max(0.0, context.battery_j - required_energy)
839 context.state = next_state
840 context.state.battery_ratio = context.battery_j / max(self.config.mission.battery_capacity_j, 1e-6)
841 if _state_near_waypoint(context.state, next_waypoint):
842 context.planned_path = context.planned_path[1:]

928-931 def _planning_window( self, context: NavigationContext, observation: Observation | None,
932-933 ) -> tuple[int, int, int, int]: width = context.belief_map.width
934 height = context.belief_map.height
935-936 radius = max(4, self.config.belief.observation_radius + 2) points = [
937 (int(round(context.state.x)), int(round(context.state.y))),
938-939 (context.mission.goal[0], context.mission.goal[1]), ]
940-941 if observation is not None: points.append((observation.x, observation.y))
942 for waypoint in context.planned_path[: min(len(context.planned_path), 6)]:
943 points.append((waypoint[0], waypoint[1]))
944-945 x_values = [point[0] for point in points] y_values = [point[1] for point in points]
946 x0 = max(0, int(math.floor(min(x_values) - radius)))
947 y0 = max(0, int(math.floor(min(y_values) - radius)))
948 x1 = min(width, int(math.ceil(max(x_values) + radius + 1)))
949 y1 = min(height, int(math.ceil(max(y_values) + radius + 1)))
950 return x0, y0, x1, y1

1109-1110 ), "return_home_radius_cells": self.return_home_radius_cells(context),
1111 "return_home_budget_j": context.mission.max_return_cost_j,
1112 "planned_path": context.planned_path,
1113 "planned_path_cost": context.latest_planning["path_cost"],
1114 "planned_path_cost_breakdown": context.latest_planning.get("path_cost_breakdown", {}),
1115 "candidate_paths": context.latest_planning["candidates"],
1116 "planning_mode": context.latest_planning.get("planning_mode", "mpc_strict_return"),
1117 "return_constraint_active": context.latest_planning.get("return_constraint_active", True),
1118 "is_keyframe": is_keyframe,
1119 "forecast_mode": "global_keyframe" if is_keyframe else "local_window",
1120-1122 "map_delta": map_delta, "sensor_packet": { "gps_position": [state.x, state.y, state.z],
1123 "heading_rad": state.heading_rad,
1124 "airspeed": state.airspeed if not observation_payload else observation_payload["airspeed"],
1125 "ground_speed": max(0.0, state.airspeed + current_u * math.cos(state.heading_rad) + current_v * math.sin(state.heading_rad))
1126-1127 if not observation_payload else observation_payload["ground_speed"],
1128 "imu_accel_longitudinal": 0.08 * magnitude(current_u, current_v) - 0.03 * belief_uncertainty,
1129 "imu_accel_vertical": 0.12 * belief_energy + 0.18 * current_w,
1130 "measured_wind_u": observation_payload["u_obs"] if observation_payload else current_u + 0.35 * (belief_confidence - 0.5),
1131 "measured_wind_v": observation_payload["v_obs"] if observation_payload else current_v - 0.25 * (belief_uncertainty - 0.5),
1132 "measured_wind_w": observation_payload["w_obs"] if observation_payload else current_w + 0.2 * (belief_confidence - 0.5),
1133-1134 "energy_rate": belief_energy, "altitude_m": state.z * context.mission.altitude_step_m,
1135-1136 "agl_m": state.z * context.mission.altitude_step_m, "msl_m": msl_height_m(
1137-1138 sample_elevation(context.terrain.elevation, state.x, state.y), state.z,
1139-1140 context.mission.altitude_step_m, ),
1141-1142 "terrain_elev_m": sample_elevation(context.terrain.elevation, state.x, state.y), },

1144 "prediction_at_drone": {"u": current_u, "v": current_v, "w": current_w, "speed": magnitude3(current_u, current_v, current_w)},

1157-1159 "altitude_level": state_level, }, "observation": observation_payload,

1164-1167 def _apply_truth_probes( self, context: NavigationContext, prediction: dict,
1168-1171 truth_field: dict | None, *, timestamp: str, ) -> list[dict] | None:
1172 """Fuse truth at uncertain ±path-normal cells (cruise-band safe).
1174 On-track observations stay the sole cruise-band signal: probes use
1175 observation_radius=0 and skip advection so on-ray wind_* is unchanged.
1176-1177 Extra gates: ambient hard floor, lateral shear, high belief uncertainty. """
1178-1179 if truth_field is None or "u" not in truth_field or "v" not in truth_field: return None
1180 replan_iv = max(1, int(getattr(self.config.planner, "replan_interval_steps", 2) or 2))
1181 interval = max(LATERAL_PROBE_MIN_INTERVAL_STEPS, replan_iv)
1182-1183 if context.step > 1 and context.step % interval != 0: return None
1184 res_m = float(getattr(context.mission, "step_distance_m", 30.0) or 30.0)
1185 offsets_m = LATERAL_PROBE_OFFSETS_M or (LATERAL_PROBE_OFFSET_M,)
1186 offset_cells_list = [float(o) / max(res_m, 1e-6) for o in offsets_m]
1187 offset_cells_list = [c for c in offset_cells_list if c >= 0.5]
1188-1190 if not offset_cells_list: return None sx = float(context.state.x)
1191-1192 sy = float(context.state.y) sz = float(context.state.z)
1193 cu = trilinear_sample(truth_field["u"], sx, sy, sz)
1194 cv = trilinear_sample(truth_field["v"], sx, sy, sz)
1195-1196 if magnitude(float(cu), float(cv)) < CORRIDOR_HARD_FLOOR_MPS: return None
1197-1199 goal = context.mission.goal dx = float(goal[0]) - sx dy = float(goal[1]) - sy
1200 span = math.hypot(dx, dy)
1201-1202 if span > 1e-6: hx, hy = dx / span, dy / span
1203-1204 else: hx = math.cos(float(context.state.heading_rad))
1205 hy = math.sin(float(context.state.heading_rad))
1206-1207 if abs(hx) + abs(hy) < 1e-9: hx, hy = 1.0, 0.0
1208-1210 nx, ny = -hy, hx width = context.belief_map.width height = context.belief_map.height
1211 z_level = int(round(clamp(sz, 0.0, float(max(context.belief_map.levels - 1, 0)))))
1212-1213 u_layers = prediction.get("u_layers") v_layers = prediction.get("v_layers")
1214 w_layers = prediction.get("w_layers")
1215-1216 if u_layers is None or v_layers is None or w_layers is None: return None
1217-1218 truth_w = truth_field.get("w") center_speed = magnitude(float(cu), float(cv))
1219 arrays = context.belief_map.field_arrays
1220 site_uv: list[tuple[str, int, int, float, float, float]] = []
1221-1222 speeds: list[float] = [center_speed] seen_cells: set[tuple[int, int]] = set()
1223-1224 ox, oy = int(round(sx)), int(round(sy)) seen_cells.add((ox, oy))
1225 lookahads = LATERAL_PROBE_LOOKAHEAD_CELLS or (0.0,)
1226-1227 for la in lookahads: cx = sx + hx * float(la)
1228 cy = sy + hy * float(la)
1229-1230 for off_cells in offset_cells_list: left = (cx + nx * off_cells, cy + ny * off_cells)
1231 right = (cx - nx * off_cells, cy - ny * off_cells)
1232 for tag, px, py in (("left", *left), ("right", *right)):
1233 ix = int(round(clamp(px, 0.0, float(max(width - 1, 0)))))
1234 iy = int(round(clamp(py, 0.0, float(max(height - 1, 0)))))
1235-1237 if (ix, iy) in seen_cells: continue unc = 1.0
1238 if arrays is not None and "uncertainty" in arrays:
1239 unc = float(arrays["uncertainty"][z_level, iy, ix])
1240-1241 if unc < LATERAL_PROBE_MIN_UNCERTAINTY: continue
1242 tu = float(trilinear_sample(truth_field["u"], float(ix), float(iy), sz))
1243-1244 tv = float(trilinear_sample(truth_field["v"], float(ix), float(iy), sz)) tw = (
1245 float(trilinear_sample(truth_w, float(ix), float(iy), sz))
1246-1247 if truth_w is not None else 0.0
1248-1250 ) site_uv.append((tag, ix, iy, tu, tv, tw)) speeds.append(magnitude(tu, tv))
1251 seen_cells.add((ix, iy))
1252-1254 if len(site_uv) < 1 or len(speeds) < 2: return None shear = max(speeds) - min(speeds)
1255-1256 if shear < LATERAL_PROBE_MIN_SHEAR_MPS: return None
1257-1258 if shear < LATERAL_PROBE_MIN_SHEAR_FRAC * max(center_speed, 1e-6): return None
1259-1260 noise = LATERAL_PROBE_NOISE_MPS payload: list[dict] = []
1261 for i, (tag, ix, iy, tu, tv, tw) in enumerate(site_uv):
1262-1263 sign = 1.0 if (context.step + i) % 2 == 0 else -1.0 obs = Observation(
1264-1268 timestamp=timestamp, x=ix, y=iy, z=z_level, u_obs=float(tu) + sign * noise,
1269-1271 v_obs=float(tv) - sign * noise, w_obs=float(tw), )
1272 pred_u = trilinear_sample(u_layers, float(ix), float(iy), sz)
1273 pred_v = trilinear_sample(v_layers, float(ix), float(iy), sz)
1274 pred_w = trilinear_sample(w_layers, float(ix), float(iy), sz)
1275-1278 self.updater.update_with_observation( context.belief_map, obs, float(pred_u),
1279-1284 float(pred_v), float(pred_w), context.step, observation_radius=0, advect=False, )
1285-1289 payload.append( { "tag": tag, "position": [ix, iy, z_level], "u_obs": obs.u_obs,
1290-1292 "v_obs": obs.v_obs, "w_obs": obs.w_obs, "shear_mps": shear,
1293-1294 "on_track_cell": [ox, oy, z_level], }
1295 )
1296 return payload or None
1298 def _refresh_prior_via(self, context: NavigationContext, truth_field: dict | None) -> None:

1300-1301 if truth_field is None or "u" not in truth_field or "v" not in truth_field: return
1302-1303 if context.step > PRIOR_VIA_MAX_STEP: return

1329 interval = max(PRIOR_VIA_REFRESH_STEPS, int(getattr(self.config.planner, "replan_interval_steps", 2) or 2))
1330-1331 if context.step > 1 and context.step % interval != 0: return
1332 cruise_z = float(cruise_z_probe)

1366-1371 via = distill_truth_1via( start, goal, context.belief_map, context.mission, truth_field,
1372-1373 cruise_z=float(cruise_z), offsets_m=PRIOR_VIA_OFFSETS_M,
1374-1376 min_truth_save=PRIOR_VIA_MIN_TRUTH_SAVE, min_belief_save=PRIOR_VIA_MIN_BELIEF_SAVE, )
1380-1381 if via is None: via = via_truth
1382 context.mission.prior_via = via
1384-1387 def _paint_truth_via_seed( self, context: NavigationContext, truth_field: dict,
1388-1391 via_xy: tuple[float, float], *, cruise_z: float, ) -> int:
1392 """Sparse radius=0 truth fuse along start→via→goal (no advection)."""
1393-1394 path = _agl_guide_polyline( (context.state.x, context.state.y, context.state.z),
1395-1397 context.mission.goal, context.belief_map, context.mission,
1398-1400 via_xy=(float(via_xy[0]), float(via_xy[1])), cruise_z=float(cruise_z), )
1401-1403 if len(path) < 3: return 0 arrays = context.belief_map.field_arrays
1404-1406 truth_w = truth_field.get("w") painted = 0 stride = max(1, int(PRIOR_VIA_TEACH_STRIDE))
1408 idxs = list(range(1, len(path) - 1, stride))
1409-1411 for idx in idxs: if painted >= PRIOR_VIA_TEACH_MAX_CELLS: break
1412 x, y, z = path[idx]
1413 ix = int(round(clamp(float(x), 0.0, float(max(context.belief_map.width - 1, 0)))))
1414 iy = int(round(clamp(float(y), 0.0, float(max(context.belief_map.height - 1, 0)))))
1415 iz = int(round(clamp(float(z), 0.0, float(max(context.belief_map.levels - 1, 0)))))
1416 if arrays is not None and "uncertainty" in arrays:
1417 unc = float(arrays["uncertainty"][iz, iy, ix])
1418-1419 if unc < PRIOR_VIA_TEACH_MIN_UNCERTAINTY: continue

1421-1422 tv = float(trilinear_sample(truth_field["v"], float(ix), float(iy), float(iz))) tw = (
1423 float(trilinear_sample(truth_w, float(ix), float(iy), float(iz)))
1424-1425 if truth_w is not None else 0.0
1426-1431 ) obs = Observation( timestamp=f"teach-{context.step}-{painted}", x=ix, y=iy, z=iz,
1432-1435 u_obs=tu, v_obs=tv, w_obs=tw, )
1436-1442 self.updater.update_with_observation( context.belief_map, obs, tu, tv, tw, context.step,
1443-1445 observation_radius=0, advect=False, )
1446 painted += 1
1447 return painted

1473def _merge_window_into_grid(base_grid: dict | None, window_grid: dict) -> dict:
1474 """Paste a local predict/physics window into a full grid (numpy slice assign)."""
1475-1476 if base_grid is None: u_layers = np.asarray(window_grid["u_layers"], dtype=np.float64)

1545-1548def _clamp_xyz( point: tuple[int, int] | tuple[int, int, int], width: int, height: int,

1558-1560def _state_level(z: float, levels: int) -> int: if levels <= 0: return 0

1564-1565def _round_state_value(value: float) -> float: return round(float(value), 3)
1568def _goal_reached(state: DroneState, goal: tuple[int, int] | tuple[int, int, int]) -> bool:
1569-1571 gx = float(goal[0]) gy = float(goal[1]) gz = float(goal[2]) if len(goal) >= 3 else 0.0

1575def _state_near_waypoint(state: DroneState, waypoint: tuple[int, int, int]) -> bool:

1579def _sample_belief_field_scalar(belief_map, attr: str, x: float, y: float, z: float) -> float:
1580 """Sample prediction-updated belief fields for telemetry / report logging."""
1581 arrays = getattr(belief_map, "field_arrays", None)
1582 if arrays is not None and attr in arrays:
1583 return float(trilinear_sample(arrays[attr], x, y, z))
1584 return _sample_belief_scalar(belief_map, attr, x, y, z)
1587def _sample_belief_scalar(belief_map, attr: str, x: float, y: float, z: float) -> float:
1588-1589 if belief_map.levels <= 0 or belief_map.height <= 0 or belief_map.width <= 0: return 0.0
1593-1594 z = clamp(z, 0.0, belief_map.levels - 1) z0 = int(math.floor(z))
1595-1596 z1 = min(z0 + 1, belief_map.levels - 1) tz = z - z0
1597 lower = _sample_belief_scalar_2d(belief_map, attr, x, y, z0)
1598 upper = _sample_belief_scalar_2d(belief_map, attr, x, y, z1)
1599 return lower + (upper - lower) * tz
1602def _sample_belief_scalar_2d(belief_map, attr: str, x: float, y: float, level: int) -> float:
1603-1604 x = clamp(x, 0.0, belief_map.width - 1) y = clamp(y, 0.0, belief_map.height - 1)
1605-1607 x0 = int(math.floor(x)) y0 = int(math.floor(y)) x1 = min(x0 + 1, belief_map.width - 1)
1608-1610 y1 = min(y0 + 1, belief_map.height - 1) tx = x - x0 ty = y - y0
1611 v00 = getattr(belief_map.cells[level][y0][x0], attr)
1612 v10 = getattr(belief_map.cells[level][y0][x1], attr)
1613 v01 = getattr(belief_map.cells[level][y1][x0], attr)
1614-1615 v11 = getattr(belief_map.cells[level][y1][x1], attr) top = v00 + (v10 - v00) * tx
1616 bottom = v01 + (v11 - v01) * tx
1617 return top + (bottom - top) * ty
1620def _wind_speed_grid(u_grid, v_grid, w_grid=None) -> list[list[float]]:
1621-1622 u_arr = np.asarray(u_grid, dtype=np.float64) v_arr = np.asarray(v_grid, dtype=np.float64)
1623-1625 if u_arr.ndim != 2 or u_arr.size == 0: return [] height, width = u_arr.shape
1626-1627 if w_grid is None: w_arr = np.zeros((height, width), dtype=np.float64)
1628-1629 else: w_arr = np.asarray(w_grid, dtype=np.float64)
1630 speed = np.sqrt(u_arr * u_arr + v_arr * v_arr + w_arr * w_arr)
1631 return speed.tolist()
1634def _residual_speed_grid(u_grid, v_grid, w_grid, truth_field: dict) -> list[list[float]]:

1646 tu = np.asarray(truth_field["u"], dtype=np.float64)
1647 tv = np.asarray(truth_field["v"], dtype=np.float64)
1648 pred = np.sqrt(u_arr * u_arr + v_arr * v_arr + w_arr * w_arr)
1649 truth = np.sqrt(tu * tu + tv * tv + tw * tw)
1650 return np.abs(pred - truth).tolist()
1653def _return_cost_margin_grid(return_cost_map: dict[tuple[int, int, int], float], budget_j: float, mission: Mission) -> list[list[float]]:
1654 width = max((state[0] for state in return_cost_map), default=-1) + 1
1655 height = max((state[1] for state in return_cost_map), default=-1) + 1
1656 layer = tuple(mission.goal)[2]
1657 grid = [[-budget_j for _ in range(width)] for _ in range(height)]
1658-1659 for (x, y, z), value in return_cost_map.items(): if z == layer:
1660 grid[y][x] = budget_j - value if math.isfinite(value) else -budget_j
1661 return grid
1664def _return_feasible_mask_grid(return_cost_map: dict[tuple[int, int, int], float], budget_j: float, mission: Mission) -> list[list[float]]:
1665 width = max((state[0] for state in return_cost_map), default=-1) + 1
1666 height = max((state[1] for state in return_cost_map), default=-1) + 1
1667-1668 layer = tuple(mission.goal)[2] grid = [[0.0 for _ in range(width)] for _ in range(height)]
1669-1670 for (x, y, z), value in return_cost_map.items(): if z == layer and value <= budget_j:
1671 grid[y][x] = 1.0
1672 return grid
1675def truth_field_from_payload(payload: dict) -> dict:
1676 return {"u": payload["u_grid"], "v": payload["v_grid"], "w": payload.get("w_grid")}

1from __future__ import annotations

12@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
13-14class FlightEnvelope: min_airspeed: float = 8.5
15-16 max_airspeed: float = 22.0 best_glide_speed: float = 13.8
17-18 best_thermalling_speed: float = 10.5 max_bank_rad: float = math.radians(42.0)
19-20 max_turn_rate_rad_s: float = math.radians(24.0) base_sink_mps: float = 0.58

25DEFAULT_ENVELOPE = FlightEnvelope()
28def uplift_energy_scale(local_w: float) -> float:
29 """Shared planning/execution discount for usable vertical lift (unitless)."""
30 return max(0.5, min(1.4, 1.0 - 0.20 * max(float(local_w), 0.0)))
37-38SPEED_TO_FLY_HEADWIND_GAIN = 0.85 SPEED_TO_FLY_HEADWIND_MIN_MPS = 0.55
40-41SPEED_TO_FLY_LIFT_EASE = 0.35 SPEED_TO_FLY_LIFT_HEADWIND_MAX_MPS = 0.25
44-45def horizontal_headwind_mps( current: tuple[float, float, float],
46-49 nxt: tuple[float, float, float], local_u: float, local_v: float, ) -> float:
50 """Along-track opposing wind only (no vertical mix-in) — used for speed-to-fly."""
51-52 move_dx = float(nxt[0]) - float(current[0]) move_dy = float(nxt[1]) - float(current[1])
53 move_norm = max(math.hypot(move_dx, move_dy), 1e-6)
54 along = (float(local_u) * move_dx + float(local_v) * move_dy) / move_norm
55 return max(0.0, -along)
58-60def speed_to_fly_airspeed( nominal_airspeed: float, headwind_mps: float,
61-63 local_w: float = 0.0, envelope: FlightEnvelope = DEFAULT_ENVELOPE, ) -> float:
64 """MacCready-style airspeed proposal: push into headwind; mild ease in calm lift.
66 Callers that charge `path_model` should energy-gate the proposal (see execution):
67-68 keep nominal when the boosted step is not cheaper under `transition_energy_j`. """
69-71 base = float(nominal_airspeed) head = max(0.0, float(headwind_mps)) boost = 0.0
72-73 if head >= SPEED_TO_FLY_HEADWIND_MIN_MPS: boost = SPEED_TO_FLY_HEADWIND_GAIN * head
74 ease = 0.0
75 if head <= SPEED_TO_FLY_LIFT_HEADWIND_MAX_MPS:
76 ease = SPEED_TO_FLY_LIFT_EASE * max(0.0, float(local_w) - 0.30)
77 return clamp_airspeed(base + boost - ease, envelope)
80def compute_heading(current: tuple[float, float, float], nxt: tuple[float, float, float]) -> float:
81-82 dx = nxt[0] - current[0] dy = nxt[1] - current[1]
83 return math.atan2(dy, dx)

97-98def transition_wind_metrics( current: tuple[float, float, float],
99-102 nxt: tuple[float, float, float], local_u: float, local_v: float, local_w: float,
103-104) -> tuple[float, float, float]: move_dx = nxt[0] - current[0]
105-106 move_dy = nxt[1] - current[1] move_dz = nxt[2] - current[2]
107 move_norm = max(math.hypot(move_dx, move_dy), 1e-6)
108 headwind = -((local_u * move_dx) + (local_v * move_dy)) / move_norm - 0.2 * local_w
109 horizontal_distance = math.hypot(move_dx, move_dy)
110 return headwind, horizontal_distance, float(move_dz)
113def energy_cost_j(airspeed: float, headwind: float, step_distance_m: float) -> float:
114 effective_speed = max(4.0, airspeed - headwind)
115 travel_time = step_distance_m / effective_speed
116 return travel_time * (35.0 + 0.7 * airspeed * airspeed)
119-121def transition_energy_j( airspeed: float, current: tuple[int, int, int],
122-125 nxt: tuple[int, int, int], local_u: float, local_v: float, local_w: float,
126-128 step_distance_m: float, altitude_step_m: float, climb_cost_per_level_j: float,
129-131 hover_power_w: float = 105.0, cruise_power_w: float = 150.0, hotel_power_w: float = 18.0,
132-133 headwind_power_per_mps_w: float = 14.0, climb_power_per_mps_w: float = 125.0,
134-136 descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w: float = 58.0, terrain_dz_m: float = 0.0, ) -> float:
137 headwind, horizontal_distance_cells, move_dz = transition_wind_metrics(current, nxt, local_u, local_v, local_w)
139 effective_dz = float(move_dz) + float(terrain_dz_m) / max(altitude_step_m, 1e-6)
140 step_distance = math.hypot(horizontal_distance_cells * step_distance_m, abs(effective_dz) * altitude_step_m)
141 bank_rad = clamp(abs(math.atan2(move_dy := (nxt[1] - current[1]), max(nxt[0] - current[0], 1e-6))), 0.0, DEFAULT_ENVELOPE.max_bank_rad * 0.45)
142-145 aero = aerodynamic_power_w( airspeed=airspeed, bank_rad=bank_rad, vertical_w=local_w,
146-147 envelope=DEFAULT_ENVELOPE, )
148 travel_time = step_distance / max(airspeed - headwind, 4.0)
149 vertical_rate_mps = (effective_dz * altitude_step_m) / max(travel_time, 1e-6)
150 baseline_power = cruise_power_w + hotel_power_w
151 wind_power = headwind_power_per_mps_w * max(headwind, 0.0) - 0.45 * headwind_power_per_mps_w * max(-headwind, 0.0)
152 maneuver_power = max(0.0, aero - 90.0)
153 climb_power = climb_power_per_mps_w * max(vertical_rate_mps, 0.0)
154 descent_credit = descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w * max(-vertical_rate_mps, 0.0)
156 uplift_credit = 0.55 * descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w * max(local_w, 0.0)
157 legacy_vertical_bias = climb_cost_per_level_j * max(effective_dz, 0.0) / max(travel_time, 1e-6)
158-159 total_power = max( hover_power_w,
160 baseline_power + wind_power + maneuver_power + climb_power + legacy_vertical_bias - descent_credit - uplift_credit,
161 )
162 return max(0.0, total_power * travel_time * uplift_energy_scale(local_w))
165-171def transition_energy_batch( airspeed: float, currents, nexts, local_u, local_v, local_w,

272-274 profile_drag = 42.0 + 0.48 * airspeed * airspeed bank_drag = 18.0 * np.maximum( 0.0,

302-309 def _transition_energy_batch_core( airspeed, cur, nxt, u, v, w, step_distance_m,
310-314 altitude_step_m, climb_cost_per_level_j, hover_power_w, cruise_power_w, hotel_power_w,
315-317 headwind_power_per_mps_w, climb_power_per_mps_w, descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w,
318-322 terrain_dz, max_bank_rad, best_glide_speed, induced_drag_coeff, base_sink_mps,
323-324 polar_quad_coeff, ):
325-326 n = cur.shape[0] out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
327 alt_step = altitude_step_m if altitude_step_m > 1e-6 else 1e-6
328-329 bank_cap = max_bank_rad * 0.45 vv = airspeed
330-331 if vv < 8.5: vv = 8.5
332-334 elif vv > 22.0: vv = 22.0 dv = vv - best_glide_speed
335 profile_drag = 42.0 + 0.48 * airspeed * airspeed
336-337 for i in range(n): move_dx = nxt[i, 0] - cur[i, 0]
338-339 move_dy = nxt[i, 1] - cur[i, 1] move_dz = nxt[i, 2] - cur[i, 2]
340 move_norm = math.hypot(move_dx, move_dy)
341-342 if move_norm < 1e-6: move_norm = 1e-6
343 headwind = -((u[i] * move_dx) + (v[i] * move_dy)) / move_norm - 0.2 * w[i]
344 horizontal = math.hypot(move_dx, move_dy)
345 effective_dz = move_dz + terrain_dz[i] / alt_step
346 step_distance = math.hypot(horizontal * step_distance_m, abs(effective_dz) * alt_step)
347 bank_rad = abs(math.atan2(move_dy, move_dx if move_dx > 1e-6 else 1e-6))
348-349 if bank_rad > bank_cap: bank_rad = bank_cap
350 cos_b = math.cos(bank_rad if bank_rad < max_bank_rad else max_bank_rad)
351-352 if cos_b < 1e-3: cos_b = 1e-3
353 bank_penalty = induced_drag_coeff * max(0.0, (1.0 / cos_b) - 1.0)
354 sink = base_sink_mps + polar_quad_coeff * dv * dv + bank_penalty
355 thermal_credit = 40.0 * max(w[i] - 0.25 * sink, 0.0) + 25.0 * max(w[i] - sink, 0.0)
356 bank_drag = 18.0 * max(0.0, (1.0 / cos_b) - 1.0)
357 aero = profile_drag + bank_drag - thermal_credit
358-360 if aero < 12.0: aero = 12.0 travel_den = airspeed - headwind
361-363 if travel_den < 4.0: travel_den = 4.0 travel_time = step_distance / travel_den
364-365 if travel_time < 1e-6: travel_time = 1e-6
366 vertical_rate_mps = (effective_dz * alt_step) / travel_time
367 baseline_power = cruise_power_w + hotel_power_w
368 wind_power = headwind_power_per_mps_w * max(headwind, 0.0) - 0.45 * headwind_power_per_mps_w * max(-headwind, 0.0)
369 maneuver_power = max(0.0, aero - 90.0)
370 climb_power = climb_power_per_mps_w * max(vertical_rate_mps, 0.0)
371 descent_credit = descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w * max(-vertical_rate_mps, 0.0)
372 uplift_credit = 0.55 * descent_power_reduction_per_mps_w * max(w[i], 0.0)
373 legacy_vertical_bias = climb_cost_per_level_j * max(effective_dz, 0.0) / travel_time
374 total_power = baseline_power + wind_power + maneuver_power + climb_power + legacy_vertical_bias - descent_credit - uplift_credit
375-376 if total_power < hover_power_w: total_power = hover_power_w
377 uplift_scale = 1.0 - 0.20 * max(w[i], 0.0)
378-379 if uplift_scale < 0.5: uplift_scale = 0.5
380-381 elif uplift_scale > 1.4: uplift_scale = 1.4
382 energy = total_power * travel_time * uplift_scale
383 out[i] = energy if energy > 0.0 else 0.0
384 return out

393-396def advance_continuous_state( state: DroneState, nxt: tuple[int, int, int], local_u: float,
397-400 local_v: float, local_w: float, safety_penalty: float, step_distance_m: float,
401-404 altitude_step_m: float, min_altitude_level: int, max_altitude_level: int, ) -> DroneState:
405-407 target_x = float(nxt[0]) target_y = float(nxt[1]) target_z = float(nxt[2])
408-410 dx = target_x - state.x dy = target_y - state.y horizontal_distance = math.hypot(dx, dy)
411 track_heading = state.heading_rad if horizontal_distance < 1e-6 else math.atan2(dy, dx)
412-413 progress_cells = min(1.0, horizontal_distance) thermal_gain = max(local_w, 0.0)
414 sink_penalty = max(-local_w, 0.0)
416 loiter_gain = 0.12 * thermal_gain * (1.0 - clamp(abs(target_z - state.z), 0.0, 1.0))
418 forward_scale = clamp(0.80 + 0.16 * thermal_gain - 0.12 * sink_penalty - 0.10 * safety_penalty + loiter_gain, 0.32, 1.12)
419 move_fraction = progress_cells * forward_scale
420 next_x = state.x + (math.cos(track_heading) * move_fraction if horizontal_distance < 1e-6 else (dx / max(horizontal_distance, 1e-6)) * move_fraction)
421 next_y = state.y + (math.sin(track_heading) * move_fraction if horizontal_distance < 1e-6 else (dy / max(horizontal_distance, 1e-6)) * move_fraction)
423 climb_command = clamp(target_z - state.z, -0.45, 1.15)
424 thermal_climb = local_w / max(altitude_step_m / max(step_distance_m, 1e-6), 1e-6) * 0.12
426-427 if state.z > target_z + 0.35: thermal_climb *= 0.15
428 climb_command = min(climb_command, 0.85 * (target_z - state.z))
429 safety_z = 0.10 * safety_penalty
430-432 elif abs(target_z - state.z) <= 0.35: thermal_climb *= 0.2
433-434 climb_command = clamp(target_z - state.z, -0.25, 0.25) safety_z = 0.10 * safety_penalty
435 elif state.z < target_z - 0.35:
438 climb_command = max(climb_command, min(1.15, 0.70 * (target_z - state.z)))
439-440 thermal_climb += 0.08 * max(local_w, 0.0) safety_z = 0.0
441-442 else: safety_z = 0.10 * safety_penalty
443 next_z = state.z + climb_command + thermal_climb - safety_z
444 next_z = min(next_z, target_z + 0.30)
445-446 if state.z > target_z + 0.2: next_z = min(next_z, state.z - 0.05)
447 next_z = clamp(next_z, float(min_altitude_level), float(max_altitude_level))
448-452 return DroneState( x=next_x, y=next_y, z=next_z, heading_rad=track_heading,
453-455 battery_ratio=max(0.0, state.battery_ratio), airspeed=state.airspeed, )
458def clamp_airspeed(airspeed: float, envelope: FlightEnvelope = DEFAULT_ENVELOPE) -> float:
459 return clamp(airspeed, envelope.min_airspeed, envelope.max_airspeed)
462-464def glide_sink_rate_mps( airspeed: float, bank_rad: float = 0.0,
465-466 envelope: FlightEnvelope = DEFAULT_ENVELOPE, ) -> float:
467-468 v = clamp_airspeed(airspeed, envelope) dv = v - envelope.best_glide_speed
469 bank_penalty = envelope.induced_drag_coeff * max(0.0, (1.0 / max(math.cos(clamp(bank_rad, 0.0, envelope.max_bank_rad)), 1e-3)) - 1.0)
470 return envelope.base_sink_mps + envelope.polar_quad_coeff * dv * dv + bank_penalty
473-476def aerodynamic_power_w( airspeed: float, bank_rad: float, vertical_w: float,
477-478 envelope: FlightEnvelope = DEFAULT_ENVELOPE, ) -> float:
479 sink = glide_sink_rate_mps(airspeed, bank_rad, envelope)
480 thermal_credit = 40.0 * max(vertical_w - 0.25 * sink, 0.0) + 25.0 * max(vertical_w - sink, 0.0)
481 profile_drag = 42.0 + 0.48 * airspeed * airspeed
482 bank_drag = 18.0 * max(0.0, (1.0 / max(math.cos(clamp(bank_rad, 0.0, envelope.max_bank_rad)), 1e-3)) - 1.0)
483 return max(12.0, profile_drag + bank_drag - thermal_credit)
486def best_thermalling_bank(vertical_w: float, envelope: FlightEnvelope = DEFAULT_ENVELOPE) -> float:
487 strength = clamp((vertical_w - 0.3) / 2.0, 0.0, 1.0)
488 return clamp((0.22 + 0.38 * strength) * envelope.max_bank_rad, 0.0, envelope.max_bank_rad)
491-494def propagate_control( state: DroneState, control: dict[str, float | str], dt_s: float,
495-498 local_u: float, local_v: float, local_w: float, step_distance_m: float,
499-501 altitude_step_m: float, min_altitude_level: int, max_altitude_level: int,
502-503 envelope: FlightEnvelope = DEFAULT_ENVELOPE, ) -> tuple[DroneState, float]:
504 mode = str(control.get("mode", "cruise"))
505 commanded_speed = clamp_airspeed(float(control.get("airspeed", state.airspeed)), envelope)
506 climb_bias = float(control.get("climb_bias", 0.0))
507 turn_rate = clamp(float(control.get("turn_rate_rad_s", 0.0)), -envelope.max_turn_rate_rad_s, envelope.max_turn_rate_rad_s)
508 heading = state.heading_rad + turn_rate * dt_s
509 bank_rad = min(envelope.max_bank_rad, abs(turn_rate) / max(envelope.max_turn_rate_rad_s, 1e-6) * envelope.max_bank_rad)
510 if mode == "thermal_orbit":
511 commanded_speed = min(commanded_speed, envelope.best_thermalling_speed)
512 if abs(turn_rate) < 1e-6:
513 turn_rate = best_thermalling_bank(local_w, envelope) / max(envelope.max_bank_rad, 1e-6) * envelope.max_turn_rate_rad_s
514 heading = state.heading_rad + turn_rate * dt_s
515 bank_rad = best_thermalling_bank(local_w, envelope)
516-517 if climb_bias >= 0.0: climb_bias = max(climb_bias, 0.65)
518 airspeed = state.airspeed + (commanded_speed - state.airspeed) * min(1.0, dt_s / 3.0)
519-520 airspeed = clamp_airspeed(airspeed, envelope) body_vx = math.cos(heading) * airspeed
521-522 body_vy = math.sin(heading) * airspeed groundspeed_x = body_vx + local_u
523 groundspeed_y = body_vy + local_v
524 x = state.x + (groundspeed_x * dt_s) / max(step_distance_m, 1e-6)
525 y = state.y + (groundspeed_y * dt_s) / max(step_distance_m, 1e-6)
526 sink = glide_sink_rate_mps(airspeed, bank_rad, envelope)
527 climb_rate = local_w - sink + climb_bias * 0.75
528-529 if mode == "thermal_orbit": climb_rate += 0.45 * max(local_w, 0.0)
530-532 if mode == "approach": climb_rate = min(climb_rate, climb_bias * 1.15 - max(sink, 0.35))
533 z_m = state.z * altitude_step_m + climb_rate * dt_s
534 z = clamp(z_m / max(altitude_step_m, 1e-6), float(min_altitude_level), float(max_altitude_level))
535-539 next_state = DroneState( x=x, y=y, z=z, heading_rad=heading,
540-542 battery_ratio=state.battery_ratio, airspeed=airspeed, )
543 return next_state, aerodynamic_power_w(airspeed, bank_rad, local_w, envelope) * dt_s

altitude.py　高度与地形跟随　（404→205 行）

1"""AGL / terrain-height helpers.
3Mission `z` is an **above-ground-level (AGL) band index**. Geometric MSL height is:
5 msl_m = elev(x, y) + z * altitude_step_m
7Constant-AGL cruise therefore follows terrain in MSL; climbing hills costs energy
8-9even when the AGL band is unchanged. """
11from __future__ import annotations
13import math
14from typing import Sequence
16import numpy as np
18from .mathutils import bilinear_sample, clamp
21def sample_elevation(elevation: Sequence[Sequence[float]] | None, x: float, y: float) -> float:
22 """Bilinear sample of a terrain elevation grid (meters). Missing → 0."""
23-24 if elevation is None: return 0.0
25-27 if isinstance(elevation, np.ndarray): if elevation.size == 0: return 0.0
28 return float(bilinear_sample(elevation, x, y))
29-30 if isinstance(elevation, (list, tuple)) and len(elevation) == 0: return 0.0
31-32 rows = len(elevation) cols = len(elevation[0]) if rows else 0
33-35 if rows <= 0 or cols <= 0: return 0.0 x = clamp(float(x), 0.0, float(cols - 1))
36-38 y = clamp(float(y), 0.0, float(rows - 1)) x0 = int(math.floor(x)) y0 = int(math.floor(y))
39-42 x1 = min(x0 + 1, cols - 1) y1 = min(y0 + 1, rows - 1) tx = x - x0 ty = y - y0
43-44 e00 = float(elevation[y0][x0]) e10 = float(elevation[y0][x1])
45-46 e01 = float(elevation[y1][x0]) e11 = float(elevation[y1][x1])
47 return (e00 * (1.0 - tx) + e10 * tx) * (1.0 - ty) + (e01 * (1.0 - tx) + e11 * tx) * ty
50-53def terrain_delta_m( elevation: Sequence[Sequence[float]] | None, x0: float, y0: float,
54-56 x1: float, y1: float, ) -> float:
57 return sample_elevation(elevation, x1, y1) - sample_elevation(elevation, x0, y0)
60_ELEV_NP_CACHE: dict[int, tuple[object, object]] = {}
63-64def _as_elev_array(elevation): import numpy as np
66-67 if elevation is None: return None
68 if isinstance(elevation, np.ndarray):
69 return elevation if elevation.dtype == np.float64 else elevation.astype(np.float64)
70-71 key = id(elevation) hit = _ELEV_NP_CACHE.get(key)
72-73 if hit is not None and hit[0] is elevation: return hit[1]
74 elev = np.asarray(elevation, dtype=np.float64)
75-77 if len(_ELEV_NP_CACHE) > 16: _ELEV_NP_CACHE.clear() _ELEV_NP_CACHE[key] = (elevation, elev)

81-82try: from numba import njit

85 @njit(cache=True)
86-87 def _bilinear_scalar(elev, x, y): h = elev.shape[0]
88 w = elev.shape[1]
89-90 if w <= 0 or h <= 0: return 0.0
91-92 if x < 0.0: x = 0.0
93-94 elif x > (w - 1): x = float(w - 1)
95-96 if y < 0.0: y = 0.0
97-100 elif y > (h - 1): y = float(h - 1) x0 = int(x) y0 = int(y)
101-103 x1 = x0 + 1 if x0 + 1 < w else w - 1 y1 = y0 + 1 if y0 + 1 < h else h - 1 tx = x - x0
104-105 ty = y - y0 top = elev[y0, x0] + (elev[y0, x1] - elev[y0, x0]) * tx
106 bottom = elev[y1, x0] + (elev[y1, x1] - elev[y1, x0]) * tx
107 return top + (bottom - top) * ty
109 @njit(cache=True)
110-111 def _terrain_delta_batch_jit(elev, xs0, ys0, xs1, ys1): n = xs0.shape[0]
112 out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
113 for i in range(n):
114 out[i] = _bilinear_scalar(elev, xs1[i], ys1[i]) - _bilinear_scalar(elev, xs0[i], ys0[i])

117 @njit(cache=True)
118-119 def _terrain_climb_batch_jit(elev, xs0, ys0, xs1, ys1, n_samples): n = xs0.shape[0]
120-121 out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64) steps = n_samples if n_samples > 1 else 1
122-123 for i in range(n): prev = _bilinear_scalar(elev, xs0[i], ys0[i])
124-126 rise = 0.0 dx = xs1[i] - xs0[i] dy = ys1[i] - ys0[i]
127-128 for k in range(1, steps + 1): t = k / steps
129-130 val = _bilinear_scalar(elev, xs0[i] + dx * t, ys0[i] + dy * t) d = val - prev
131-133 if d > 0.0: rise += d prev = val
134 out[i] = rise
135 return out
137except Exception: # pragma: no cover
138-139 def _terrain_delta_batch_jit(elev, xs0, ys0, xs1, ys1): import numpy as np
141-143 n = xs0.shape[0] out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64) h, w = elev.shape
144-145 for i in range(n): for label, x, y in ((0, xs0[i], ys0[i]), (1, xs1[i], ys1[i])):
146 xx = min(max(float(x), 0.0), float(max(w - 1, 0)))
147-149 yy = min(max(float(y), 0.0), float(max(h - 1, 0))) x0 = int(xx) y0 = int(yy)
150-153 x1 = min(x0 + 1, w - 1) y1 = min(y0 + 1, h - 1) tx = xx - x0 ty = yy - y0
154 val = (elev[y0, x0] + (elev[y0, x1] - elev[y0, x0]) * tx) * (1.0 - ty) + (
155 elev[y1, x0] + (elev[y1, x1] - elev[y1, x0]) * tx
156 ) * ty
157-158 if label == 0: a = val
159-160 else: out[i] = val - a
161 return out
163-164 def _terrain_climb_batch_jit(elev, xs0, ys0, xs1, ys1, n_samples): import numpy as np
166-168 n = xs0.shape[0] out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64) steps = max(1, int(n_samples))
169 h, w = elev.shape
170-171 for i in range(n): ts = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, steps + 1)
172 xs = np.clip(xs0[i] + ts * (xs1[i] - xs0[i]), 0.0, max(w - 1, 0))
173 ys = np.clip(ys0[i] + ts * (ys1[i] - ys0[i]), 0.0, max(h - 1, 0))
174-175 x0i = np.floor(xs).astype(np.int64) y0i = np.floor(ys).astype(np.int64)
176-178 x1i = np.minimum(x0i + 1, w - 1) y1i = np.minimum(y0i + 1, h - 1) tx = xs - x0i
179-180 ty = ys - y0i vals = (
181 (elev[y0i, x0i] + (elev[y0i, x1i] - elev[y0i, x0i]) * tx) * (1.0 - ty)
182 + (elev[y1i, x0i] + (elev[y1i, x1i] - elev[y1i, x0i]) * tx) * ty
183-184 ) out[i] = float(np.maximum(0.0, np.diff(vals)).sum())
185 return out

232def effective_dz_levels(agl_dz: float, terrain_dz_m: float, altitude_step_m: float) -> float:
233 """Vertical work in altitude-level units: AGL change + terrain follow."""

238-240def cruise_agl_floor( horizontal_cells: float, min_altitude_level: float,
241-243 clearance_agl_level: float, approach_cells: float = 5.0, ) -> float:
244 """Hard AGL floor: clearance while cruising; allow landing near the goal."""

257-262def snap_cruise_agl( z: float, *, clearance: float, min_level: float, max_level: float,
263-264 step: float = 0.025, ) -> float:
265 """Snap AGL to the cruise grid relative to clearance (physics stays continuous)."""
266-267 step = max(float(step), 0.01) lo = max(float(clearance), float(min_level))
268-270 hi = float(max_level) z = clamp(float(z), lo, hi) k = round((z - lo) / step)

274-278def cruise_agl_band_grid( clearance: float, min_level: float, max_level: float, *,
279-281 step: float = 0.025, span_levels: float = 2.0, sticky: float | None = None,
282) -> list[float]:
283 """Fine AGL cruise candidates from clearance up to clearance+span (capped by max).
285 Default step 0.02 level ≈ 1 m when altitude_step_m=50. Not a physics limit —
286-287 only a planning discretization; wind is still sampled continuously via interpolation. """
288-289 step = max(float(step), 0.01) lo = max(float(clearance), float(min_level))
290 hi = min(float(clearance) + float(span_levels), float(max_level))
291-292 if hi < lo - 1e-12: return [clamp(float(clearance), float(min_level), float(max_level))]
294-295 n = int(round((hi - lo) / step)) bands = [round(lo + i * step, 10) for i in range(n + 1)]
296-300 if sticky is not None: bands.append( snap_cruise_agl( float(sticky), clearance=clearance,
301-304 min_level=min_level, max_level=max_level, step=step, )
305 )
307 uniq: dict[int, float] = {}
308-309 for z in bands: z = clamp(float(z), float(min_level), float(max_level))
310-312 if z + 1e-9 < lo: continue key = int(round((z - lo) / step))
313 uniq[key] = round(lo + key * step, 10)
314 return [clamp(uniq[k], float(min_level), float(max_level)) for k in sorted(uniq)]

343-344def straight_agl_guide_polyline( start: tuple[float, float, float] | tuple[float, float],
345-346 goal: tuple[float, float, float] | tuple[float, float], agl_cruise: float = 1.0,
347) -> list[tuple[float, float, float]]:
348 """Constant-AGL straight guide matching eval `straight_agl_baseline` packing.
350-351 Profile: climb in the first ~12% of XY, hold cruise, descend in the last ~12%. """
352-353 sx, sy = float(start[0]), float(start[1]) sz = float(start[2]) if len(start) > 2 else 0.0
354-355 gx, gy = float(goal[0]), float(goal[1]) gz = float(goal[2]) if len(goal) > 2 else 0.0
356 dist = max(math.hypot(gx - sx, gy - sy), 1.0)
357 climb_levels = max(0.0, float(agl_cruise) - sz)
358 desc_levels = max(0.0, float(agl_cruise) - gz)
359 climb_steps = max(1, int(math.ceil(climb_levels))) if climb_levels > 0.05 else 0
360 desc_steps = max(1, int(math.ceil(desc_levels))) if desc_levels > 0.05 else 0
361 n_cruise = max(1, int(math.ceil(dist)))
362 pts: list[tuple[float, float, float]] = [(sx, sy, sz)]
363-364 climb_frac = 0.12 if climb_steps else 0.0 desc_frac = 0.12 if desc_steps else 0.0
365-366 for i in range(1, climb_steps + 1): t = climb_frac * i / climb_steps
367 z = sz + (float(agl_cruise) - sz) * i / climb_steps
368 pts.append((sx + (gx - sx) * t, sy + (gy - sy) * t, z))
369-370 cruise_start = climb_frac cruise_end = 1.0 - desc_frac
371 for i in range(1, n_cruise + 1):
372 t = cruise_start + (cruise_end - cruise_start) * i / n_cruise
373 pts.append((sx + (gx - sx) * t, sy + (gy - sy) * t, float(agl_cruise)))
374-375 for i in range(1, desc_steps + 1): t = cruise_end + desc_frac * i / desc_steps
376 z = float(agl_cruise) + (gz - float(agl_cruise)) * i / desc_steps
377 pts.append((sx + (gx - sx) * t, sy + (gy - sy) * t, z))
378 if math.hypot(pts[-1][0] - gx, pts[-1][1] - gy) > 0.01 or abs(pts[-1][2] - gz) > 0.01:
379 pts.append((gx, gy, gz))
380 return pts
383-387def next_polyline_waypoint( path: list[tuple[float, float, float]], x: float, y: float, *,
388-389 ahead_cells: float = 0.05, ) -> tuple[float, float, float] | None:
390 """First polyline vertex strictly ahead of (x, y) along the path chord."""
391-393 if len(path) <= 1: return None sx, sy = path[0][0], path[0][1]
394-395 gx, gy = path[-1][0], path[-1][1] total = math.hypot(gx - sx, gy - sy)
396-397 if total < 1e-9: return path[-1]
398 cur_along = ((float(x) - sx) * (gx - sx) + (float(y) - sy) * (gy - sy)) / total
399 cur_along = clamp(cur_along, 0.0, total)
400-401 for p in path[1:]: along = ((p[0] - sx) * (gx - sx) + (p[1] - sy) * (gy - sy)) / total
402-403 if along > cur_along + ahead_cells: return (float(p[0]), float(p[1]), float(p[2]))
404 return (float(path[-1][0]), float(path[-1][1]), float(path[-1][2]))

90def _trilinear_sample_batch_numpy(arr: np.ndarray, x: np.ndarray, y: np.ndarray, z: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
91-93 z_n, h, w = arr.shape x = np.clip(x, 0.0, max(w - 1, 0)) y = np.clip(y, 0.0, max(h - 1, 0))

105-106 def _plane(zi: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray: v00 = arr[zi, y0, x0]
107-109 v10 = arr[zi, y0, x1] v01 = arr[zi, y1, x0] v11 = arr[zi, y1, x1]

123-124 def _trilinear_sample_scalar_jit(arr, x, y, z): z_n = arr.shape[0]
125-126 h = arr.shape[1] w = arr.shape[2]
127-128 if x < 0.0: x = 0.0
129-130 elif x > (w - 1): x = float(w - 1)
131-132 if y < 0.0: y = 0.0
133-134 elif y > (h - 1): y = float(h - 1)
135-136 if z < 0.0: z = 0.0
137-140 elif z > (z_n - 1): z = float(z_n - 1) x0 = int(np.floor(x)) y0 = int(np.floor(y))
141-142 z0 = int(np.floor(z)) x1 = x0 + 1 if x0 + 1 < w else w - 1
143-145 y1 = y0 + 1 if y0 + 1 < h else h - 1 z1 = z0 + 1 if z0 + 1 < z_n else z_n - 1 tx = x - x0
146-149 ty = y - y0 tz = z - z0 v000 = arr[z0, y0, x0] v100 = arr[z0, y0, x1]
150-152 v010 = arr[z0, y1, x0] v110 = arr[z0, y1, x1] v001 = arr[z1, y0, x0]
153-155 v101 = arr[z1, y0, x1] v011 = arr[z1, y1, x0] v111 = arr[z1, y1, x1]
156-157 top0 = v000 + (v100 - v000) * tx bot0 = v010 + (v110 - v010) * tx
158-159 top1 = v001 + (v101 - v001) * tx bot1 = v011 + (v111 - v011) * tx
160-161 lower = top0 + (bot0 - top0) * ty upper = top1 + (bot1 - top1) * ty
162 return lower + (upper - lower) * tz
164 @njit(cache=True)
165-166 def _trilinear_sample_batch_jit(arr, xs, ys, zs): n = xs.shape[0]
167 out = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
168-169 for i in range(n): out[i] = _trilinear_sample_scalar_jit(arr, xs[i], ys[i], zs[i])
170 return out
172 _HAS_NUMBA = True
173except Exception: # pragma: no cover
174 _HAS_NUMBA = False
176 def _trilinear_sample_scalar_jit(arr, x, y, z):
177 return float(_trilinear_sample_batch_numpy(arr, np.asarray([x]), np.asarray([y]), np.asarray([z]))[0])
179 def _trilinear_sample_batch_jit(arr, xs, ys, zs):
180 return _trilinear_sample_batch_numpy(arr, xs, ys, zs)
183def trilinear_sample(layers, x: float, y: float, z: float) -> float:
185 if isinstance(layers, np.ndarray) or (hasattr(layers, "ndim") and not isinstance(layers, (list, tuple))):
186 arr = np.asarray(layers, dtype=np.float64)
187 if arr.ndim == 3 and arr.size > 0:
188 return float(_trilinear_sample_scalar_jit(arr, float(x), float(y), float(z)))
189 return 0.0
190-191 if layers is None or (isinstance(layers, (list, tuple)) and len(layers) == 0): return 0.0
192-193 z = clamp(z, 0.0, max(len(layers) - 1, 0)) z0 = int(math.floor(z))
194-196 z1 = min(z0 + 1, len(layers) - 1) tz = z - z0 lower = bilinear_sample(layers[z0], x, y)
197 upper = bilinear_sample(layers[z1], x, y)
198 return lerp(lower, upper, tz)
201def trilinear_sample_batch(field, xs, ys, zs):
202 """Vectorized trilinear sample on a (Z, H, W) ndarray.
204-205 ``xs``, ``ys``, ``zs`` are 1-D arrays of equal length; returns float64 (N,). """
206 if isinstance(field, np.ndarray) and field.dtype == np.float64 and field.ndim == 3:
207 arr = field
208-209 else: arr = np.asarray(field, dtype=np.float64)
210-211 if isinstance(xs, np.ndarray) and xs.dtype == np.float64 and xs.ndim == 1: xs_a = xs
212-213 else: xs_a = np.asarray(xs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
214-215 if isinstance(ys, np.ndarray) and ys.dtype == np.float64 and ys.ndim == 1: ys_a = ys
216-217 else: ys_a = np.asarray(ys, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
218-219 if isinstance(zs, np.ndarray) and zs.dtype == np.float64 and zs.ndim == 1: zs_a = zs
220-222 else: zs_a = np.asarray(zs, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1) n = int(xs_a.size)
223-224 if arr.ndim != 3 or arr.size == 0 or n == 0: return np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
225-226 if _HAS_NUMBA: return _trilinear_sample_batch_jit(arr, xs_a, ys_a, zs_a)
227 return _trilinear_sample_batch_numpy(arr, xs_a, ys_a, zs_a)
